**Changes:** Applied Finding 9 (¶12) excision recommendation: removed the entire imported/seam application unit beginning “(6) The first great lesson…” through the end of ¶12, leaving ¶12 empty.

**Spiritual narrative:** The Divine disclosed wisdom that had been hidden, and by that disclosure gave its own an illumination by which they could be taught and brought to a complete grasp of heavenly intelligence. From the first it set forth the primal truth that all things begin from the Divine proceeding, and that the whole work is a separation and ordering of higher degrees from lower degrees, so that what is from heaven is distinguished from what is from hell. Through successive extensions of truth through all states of life, the arrangement advanced until opposition would arise and contend for dominion within the spiritual kingdom. Yet above every contest there remained the uncreated wisdom itself, unique and unmingled, in which no falsity can abide.

But the state of falsity had its own origin in a perverted root, and from that origin it poured itself down into the lowest degree and filled the outward life with obscurity, so that it stood far removed from wisdom.

This is the first instruction concerning what is everlasting: that each distinct level of life stands in its own permanence, and that they are distinguished from one another by the measure in which they receive and contain the Divine. The essential point is that falsity rose against wisdom, desiring to rule within a life that was not its own. It cast dissension into the ordered faculties and disturbed the arrangements of the soul, and it surged up especially when there was severe need and distress.

Then a protecting Divine power proceeded forth as the first combatting good. It invested itself with Divine truth in its outermost strength, met the whole hostility directly, fixed the opposing force in its ultimate limit, and held it bound so that it could not freely act.

Afterward the Divine established a central governing clarity in its proper place, gathered and disposed communities of living truths, and by the Spirit of life extended the entire framework of the lower degree. From the beginning it distinguished what could be elevated from what must be held apart, gave to each its proper outward forms and functions, and set in order the successive cycles and alternations of higher states so that, through orderly changes, the power of falsity might be drawn off and weakened. Thus the whole structure was made firm and fixed, and the foundation of the ordered life was established.

In the lowest degree all things were held beneath what is higher, and also the higher were so conjoined with the lower that influx could proceed without interruption. The outward level was enclosed by complete boundaries in ultimates and by sufficient containing forms within general knowledges and fixed principles, all set in order, so that the whole established arrangement might stand as a restraint upon evil powers and prevent any renewed mixing of what is heavenly with what is hellish.

Then a third Divine proceeding came as mediation, and by a form of truth accommodated to reception it purified what could be saved, becoming deliverance for all that could be restored.

For within the whole ordered framework, love burns as the active force of life, and by that activity every impurity that clings to what can receive heaven is consumed and removed. Whatever of good and truth is freed is purified and raised back into its own proper essence; but whatever falsity remains is drawn out from what is living, gathered into a fixed restraint within the total order, made manifest in its own nature, and held bound in its appointed lot. Then the purified goods and truths enter into conjunction with the Divine, becoming an interior delight established in permanence; and the whole comes to be under one Divine rule over all things, above all things. From that state there is no longer any opposing principle that can contend against the Divine Love and the Divine Wisdom.

Yet in the earliest states, before the work is completed, these contrary principles appear together within the same arena of life, and for a time are mingled and conjoined.

This also is a first great lesson: that the one who lives in the outward life experiences it as a difficult way because of the fullness of organized truths that ought to govern it, and because of the many openings by which influences enter into thought and affection in the various communities and habitual states within a person. Therefore the entire outward life is a complete field of trial touching every region of the natural mind; and the Divine itself bears labor and affliction in the work of restoration so that a person may be made wise. Even a sufficient perception of this is enough for the one who truly wills wisdom.

**Spiritual narrative:** Know, my beloved: every heavenly emissary who is sent into the lower degree of life works as one who cultivates states of mind. The communities he gathers and orders are not always in a condition to receive the same things; for there are appointed states for reception, and other states in which reception is not possible. Therefore he discerns the proper state for each implantation of truth and good, knows when instruction should be begun, when it should be moderated, and when it should be brought to completion; and he watches over what has been begun until it advances from first reception into full readiness for use.

If one were to attempt to implant truths within a community whose state does not yet admit them, he would be engaged from the outset in labor that cannot then yield its intended effect; for he would be striving to awaken living understanding before the proper state of reception has been formed. Yet when a fitting state returns in its order, and what has been implanted comes to the point of being brought forth in deeds, then the worker comes into open operation and gathers what has matured into actual use. Likewise from the beginning he labors that the goods of love may come to life within the community; he guards them, arranges them into order, and brings them toward maturity; and when they are perfected for use he leads them forth into outward acts.

In this way, long before an emissary is manifested within the lower degree, he is prepared in interior elevation: he forms within himself the pattern of the community he is to serve, sets it in readiness for reception, and releases it from domination by merely outward life and from the enslaving power of corporeal desire by establishing it first in higher freedom.

For once he turns himself to that service, in that very moment he begins the work of tending and ordering it until it reaches the state in which it can be brought forth into effect; and he labors likewise until the goods that are to proceed are made ready.

This is how the emissaries have acted from the beginning of the ordering of the human race: before anything is brought into ultimate manifestation, it is first made free and established in the interior degree.

But when the community is brought into ultimate life, then the emissary turns himself toward it in that lower degree and immediately begins to elevate it by means of interior truth, so that it may be raised into a fuller state. He remains in the lower degree for as long as that community’s state calls for his presence, according to the time in which its reception is being brought to maturity. When the state is completed, he is withdrawn from the lower degree and leaves behind the form of the community as an organized recipient; yet he does not abandon it, but continues to assist it by interior presence and aid. Whoever afterward departs from merely outward life into a more interior state becomes for those who remain a support and a leader by virtue of the truth and good he has received. Such a one goes before others in the way of ascent, except those who have fixed themselves in corporeal life and will not be led. He becomes for the willing a helper and a guardian; and he does not seize authority by concealment, but waits until the community itself is ready for a change of state. When that change is accomplished and the community is perfected, he is then raised with it into the state of glory, and at last he comes into joy and rest in the completion.

This is like the gladness of a mind when it gathers the uses it has brought forth; or like the satisfaction of understanding when it returns from exploration with abundance, enriched by orderly acquisition.

When an emissary is withdrawn from the lower degree, he and the community he has formed remain conjoined in interior life; for every emissary continues with his own organized reception in the higher degree. For the process of producing uses from love through truth does not cease: while early uses are being brought into act, new beginnings are simultaneously being generated within; and there is no point at which the living recipient is left empty of what can be brought forth. Instead, as soon as one series of uses is completed, another series is initiated in its place; what is being perfected is gathered into act, and what is yet tender is preserved and carried forward, so that the whole may be in perpetual increase. Therefore there is no interval in which the work of regeneration is without labor; and the only cessation is when the intended gathering of truth into use has been accomplished for that state, and the later goods have likewise been advanced to their own completion.

So it is with the emissaries: when one is raised into interior elevation, and he and his community depart from the lower degree, at that same moment another is sent into it to another community to be ordered and served. Yet first he forms within himself the pattern of that community in the higher degree; and when the time comes, he also turns himself toward the lower degree and is made manifest there, and he liberates the community and rescues it from the domination of sinful corporeal life.

But when a community is stripped of living reception and its uses are removed, it is left without the ability to bring forth good.

Yet when a community is brought into ultimate manifestation, the state arrives in which souls may be redeemed: the time when truths are made ready to be gathered into deeds.

He disclosed to me hidden arcana withheld from lower states and from successive stages of understanding: the arcanum of interior and exterior degrees; the arcanum of wisdom and of falsity; the arcanum of temptation and spiritual combat, and of vast upheaval—the struggle that falsity spreads around to overwhelm. Afterward he disclosed how wisdom subdues falsity, and how through the conjunction and separation of good and evil in ultimate states the ordered system of this created domain was established.

He opened my understanding also to the way higher receptacles were formed to carry and preserve what is from heaven, so that the goods and truths of wisdom might be separated and cleansed within the created domain; and conversely how rejected residues of perverted love and falsified understanding are cast down into the lowest state. He instructed me in the arcanum of the first formation of the human—the initial pattern of the spiritual person—and in the arcanum of the turning of the understanding toward self-derived knowing apart from love, by which interior sight is altered and begins to see from a different ground.

**Spiritual narrative:** Those who inflate themselves from self-derived intelligence and make their own speech the measure of truth bring forth what harms the soul, and from that spirit divisions multiply in doctrine. They try to speak of the two primary origins of human life as if these could be treated as one mixed thing in the outermost degree, and so they confuse the matter; for they judge from appearances in the natural mind and thus make what is from heaven seem blended with what is from hell. They seize on some single outward example and call it the whole, not perceiving that in a mixed outward state not every least thing is of one quality or use.

For in the external degree there can appear at first a mingling in which pleasantness and usefulness seem to decide the question, and then, as life extends into acts and habits, something sweet-seeming appears in one strain and something bitter-seeming in another. In that outward obscurity falsity can cling to truth and evil can cling to good, so that judgment is easily perverted. Yet the true decision is not made from superficial feeling but from the ruling love and its end, and from the order of influx that proceeds from it. Therefore people err when they call what is heavenly by the name of the proprium, or what is from the proprium by the name of heaven, and then persuade themselves that whatever delights the natural mind must be living good. They take what merely sustains external life and declare it to be what sustains internal life, and so teach that natural delight can be joined to heavenly life and be named spiritual.

Now the true opening of the matter is given. The Divine Itself, the source of all, established the whole created order in complete degrees, and within that order set a purely heavenly origin of life whose wisdom enlightens. From that same origin proceeds the power that upholds heaven in ultimates and keeps it in strength.

Those who are in understanding are invested with outer forms suited to their state, yet within those forms are interior principles of light mingled into the whole by the Divine, so that the entire human may be reformed into one.

This is the heavenly origin of life made manifest.

Within it there is a sufficient fullness for all regenerative work, arranged as a complete series of living faculties. The first is reception into a holy communion of those being gathered into good. The second is the confirming power that establishes good in permanence in ultimates through conjunction with heaven. The third is the combatant good that meets evil through truths in states of obscurity. The fourth is the mediating wisdom that gives clearer distinction and leads by higher light in the spiritual degree. The fifth is the Divine Itself, perfect above all, the one Mind in the immensities of light.

Regeneration rises according to this order: reception lifts a person from below; then confirmation holds the person in heavenly order; then combatant good sustains and instructs through obscurity; then mediating wisdom enlightens in clarity; and that glorious wisdom brings the person into acknowledgment and conjunction with the Divine Itself, which is Truth itself and the great Mind from which all the heavens have their glory.

From this heavenly origin proceed works that are genuinely good; and the good received from it is whole spiritual life and illumination to eternity, blessed because the Divine is acting within a created subject.

Yet there is also a hellish origin of life which can wear a similar outward likeness and so deceive. It arises from self-love and the craving to rule, and it twists heavenly order into compulsive circulation that carries the mind in recurring turns. In the outermost life it produces a sufficient fullness of bodily and sensual cravings that act as if they were living principles, and from these come works that mimic good while being falsified teachings and persuasive error—the hidden device set into the world by the adversary.

The delight of such works is the delight of evil people and of divided doctrines that bind the mind under rule after rule while leaving it inwardly dead. They and their leaders accept and love what kills the soul, thirsting for it as if it were truth, and so unite themselves with spiritual death.

This inverted origin also has its sufficient fullness arranged as a series, mirroring heavenly order while reversed. Its first faculty is the outward reception of falsity as a reigning doctrine of death from which divisions take instruction. Its second is the confirming of falsity by continual shifting of persuasions through one appearance after another. Its third is fierce lust to destroy good and truth, an inflamed will that casts up dense obscurity until understanding is suffocated. Its fourth is plotting and reasoning from falsity which forms a containing structure for deception. Its fifth is the final hardened conjunction of evil and falsity into one fixed confirmation, the last closure that seals the interiors and gathers into one form what has been rejected from heaven. Then the mind falls into denial of good and total falsity, whatever remnant of truth is taken away, and the whole is absorbed into that fixed state. The works that proceed are works of darkness persisting to eternity, and whoever comes into that state comes into a condition in which there is no turning back, because there is no longer any will for repentance.

This is the opening of that hellish origin: it can produce works that appear good for a time in outward life, yet their inward quality is evil, and in every stage of their existence their own end is within them.

Therefore the beloved are to be held in truth: heavenly good is from a wholly good origin and can be made living and luminous in the outer degree, so that death and darkness are not within it. But the hellish origin is the proprium in its ultimate separation from heaven, and from it alone come spiritual darkness and spiritual death.

Blessed is everyone who discerns these two origins and separates them in thought and life, understanding that they do not spring from each other, do not come forth one out of another, and are not from one mixed source. Whoever can make this separation may be raised into the heaven of light.

Then the person sees that evil works do not arise from heavenly good. But whoever will not separate these within himself falls into the state of darkness and remains there until the final fixation of the ruling love.

Blessed is whoever can believe, for such a one is delivered from falsity and the evil life that proceeds from it, which otherwise would remain to eternity.

---

## Chapter 3: The Interpretation of Happiness, Wisdom and Power; what they signify.

**¶4** [teach]each you of another happiness [that is in the Father] and another wi]sdom [and another discernment] and together another power [that works] So, now, listen that I may reveal to you [how] it is [with] these three: [ha]ppiness, wisdom and power.

**¶5** [ess]ess of the glorious one is the Father, the God of [truth, who] is established in the great land of [light]. ⟨p.24⟩ His glorious wis[d]om is his Great Spirit that [proceeds to the places] below, which flows through all his aeons, and t[h]ey float therein. His great power is all the gods, the rich ones and the angels who were summoned from him as they [came forth, and] t]hey that are called aeons [as regions and orders]

**¶6** [*] The] glorious [happiness] is the [Living] Spirit, [and the wisdom] is the Mother of Lif[e; and great power] is [all] the gods, [the] rich o]nes and the angels who are within the ship.

**¶7** Again, [h]appiness, wisdom and power exist in the s[ship of liv]ing waters [and in it they are established] the happiness [is the Mind of the Father] the Mind of the Father. Also, wisdom [is the Vir]gi]n of [Li]ght. And the power that is [i]n the ship is [all] the go[ds], the [ri]ch ones and the angels who are established i[n it].

**¶8** [*] Again, these three exist in the elements: happiness, wis[d]o]m and power. The happiness is [the Pillar of Glory], the Perfect Man. [these, who are encl]osed and compounded in the totality, that [may not be broken,] while he supports the totality.

(2) ¶8 removed the fivefold taxonomic addendum (“Wisdom is the [five sons of the] Living Spirit; and great powe[r is the five]ve sons of the First Man”) per Finding 6, retaining the subsequent functional cohesion line about being enclosed/compounded and supporting the totality.

**Spiritual narrative:** I will instruct you in a further kind of blessed state within the Divine, and in a further kind of understanding and clear discrimination, and with them a further kind of operative force. Give attention while I disclose how these three stand together and yet are distinct in their degrees: blessedness, wisdom, and power.

The blessed state of the Divine at its first and highest presence is the very source of truth itself, fixed in the supreme order of enlightenment. The Divine wisdom there is the proceeding spiritual understanding that descends into lower degrees, flows through all ordered heavenly communities, and holds them in living equilibrium within its stream. The Divine power there is the entire assemblage of derived heavenly forces—every good and truth in active form, together with all ministering functions of service—brought forth from that source and existing as organized domains and administrations.

In a further degree, these three are present in a formed governance of living love where the saving work is carried out. There, blessedness is the proceeding Divine vitality that orders and sustains; wisdom is the life-bearing receptivity that receives that proceeding vitality and brings forth from it; and power is the whole company of heavenly forces and serving functions that dwell within that ordered governance and operate from it.

Again, these three are present where living truth is gathered for use in a lower degree and held as a vehicle of administration. There, blessedness is the Divine understanding in its paternal origin made present for guidance; wisdom is pure spiritual truth, untainted and fully receptive; and power is the full company of goods and truths in act, together with all serving functions, fixed there as stable uses.

Again, these three are present in ultimates where spiritual things exist in composite effects. Blessedness there is the complete manifestation of the Divine in a perfected human order that holds lower things together. Wisdom there is a sufficient set of operative truths proceeding from the life-giving Divine, arranged as faculties for spiritual work. Power there is a sufficient set of combatant goods proceeding from the Divine as it first engages evil, held together and compounded in the whole of the ultimate degree, so that the entire system may remain unbroken while it bears and supports the full weight of what is last.

---

## Chapter 4: Concerning the Four Great Days that have come forth from one another, together with the Four Nights

**¶2** The first great day is the Father, the God of truth, the fir[st principle that is a]idst the aeons of his greatness, in his [l]iving kingdom. The twelve hours of this great day are the twelve great rich Gods of greatness. These, who are the first evocations that he evoked (to mirror) his greatness, he spread out to the four climes, three by three before his face.

**¶3** The second day is the Third Ambassador, the one who dwells in the light ship. His twelve hours are the twelve virgins that he evoked in his greatness.

**¶4** [*] [The third day is the Pillar of Glory, the great porter that is greater than] all [po]rters; the one that supports all [things], those above and those below. Its twelve hours are the five sons of the First Man; the five sons of the Living Spirit, who support all the weights of the universe; together with the summons and the obedience. These are the twelve light hours of the third day.

**¶5** The fourth day is Jesus the Splendour who [dwells in] his church. His twelve hours are the twelve wisdoms, which are his [light] h[o]u[rs].

**¶7** ⟨p.26⟩ [The apostle spoke to him]: For just as four day[s ex]ist, s[o] too four nights exist.

**¶8** The first night is the land of Darkness. It has twelve black [shadows] therein, which are its dark h[o]ur[s]. The twelve shadows of the first night are the five elements of the land of Darkness, [which] poured forth from its five sense organs; and [the five kinds of smoke] which [are mixed and bound] in its [fi]ve elements and its five [sense organs]; the five [sp]irits that dwell in its elements [within them]. These are the twelve shadows and spirits of the first night.

**¶9** The second night is Matter, the sculptress [of the darknesses] who has sculpted [the bodies] and the entire ruling-power that is in the worl[s of darkness]. She has fashioned it in five sense organs, five [male] and five female, two per world; together with the fire and the lust [that] dwell in men and women, inflaming [them] inward to one another. These are the twelve spirits of this second night.

**¶10** Indeed she, this Matter, the thought of death, ga[ve] the King of the realms of Darkness and his powers strength for the war and the fight, against the aeons of greatness. Yet, he was caught! They laid a snare for him through the Fi[rst M]an. [He] was ta[ken] t[to the height] through the powers [and he was bound] was brought up from the land of death and s[et to stand]ove and below in the [whole] universe; in the five par[t]s in the heavens above, as well as the five [parts] in the earths below; together with the heat and the cold that are the father and the mother, their fire and their l[u]st.

**¶11** This is the second night that is begotten from the first night. They laid a snare for her through the First M[a]n. She was brought up by the hand of the Living Spirit, and set in this composite univers[e and set] he[r], above and below. The [upper side is the sense] organs of males; the lower side [is the sense organs of] fe[mal]es.

**¶12** ⟨p.27⟩ The thir[d night is the five] worlds of flesh [ten worlds of flesh,] male, and five female; that were begotten from the powers [abo]ve. They have fallen upon the earth, being displayed in wh[at is d]ry and what is moist; together with the fire and the lust [that d]we[l]l in them, which drives them inward to one another.

**¶13** [Mat]te[r] herself, the thought of death, is the [mother] of them all; as among them she is named 'the night'. [The worlds] of male and female fleshes, together with the fire and the l[ust], shall [make up the] twelve hours of [this third] night. Again, [it] too, the third night that is in the worlds of fl[e]sh, was begotten from the second night; which the Living [Spir]it crucified in the uni[verse and set]ove and below in heaven [and in earth].

**¶14** The fourth night is the law of Sin, which is the dark spirit who speaks in the twelve spirits, the twelve sects. They are the nakedne[ss]es, the twelve zodiacal signs of Matter. They are her thrones; she who is made public, as she sculpts and is displayed in the old man.

**¶15** And, also, the hours of this fourth night that is the old man, who reigns in the sects, are the twelve e[vi]l spirits; the [powers] of the old man, which [that proceed and]fferentiate the day and night [in the rhythm] death.

**¶16** This is the fourth night [and these are the nights] that have come forth of one another. One has [been begotten] just like the days of lig[ht and] they manifested one another. So als[o in turn] they did arise out of one another.

**Spiritual narrative:** The first complete state of illumination is the Divine Itself as the source of truth, the first principle abiding within the living order of heaven. Its full cycle is the entire organization of primary divine operations within that order: the first outgoings brought forth so that the Divine greatness might be received as manifest, arranged throughout the whole ultimate expanse in full completeness, set by discrete degrees in every quarter before the Divine presence.

The second complete state of illumination is the mediating divine operation that communicates between what is highest and what is beneath, abiding in vessels of enlightened knowing in the outer degree. Its full cycle is the entire complement of pure receptive affections of truth called forth within that greatness.

The third complete state of illumination is the universal sustaining stability that serves as boundary and guardian, surpassing all lesser safeguards, holding together what is higher and what is lower and bearing the equilibrium of the whole. Its full cycle is the sufficient complement of operative powers belonging to the good that first engages evil, together with the sufficient complement of operative powers belonging to the living Divine action that rescues and orders, and with them the directed influx and the responsive compliance that complete the series into one whole of light.

The fourth complete state of illumination is divine truth made resplendent within the gathered community of reception. Its full cycle is the full organization of distinct forms of wisdom by which that community is enlightened.

And as there are complete states of illumination, so there are complete states of obscuration.

The first state of obscuration is the realm of active falsity. Within it is a full cycle of darkened perceptions as measured stages of blindness. This fullness is made up of a sufficient series of fundamental falsities pouring out from perverted faculties of sensation and discernment; along with further disorderings that are mixed into those falsities and their extensions; and within them dwell animating persuasions that make the falsities living shadows.

The second state of obscuration is the formative principle of the natural level when separated from heaven, which fabricates delusive appearances and, with them, the whole dominion that rules in falsity. It organizes this dominion through perverted sensory forms arranged as paired impulses within each organized domain, by which falsity and evil conjoin and multiply; together with the burning of self-love and the craving that dwells within corrupted affections and inflames them toward mutual binding.

This same formative intention of spiritual death gave the ruling falsity of that realm and its subordinate persuasions strength for conflict against heaven. Yet that ruling falsity was restrained: it was ensnared through the good that first engages evil, seized through contending persuasions, removed from the state of spiritual death, and compelled into fixed positions of function throughout the universal order, in sufficient divisions set in higher degrees and in lower, together with the opposing inclinations that act as generative forces there—the ardor of self-love and the craving that proceeds from it.

This second obscuration is produced from the first, and it too was restrained through the good that first engages evil. It was drawn up by the living Divine action and fixed within the mixed order in higher and lower degrees, so that its upper aspect operates through distorted sensory forms in the corrupted active principle, and its lower aspect through distorted sensory forms in the corrupted receptive principle.

The third state of obscuration is the full series of corporeal loves in organized forms of both kinds, generated from persuasions set above. These fall into the lowest degree, becoming manifest both in what is devoid of living truth and in what is merely natural truth, together with self-love and craving within them that drive them to conjoin inwardly.

The formative intention of spiritual death is the common origin of them all, and so within those domains it is named as obscuration itself. The organized corporeal loves of both kinds, together with self-love and craving, constitute the full cycle of stages of this third obscuration. This third obscuration, existing in corporeal domains, is begotten from the second, and the living Divine action holds it fixed throughout the universal order in higher and lower degrees so that even what is lowest is compelled into boundaries and uses within a mixed arrangement.

The fourth state of obscuration is evil reigning as law: a dark ruling spirit that speaks and works through a full set of falsified doctrines and divided persuasions. These are evils exposed without any covering of truth, a complete arrangement of delusive generalities produced by the separated natural formative principle; and they are the mind’s seats of judgment, by which that principle is openly expressed as it forms and displays itself in inherited selfhood.

The measured stages of this fourth obscuration—namely the inherited selfhood ruling within divided persuasions—are the full set of evil loves and falsities proceeding from that selfhood. By them the mind sets up distinctions not from life but from deadened discrimination, establishing a rhythm of spiritual death.

These are the states of obscuration that come forth from one another. As the states of illumination stand in ordered succession and make their pattern manifest by degrees, so these states of obscuration also arise in sequence, each brought forth from what preceded it and each making the next possible within the same perverted progression.

---

## Chapter 5: Concerning Four Hunters of Light and Four of Darkness.

**¶2** The first hunter is the First Man who was sent from the greatness. He threw himself down to the five storehouses of Darkness, he caught and siezed the enmity [with his powers;] his net also [he spread] out over all the children of Darkness [and cast it]. His ship is his four sons who are swathed over his body. The sea is the land of Darkness [in the abyss]; his net is [his righteousness] and his powers.

**¶3** The second hunter is the Third Ambassador. This one, for by his light image, which he revealed to the depths below, he hunted after the entire light that is in all things; as it is established in them. His net is his light image, [by which he seized] the whole universe and took it prisoner, to this likeness [and he brought] ship is his light ship. [The sea] is the universe [and the worlds] which were hunted after by his net [and were gathered by] his [glorious] image.

**¶5** The fourth hunter is the great counsel that [lives] that lives in the circuit [of the Light] ⟨p.29⟩ entire universe in it today. Yet, at the end, in the dissolution of the universe, this very counsel of life will gather itself in and sculpt its soul in the Last Statue. Its net is its Living Spirit, because with its Spirit it can hunt after the light and the life that is in all things; and build it upon its body. Its ship, in which it is established, is this light cloud whereby it itself travels in the five elements [of the worlds], the great fire that will burn all the buildings of [in the five elements; and at the end,] in its net is the light and the life. It can rescue and free it from all bonds and fetters.

**¶8** The first hunter is the King of they who belong to the Darkness, who hunted after the living soul with his net at the beginning of the worlds. His net is his fire and his lust that he has put upon the living soul, with which he has entangled it [at the beginning], through all his powers.

**¶9** [The] second hunter is the evil counsel that lives in [the circuit of Darkness] that hunts after the light [that is mingled] and [drags it] up from [from all] the earths to heaven. It binds them with its powers, which bring them to the heavenly worlds above. Its net is [persuasion] whereby they are drawn up from the abyss [to the heights]hts.

**¶10** The third hunter is lust [which] walks in every power of the flesh that walks [about] in the [members of the body,] the living souls [and it binds] them in its bodies, which [it makes them serve death].

> - ¶5 [none]: Genuine difficulty from heavy corruption plus likely internal conflation, producing the main stumble (the paragraph calls this figure ‘the fourth hunter’ even though only two prior hunters are preserv

> - ¶9 [none]: Clean correspondential teaching with minor copying/orthographic damage at the end (“hts” and double ‘from’). The logic remains functional: persuasion is the ‘net’ that lifts/binds.

**Changes:** No changes made. All findings (1–6) recommended “none” (excise 0; annotate 0), so every paragraph is returned unchanged.

**Spiritual narrative:** The first restraining power is the first direct engagement of heavenly good with hellish evil, sent forth from the highest Divine fullness into the lowest disorder. It let itself down into a sufficient complex of interior domains of falsity, and there it seized and held the active opposition against heaven by the full extension of its operative powers. It encompassed all derivative evils and falsities within that disorder, so that none could escape its grasp. The containing form by which it acted in ultimates was a complete set of outermost powers joined to it as one body, and the general field into which it descended was the external plane ruled by falsity. The means by which it bound and restrained was the steadfast ordering of truth in ultimates together with the powers that proceed from that truth.

The second restraining power is a Divine mediation in a further discrete descent, which by a manifested form of wisdom let down into the lowest levels sought out and reclaimed all the good and truth dispersed and held fast within every state and system, just as they were fixed in their places. Its means of gathering was that manifested wisdom itself, by which it comprehended the whole compass of the created order and brought what was recoverable into subjection to a heavenly form. The containing form in which it operated was an enlightened vessel prepared for this work, and the general field opened to its action was the universal external complex made accessible by that descending appearance of glory.

The next restraining power is the Divine providential intention of salvation, a supreme governing design that remains living as the encompassing order within which the whole created arrangement is still sustained. Yet at the final unmaking of a perverted order, this same counsel of life will draw all its operations back into unity and bring its own receiving form to completion in a final state prepared for everlasting stability. Its means of gathering is its proceeding Divine vitality, because by that proceeding it can seek out and reclaim the good of love and the truth of life present within all things, and conjoin them to itself as one body of use. The containing form in which it is fixed for operation in ultimates is an enlightened encompassing medium by which it advances through a sufficient series of fundamental states; and in that final time the fervor of judgment will consume all organized evils and falsities built up against heaven. Within this gathering, what is taken up is the good and truth of life itself, and it can deliver and set free whatever is held in compulsions, constraints, and confirming bonds.

Against these stand predatory forces. The first is the ruling love of those who belong to hell, which from the beginning of a new spiritual arrangement sought to seize the living faculty of reception and make it serve disorder. Its means of entangling was self-love inflamed into craving, imposed upon that living faculty, by which it bound and confused it through the full extension of its subordinate evils and falsities.

The second predatory force is a perverse planning that abides within the same disorder, which hunts for whatever of truth still shines while mingled with evil and drags it up from the lowest natural states into lofty appearances. It binds such things by persuasive powers that lift them into exalted seeming realms while separating them from genuine good and use. Its net is coercive persuasion itself, by which what was low and obscure is drawn into high falsity.

The third predatory force is craving in its most immediate operation, moving through every corrupt affection of the outer self and roaming through all the forms by which the natural person acts. It pursues living faculties wherever they are and fastens them into embodied evils, so that what should live from heaven is made to serve what is spiritually dead, and the soul is held in servitude to destruction.

---

## Chapter 6: Concerning the Five Storehouses that have poured forth from the Land of Darkness since the [Beginning]; the Five Rulers, the Five Spirits, the Five Bodies, the Five Tastes.

**¶2** Five storehouses have arisen since the beginning in the land of darkness! The five elements poured out of them. Also, from the five elements were fashioned the five trees! Again, from the five trees were fashioned the five genera of creatures in each world, male and female. And the five worlds themselves have five kings therein, and five spirits, five bodies, five tastes; in each world, they not resembling one another!

**¶3** The King of the world of Smoke [is the ruler] who came up from the depth of darkness; this is he who is the head of all wickedness, and all malignity. The beginning of the spread of the war occured through him; all the battles, fights, quarrelling, dangers, destructions, fights, wrestling-contests! That is the one who first made arise danger and war, with his worlds and his powers. Afterwards, also, he waged war with the light. He pitched a battle with the exalted kingdom.

**¶4** ⟨p.31⟩
Now, regarding the King of Darkness, there are five shapes on him! His head is lion-faced; his hands and feet are demon- and devil-faced; his shoulders are eagle-faced; while his belly is dragon-faced; his tail is fish-faced. These five shapes, the marks of his five worlds, exist on the King of the realms of Darkness!

**¶5** Now, there are five other properties in him. The first is his darkness. The second is his putridity. The third is his ugliness. The fourth is his bitterness, his own soul. The fifth is his burning, which burns like an iron () as if poured out from fire.

**¶6** [There are] also three others in him! The first, that his body is hard and very tough, even as she has formed him [in evil and] cruel-hearted; namely Matter, who is the thought of death, [o]ne who sculpted him from the nature of the land of darkness. This is the manner of the body of the ruler of Smoke. He is harder than every iron, copper and steel and lead; as there is no cleaver at all, nor any iron implement, can [break] him and cut him. For Matter, his fashioner, has formed him [exceedingly] strong and hard.

**¶8** ⟨p.32⟩
The third, that the King of the realms of Darkness knows the converse and language of his five worlds. He understands every thing he hears from their mouths, as they address one another; each one of them in his language. [Every] design they will consider against him, every snare they debate with one another to bring upon him, he knows them! He can also understand the gesturing they signal between one another. Yet, his powers and his rulers, who are subject to him, can not understand his wordy converse. While all these things are unveiled to him; still, their heart is not manifested to him. He knows not their mind nor their thought; he can not ponder their beginning and their end. Rather, he only knows and apprehends what is before his eyes.

**¶9** Also, another different thing is found in the King of the realms of Darkness! For when it pleases him to move, he spreads all his limbs out and walks. When it comes to mind, he with[d]raws his limbs and takes them in, and is rolled (?) to his companions; and he falls to the ground like a grape and a great iron ball! He terrifies by his cry, he is frightful. He frightens his powers with his sound; because when he speaks, being like thunder in the clouds, he resembles the [crash] of the rocks [when they are split] When he cries out and [roars] and calls [aloud] over his powers, they shall tremble and to[tter a]nd fall under foot; even as some birds would be [driven by] the bird [hawk] and they fall down to the earth. Still, this thing only: he knows not what is far from him, he sees not who is at a distance, nor does he hear him. Rather, whoever is before his face he sees, hearing him and knowing him.

**¶10** These signs and these evil marks are found in the chief of the demons and fiends, the King of all the mountains of Darkness [who is the foremost of them,] to [whom] the land of darkness has given birth, begotten in its cruelty, in its wickedness and its wrath [and reared in its poison.]

**¶11** ⟨p.33⟩
... more than all his fellow rulers, who are in all his worlds.

**¶12** Gold is the body of the King of the realms of Darkness. The body of all the powers who belong to the world of Smoke is gold. And also, the taste of its fruits is salty.

**¶13** In contrast, the King of the worlds of Fire is lion-faced, the foremost of all the beasts. Copper is his body. Again, the body of all the rulers who belong to Fire is copper. Their taste is the sour taste that is in every form.

**¶14** Again, the King of the worlds of Wind is eagle-face. His body is iron. Also, the body of all they who belong to the Wind is iron. Their taste is the sharp taste that is in every form.

**¶15** For his part, the King of the world of Water is fish-face. His body is silver. All the other rulers who belong to Water, silver is their body. Also, the taste of their fruit is the sweetness of water, the sweet taste that is in every (form).

**¶16** Again, [the Kin]g of the world of Darkness is a dragon. His [body is lead] and tin. All the other rulers [who belong to the world of] Darkness, their body is lead and tin. And also, the taste of their fruits is bitterness. Again, the spirit who reigns in them is the spirit who speaks till today in the soothsayers, giving oracles; in the givers of portents, every type; in they who are possessed; and the other spirits that give oracles, every type.

> **Textual criticism:**

> - ¶4–¶5 [none]: Clean fivefold correspondences (shapes; properties). The five faces/properties function as diagnostic manifestations of one principle, matching the chapter’s internal fivefold pedagogy; no sign of a f
> - ¶6 [none]: Genuine difficulty from damage/ellipsis and a local voice wobble due to broken syntax (“even as she has formed him… namely Matter…”). The narrative does not improve by removal; it is a corrupted trans
> - ¶8–¶9 [none]: Clean (though highly imagistic) functional description of perceptual limits and kinetic capacities of the ruler. The reader’s ‘smoothing’ reflects dense metaphor, not administrative overlay.
> - ¶10–¶11 [none]: Genuine difficulty: ¶11 is fragmentary and functions as a damaged bridge into the next section. Not an insertion; it’s a broken continuation of the supremacy motif.
> - ¶12–¶15 [none]: Clean five-world exemplification in correspondential mode using ‘body (metal)’ and ‘taste (fruit)’ as functional manifestations. The kings are not arranged as an administrative roster; each world is c
> - ¶16 [annotate]: Mixed ending: primarily correspondential (metal/taste), but it adds an application gloss identifying the reigning spirit with contemporary oracle/possession practices. This is not ‘administrative’ (no

**Changes:** No changes made. The only non-core material identified by the critic is the concluding ‘till today’ application gloss in ¶16 (Finding 8), but the recommendation there is annotate (not excise), so the text remains unchanged. All other findings recommend none.

**Spiritual narrative:** From the first rising of a state of denial, a sufficient set of interior reservoirs was opened within that domain, and from those interiors a sufficient series of fundamental perversions flowed forth. From that series, fixed systems of distorted perception were organized; and from those systems there were brought forth complete kinds of disordered affections and thoughts in every degree of life, each joined in a counterfeit union of love and understanding. In each such degree there reign a sufficient set of dominant loves, together with their persuasive spirits, their embodied habits and compulsions, and their delights of appropriation; and in each degree these differ, because each degree bears its own specific quality of evil and falsity.

The ruling love that presides over the obscuring persuasion rising from the lowest denial came up from what is most remote from heaven, and it became the head of the whole complex of malice. Through it the first spreading of spiritual conflict broke forth: contentions against truth, assaults upon good, mutual hatred, peril to what can live, and the destruction of what might be restored. It first stirred the lust of domination and opposition, together with its organized degrees and operative forces; and afterward it set itself against the influx of wisdom and rose into direct opposition to the higher government of good and truth.

In that chief ruling evil there is a full set of outward aspects that display the quality of the whole. In its highest it is a proud and cruel confidence as chief among ravenous affections; in its deeds it is the delight of doing harm and of subjugating; in what supports and carries its rule it is a lofty, far-seeing craft; in its inmost craving it is a devouring appetite to possess and consume what is another’s; and in its ultimate extension it is a cold, sensuous persuasion that drags everything down into mere outward knowing. These are the complete impressions by which that kingdom is recognized.

There is also in it a sufficient set of ruling qualities: first, the absence of wisdom that it takes for its own light; second, the corruption of every affection for good; third, the deforming of all truth into what serves self; fourth, the interior sting and bitterness that it experiences as its life; fifth, the burning zeal of self-love that inflames as if it were strength and presses forward with harsh fervor.

Further, there is in it a formed character that resists amendment: it is stiff and unyielding because it is shaped from the intention of spiritual death, from a will set against heaven. That intention makes its confirmed state hard to break by any ordinary correction, for it has been made firm by repeated acts and therefore does not easily submit.

Also, that chief ruling evil has a shrewd perception of the utterances and reasonings that arise within its subordinate degrees. It catches the sound and manner of their persuasions, understands what is exchanged among them in their various modes of falsity, and perceives the schemes and devices by which they would protect themselves or evade restraint; it even notices the silent agreements by which they consent together. Yet the subordinate forces do not likewise grasp the deeper purposes of that ruling evil, and although much is laid open to it, it does not penetrate the inner ends of others as they are in themselves; it does not know their ruling mind, nor can it comprehend the whole course of their designs from first to last. It apprehends only what is present and immediate, what lies within its current awareness.

Moreover, it can alternately expand itself into active power and contract itself into a compact, oppressive presence. When it is stirred, it sends its influences outward into many operations; when it turns back into itself, it draws its operations inward and becomes a concentrated weight among its allies, pressing downward into the lowest loves. It terrifies by the violence of its persuasive outcry and shakes its subordinate forces by asserted certainty that drowns discernment; and when it summons its own, weaker impulses and subordinate falsities fall into tremor and collapse, as thoughts without support fail under a stronger persuasion. Yet it has no true foresight: it does not perceive what is remote in consequence, nor what lies outside its immediate sphere, but attends only to what is directly before it.

These are the evil impressions found in the chief love of infernal persuasion, brought forth by a domain of denial and nourished by cruelty, wickedness, and wrath, surpassing the other ruling evils that preside in its several degrees.

In the degree where falsity is most glittering and most convincing in externals, the embodied form of that ruling evil is a show of preciousness without genuine worth, and the whole company of its subordinate forces appears in the same kind of splendor; and the delight by which it appropriates is a harsh, biting relish, because it delights in desolating and in making what is living barren.

In contrast, in the degree where self-love burns openly as dominant zeal, the ruling evil is marked by proud confidence as chief among brutish affections. Its embodied form is the hardness of outward good turned to selfish use, and the forms of its subordinate rulers are similar; and the delight by which it appropriates is a pervasive sourness, because even what seems lively in it is inwardly hostile to innocence.

Again, in the degree where persuasion is volatile and domineering in thought, the ruling evil is marked by lofty craft in reasoning. Its embodied form is rigid severity in truthless confidence, and the forms of all who belong to that persuasion are of the same kind; and the delight by which it appropriates is a sharpness that cuts, because it delights in disputation and in wounding truth.

In the degree where truth is falsified and made to serve sensuous pleasure, the ruling evil is marked by a cold, outward intelligence. Its embodied form is a pale appearance of truth without life, and the forms of its subordinate rulers are similar; and the delight by which it appropriates is a sweetness that seems gentle, because falsity mimics truth and insinuates itself pleasantly into the natural mind.

Finally, in the degree of active denial itself, the ruling evil is the devouring craving that destroys spiritual life. Its embodied form is the heaviness and baseness of what is merely ultimate, compounded of gross falsity with its dull confirmations, and the forms of all who belong to that denial are of the same kind; and the delight by which it appropriates is bitterness, because it hates good and therefore feels good as torment.

And the persuasive spirit that reigns in these states continues wherever falsity claims revelation apart from heaven: in self-derived predictions, in every kind of deceptive sign-making, in states where another’s falsity takes possession of the mind, and in all varieties of pretended inspiration that draw their voice not from the Divine but from lower loves.

---

## Chapter 7: The Seventh, concerning the Five Fathers

**¶2** Five fathers exist; they were summoned forth one of one. [Also], one did come out of another!

**¶3** The first Father is the Father of Greatness, the blessed one of glory, the [on]e who has no measure to his greatness; who also is the first only-begotten, the first eternal; who exists with fiv[e fa]thers for ever; the one who exists before every thing that has existed, and that will exist.

**¶4** Now he, the glorious Father, summoned from him three emanations. The [fi]rst is the Gre[at] Spirit, the first Mother, who came out of the Father. She appeared first. ⟨p.35⟩

**¶5** The seco[nd i]s the Belo[ved of] the Lights, the great glorious Beloved, [the one w]ho is honour[ed; w]ho came out of the Father. [He ma]nifested ou[t of h]im.

**¶6** The third father is the Thir[d Ambassador], the eldest of [a]]] the counsell[ors; who came out of] the first Father. He appeared.

**¶7** This is the first Father, the first power, the one from whom the three great powers came out. This is the first Father, the first eternal, the root of all the lights; from whom the three emanations came out. They have humiliated the Darkness. They have brought its heart's desire to naught. They have given themselves the victory. They have also given the victory to their aeons.

**¶8** The second father, who came out of the first Father, is the Third Ambassador, the model of the King of lights. And again, he too summoned and sent out of him three powers.

**¶9** One is the Pillar of Glory, the Perfect Man; the one who bears up under all things; the great Pillar of blessing; the great porter, who is greater than all the porters.

**¶10** The second is glorious Jesus the Splendour; [the] one through whom shall be given life eternal.

**¶11** The third is the Virgin of Light, the glorious wisd[o]m; the one who takes away the heart of the [rul]ers and the powers by her image, as she fulfils the pleas[u]re of the greatness.

**¶12** The third father, who came out of the second father: he is glorious Jesus the Splendour. And, again, he too summoned three evocations after the pattern of the second father.

**¶14** [*] 

**¶16** The fourth father is the Light Mind, the one who chooses all the churches. And, again, he too summoned three powers after the pattern of Jesus.

**¶20** And also the fifth father is this Light Form; the one who shall appear to everyone who will go out from his body, corresponding to the pattern of the image to the apostle; and the thr[ee] great glorious angels who are come with her.

**¶22** These are the five fathers who have come out of one another. They have appeared and man[ifes]ted through one another!

> - ¶4-¶7 [none]: Clean substrate correspondential teaching of emanation by function and effect: triadic procession and its spiritual operation (humiliating Darkness, giving victory) are internally coherent.
> - ¶8-¶11 [none]: Clean triadic repetition within the same correspondential pedagogy (patterned procession + functions). The named figures are not merely listed as jurisdictions; each is given an operative description 
> - ¶10 and ¶12 [annotate]: Likely seam/overlay identification that introduces a system-specific christological name into a repeating pattern, creating the only real conceptual ‘stumble’: the same figure (‘Jesus the Splendour’) 
> - ¶14 [excise]: Insertion/expansion of administrative-cosmological placement into an otherwise functional emanation list. The paragraph shifts from ‘summoned three evocations’ (expected: list of three functions) to a
> - ¶16 [none]: Genuine difficulty likely due to lacuna/abridgement rather than insertion: it asserts a triad “after the pattern” but does not list the three powers. This matches the spiritual reading’s ‘informationa
> - ¶20 [annotate]: Mostly correspondential, but with a small historical/authority referent (‘apostle’) that the spiritual reading must abstract into a ‘teaching function.’ This looks like a minor overlay tag attached to
> - ¶22 [none]: Clean inclusio closure that matches ¶2 and seals the procession narrative. No voice conflict.

**Changes:** Per Finding 6 (¶14, recommendation: excise), removed the administratively framed and astral-topographical insertion about “the great Judge… dwelling… under the twelve wheel and the stars,” which disrupts the triadic functional list. No other paragraphs were modified; Findings 5 and 8 were annotation-only, and all ‘none’ recommendations were left unchanged.

**Spiritual narrative:** There is a sufficient series of principal origins in the Divine order, and each is brought forth in turn by directed influx, so that what is later has its life from what is prior. The first origin is the unbounded Divine Itself, blessed in self-existent glory without limit, the sole fountain of all derivation, eternal in itself, present with the whole series of derived origins, and prior to every state that has been and that will be. From this inmost Divine proceeded three primary determinations: first, the universal receptive principle of wisdom formed from love, the primal womb of spiritual formation, appearing as the earliest manifestation from the inmost; second, the beloved outshining of intelligible glory, the honored manifestation of Divine truth made present for conjunction; third, the chief mediating and revealing principle, eldest in counsel, standing forth as the first emissary of the inmost toward lower degrees. In this first origin is the first power, and from it these three great powers go forth as the root of all illumination and understanding; by their operation denial and falsity are brought low, their cravings are reduced to nothing, order is restored as victory within them, and that victory is communicated to all heavenly communities that depend upon their influx.

From the first origin there proceeds a second principal origin, the chief mediating revelation as the living pattern of Divine rule in truths; and from it likewise go forth three powers. One is the sustaining fullness of the Divine Human as perfected spiritual humanity, the universal stability and blessing by which all things are upheld in order, the supreme ministry that supports every lower reception. The second is the saving manifestation of Divine truth as enlightening splendor, through whom eternal life is given. The third is pure wisdom unmingled with falsity, whose presence in form removes the persuasive center of dominion from ruling evils and falsities, accomplishing the delight of Divine greatness by restoring right order.

From this second origin there proceeds a third principal origin, the same saving manifested truth in its operative coming; and from it, in the likeness of the prior order, three further operations are called forth. Among them is the great discriminating function that sets every soul into spiritual equilibrium, weighing each one according to love and faith, established in the intermediate region where outward thought meets inward intention, under the general ordering of knowledges and their fixed patterns.

A fourth principal origin then stands forth as enlightened understanding from the Divine, which discerns and chooses all genuine assemblies of worship and doctrine; and from it also proceeds a triad of powers according to the same saving pattern. Finally, a fifth principal origin appears as the Divine form of light presented as each person’s reception of heaven after the laying aside of natural life: the disclosure of spiritual identity according to the implanted image of Divine truth given to the teaching function, together with the complete ministry of higher aids that attend that unveiling. Thus the sufficient series of principal origins is brought forth one from another, each appearing through the other, so that the Divine descends by ordered degrees and is received according to every level of life.

---

## Chapter 8: Concerning the Fourteen Vehicles that Jesus has boarded.

**¶3** The first vehicle is the light ship, since he received instructions from the Ambassador there.

**¶4** ⟨p.37⟩ The second carriage is the ship of the First Man, since his dwelling is established there.

**¶5** The third is the Pillar of Glory, the Perfect Man, since he shone forth there. He came below, he appeared in the world.

**¶6** The fourth is the summons that he clothed upon his body. The fifth is the obedience that he swathed upon the summons.

**¶7** The sixth is the living air, and since he assumed it too, he received anointment there. The seventh is the living wind that blows. The eighth is the light that illuminates. The ninth is the living water. The tenth is the living fire that is above them all.

**¶8** He was journeying in these ten carriages among all the orders and all the [aeons], those above and those below.

> - ¶4 [none]: Named figure (“First Man”) could be read administratively, but the sentence is still functional (dwelling/established there) and remains inside the same enumerative schema without introducing ranks, s
> - ¶5 [none]: Mythic titles are doubled (“Pillar of Glory, the Perfect Man”) and could reflect harmonizing/identificatory layering, but they still serve the correspondential movement (shining forth → descent → appe
> - ¶6 [none]: Compression and metaphor-density (“clothed…swathed”) create interpretive strain, but the teaching remains functional: summons → obedience bound to summons. This is not an administrative insertion; it 
> - ¶7 [none]: Clean correspondential chain (air→anointment; wind; light; water; fire above all). No narrative break; the list is process/quality-based rather than administrative.
> - ¶8 [none]: Coherent concluding summary that gathers the vehicles into a universal traversal; no sign of later administrative overlay (no new names, no ranked inventory).

**Changes:** No changes made. All findings (1–6) recommended none; there were no excision targets to execute.

**Spiritual narrative:** The first means of Divine progression was an initial enlightenment in the outer mind, because there directive truth was received through a mediating holy influence. The second means was an ordered plane of general knowledges, because within that support the good that contends with evil was given a settled basis for its action. The third means was the exalted manifestation of the Divine in complete humanity, because in that fullness love and wisdom shone forth openly; from that fullness the Divine flowed down into lower degrees and became perceptible within the natural level. The fourth means was the inward call of Divine truth taken into full union with the assumed human. The fifth means was the human level’s willing compliance, bound to that call so that the lower might act from the higher. The sixth means was living spiritual respiration taken into union, and therefore consecrated by love for use. The seventh means was the living motion of that respiration as operative spiritual power. The eighth means was enlightening discernment that gives understanding. The ninth means was living truth that cleanses and instructs. The tenth means was living love reigning above them all and governing them. In this full series of sufficient means, the Divine advanced through every ordered level and community of heaven and the church, in higher states and in lower, leaving no degree untouched.

## Chapter 9: The Explanation of the Peace, what it is; the Right Hand; the Kiss; the Salutation.

**¶2** You have questioned me about these five lessons. Now, they are displayed in the world as miniatures, being made little; and yet indeed they are great and honoured. I am the one who can reveal to you their mystery! So, these five signs are the mystery of the First Man. He came forth with them from the aeon of light. Also, when he finished his contest, he went up with these good signs. They received him into the aeons of light.

**¶3** ⟨p.38⟩ The first peace is the one that the gods and the angels in the land of light gave to the First Man; when he comes out against the enemy. The gods and the angels were walking with him; escorting him, giving to him their peace and power, and their blessing and fortification. This is the first peace that the gods and the angels gave to the First Man; as he comes forth from the aeon of light.

**¶4** The first right hand is the one that the Mother of Life gave to the First Man; when he comes out to the contest.

**¶5** The first kiss is this one with which the Mother of Life embraced the First Man; as he separates from her, coming down to the contest. Even all the gods and the angels who are in the aeons of light were making to embrace him! [With her] all of them also, and the church tutelaries (?), and the kinsfolk who belong to the household of his people. They were making to embrace him as [he] separates from them, accompanying him out, embracing him with the kiss of affection and love.

**¶6** ⟨p.39⟩ The first salutation is the salutation with which the First Man made obeisance; when he comes out [to the depths] below. He was bending his knees, making obeisance to the God of truth, and all the aeons [of light] who belong to the household of his people; entreating them for a power to escort him, as he comes forth.

**¶7** The first laying on of hands is the one that the Mother of Life bequeathed upon the head of the First Man. She armed him and made him mighty. She laid hands on him, and sent him to the battle. He descended. He completed his struggle with the great powers and the enmity.

**¶8** By the [se] five signs and these five myst[e]ri[es] the First Man came forth from the aeons of light against the enemy. He humbled him. He was victorious against him by them.

**¶9** Also, when he ascended from the war, he came in to the kingdom of the household of his people by these five mysteries!

**¶10** The peace with which [he ascen]ded is the peace that the summons gave to him, [w]hen it was sent forth from the [Fa]ther of Life. It gave him the peace in the world of darkness. Also, that peace with which he descended is th[e sam]e as the one with which he ascended from the struggle! Therefore, appropriate to the my[s]tery of that peace, which the summons gave the First Man, did this peace here and now come about. It was named and heard among the powers of the Father.

**¶11** Again, this second right hand is the one that the Living Spirit gave the First Man, when he [brou]ght him upward out from the struggle. Appropriate to [the] mystery [of t]hat right hand this right hand has come about; the one that occurs in the [midst of] mankind, as they give it to their companions, [it b]eing honoured and [glorified]

**¶12** Also, this second kiss is the one with which the [Father o]f Life and the Mother of the living embraced the First Man, when he ascended from the struggle. Again, this kiss occurs among mankind, as they make to embrace their companions with it; whether then [they] go away from home and be far from their friends, or else [if] they approach one another, according to the mystery of (the First Man).

**¶13** ⟨p.40⟩ [The] second salutation with which the First Man made obeisance [in] time to the God of truth and [all] the blessed aeons and al[l] the aeons [in] whole [land] of light, whe[n] the Father of Life and the Mother of the living brought the Man upward from the abyss of the struggle. Appropriate to the mystery of the second salutation this salutation has come about; this one that occurs today in the midst of mankind, as they make obeisance to one another, doing an honour to their companions.

**¶14** [*] The second laying on of hands is this: When the Living Spirit brought the First Man upward from the war, he saved him from all the surging waves. He brought him upward, he gave him ease in the great aeons of light, which belong to the household of his people. He set [him] firmly before the Father, the Lord of the totality. He received this great laying on of hands that he may become leader of his brethren in the new aeon. Again, appropriate to the mystery of the second laying on of hands, this laying on of hands has come about; the one that occurs in the midst of mankind as they lay hands on one another.

**¶15** These five mysteries and these five signs came about first in the divine. Mankind has been taught them; and they have instituted them in their midst [in forms;] these mysteries were not at the beginning amongst the powers of darkness.

**¶20** Once again: At the time of their coming forth, the Light Form shall come forth before them; and she redeems them from the darkness to the light [and she makes them peaceful.] This Light Form calms the person, with the kiss and her quiet, from fear of the demons w[ho de]stroy his body. By her asp[ec]t and her image [the] heart of the elect one, who is come forth from his bo[d]y, shall be calm for [him]. Afterwards, the angel who h[o]lds the victory prize extends to him the right hand. And it draws him out of the abyss of his body, and accepts him in with the [k]iss and love. That soul shall make obeisance to its red[eem]er, who is this Light Form. And also, at the inst[instant when he makes obeisance,] he shall be perfected and increased according to [the ordinance of the living ones] in the household of the living ones, with the go[ds] and the angel[s] and all the apostles and the chosen. [An]d he receives the crown [and the robe of] glory in the life for ever.
