**Spiritual narrative:** You have asked about five holy operations. Though they appear among people in a diminished way, they are in themselves great and held in honor, because I can open their hidden depth. These five operations are the inner secret of the Divine good that first went forth from heaven furnished with them, and after its trial returned with them into heaven, where heaven received it.

The first operation is the peace that the whole heaven imparted when the Divine good advanced to meet hell. All heavenly societies were in consort with that advance, surrounding it with companionship, communicating inward tranquility and power, and establishing it in blessed firmness; for such is the peace heaven gives when Divine good goes forth from heaven.

The first communication of power is the strengthening that Divine wisdom gave to Divine good when it advanced into temptation.

The first conjunction of love is the intimate union by which Divine wisdom joined itself to Divine good as it separated into the lower state for trial. All heavenly societies pressed into that union, together with every guardian care and every kindred good and truth within the one heavenly household, because they longed to accompany the saving descent with affection and love.

The first reverent acknowledgment is the humility by which Divine good bowed itself when it entered the lower states. It submitted itself in worship to Divine truth itself and to all heavenly orders within one household, and it petitioned that accompanying power might be granted as it went forth.

The first investiture is the authority and might that Divine wisdom placed upon Divine good. By that empowerment it was armed and made strong, and was sent into spiritual battle. It entered the lower state, carried the struggle through, and brought it to completion against the great aggregated forces and their hostility.

By these five operations the Divine good went forth from heaven against hell, subdued it, and conquered by them.

And by these five it also returned from the conflict into the heavenly kingdom of its own household.

The peace in which it returned is the peace given by the Divine call that proceeds from the inmost Divine; this peace reached it even within hellish obscurity. The peace with which it went down into trial and the peace with which it rose out of it are one in essence; therefore the peace that now exists among the heavenly powers took its form from that same hidden peace, and it is known and received in heaven.

The second communication of power is the empowering that the restoring Divine operation gave when it lifted the Divine good up from temptation; from the inner pattern of that empowering comes the honored practice among people of communicating faithful support and strength to a companion.

The second conjunction of love is the embrace of reunion from the inmost Divine and from Divine wisdom when the Divine good ascended from temptation; from that hidden pattern comes the human practice of joining in affection with companions, whether in separation and distance or in renewed approach.

The second reverent acknowledgment is the worship rendered in due order to Divine truth and to the whole blessed heaven when the inmost Divine and Divine wisdom raised the Divine good out of the lowest distress of trial; from that hidden pattern comes the human practice of honoring companions by humble acknowledgment.

The second investiture is the confirmed conferral of authority when the restoring Divine operation raised the Divine good out of conflict and saved it from the tumult of falsities. It introduced it into ease within its own heavenly societies, established it firmly before the inmost Divine who rules all things, and by this great confirmation made it able to lead kindred goods and truths in a new heavenly state. From this hidden pattern comes the human practice of conferring authority, in which what is greater strengthens what is lesser.

These five hidden operations first existed in the Divine itself. From there people have learned them and set them among themselves in outward forms; but they did not exist at the beginning among hellish powers.

Again, when those who are to be saved are brought forth from natural life, a manifestation of saving good and truth appears before them and redeems them out of evil and falsity into good and truth, establishing peace. This manifestation quiets the person by conjunction of love and by a stilling influx, removing the terrors that arise from hellish persuasions intent on destroying the last order of life. By the clarity of that presence and the recognizable form of mercy, the inner affection of the one being saved is calmed as the person is released from the natural.

Afterwards, the confirming heavenly power that holds the sign of victory communicates support and elevating strength, drawing the spirit out of its lowest entanglement and receiving it into love and peace. The saved one then offers humble acknowledgment to the Divine that redeems; and in that acknowledgment the person is perfected and enlarged according to heavenly ordinance within the society of the living, among angels and all who are in truths from good. Thus the person receives confirmed victory and a state of glory within life that does not end.

---

## Chapter 10: Concerning the Interpretation of the Fourteen [Great Aeons] of Light, about which Sethel has spoken in [his Prayer]

**¶3** ⟨p.43⟩ Happen you know this, that the [seven praises] below abide in fourteen [great aeons of light]: the five elements, the sons of the First Man, together with the call and the hearing. They anoint them, living in them. These shall make seven, five plus two.

**¶4** [There are also the] f[ive s]ons of the Living Spirit who [support] all [the wei]ght of the universe; together with the [Li]vi[ng] Spirit [and the Fir]st Man, their fathers. These are the fourt[een p]raises that he has proclaimed, the praise that is made of fourteen pra[yers], by which the universe is established.

**¶5** [*] [There are fourt]een aeons of light [of the great aeons of light] of Seth [e] ... ] about them in his prayer, for they are expl[ai]ned [in the land that is not revealed]nd that is not revealed. This land [which is established and made firm]lished. They bore up under the enemy [and nothing among them equals]ls them.

> **Textual criticism:**
> - ¶3 [none]: Clean correspondential substrate: a functional grouping (5+2=7) presented as an interior anointing/indwelling, with no jurisdictional list-making beyond the numeration.
> - ¶4 [none]: Mostly correspondential but with mild systematizing pressure: the paragraph shifts from indwelling/anointing (¶3) to cosmic support/establishment language; still, it remains functional (supporting wei
> - ¶5 [excise]: Genuine difficulty from damage plus a probable seam where a mythic/administrative christological promotion overlays a prior correspondential point about hidden stability. The reading stumbles because 

**Changes:** Implemented Finding 3 (¶5) excision: removed the enthronement/governance overlay beginning with “The hidden plenitude that [is within them] the Ambassador…” through the end of the paragraph (“…to the end of all things”) to restore a coherent correspondential statement about the concealed land’s firmness against the enemy. No changes to ¶3 or ¶4 per Findings 1–2 (recommendation: none).

**Spiritual narrative:** Know that in the ordered fullness of interior light there abides a complete acknowledgment made up of a sufficient series of fundamental falsities and the operative powers that proceed from them, together with the twofold proceeding by which influx is directed and then received. Divine love consecrates these things and gives them life within their recipient forms, so that the whole becomes a full complex: what is sufficient for operation conjoined with the marriage of willing and understanding.

There is also a sufficient complement of sustaining powers that uphold the entire weight of the created effect in the lowest degree, together with the two principal origins from which those powers are derived. This is the complete acknowledgment that has been uttered: worship brought to fullness by a complete measure of supplications, through which the created order in its lowest degree is held in stability.

Further, there are complete interior states of light within the highest ordering, and the inner church has spoken of them in confession because they are set forth within doctrine concealed from what is merely external and not yet disclosed. This concealed doctrine is established and made firm; it endured the assault of evil, and nothing in what opposes could be equal to it or prevail against it. Within these things there is a hidden fullness.

Therefore Divine truth proceeding from Divine good was sent forth and took its station with power in the midst of these living ordered realities. It is the beneficent origin of all interior states and of all holy truths conjoined into one. It became the leader and governing wisdom in the created order, according to the likeness of the Divine itself, which is the source and lord of all orderly dispositions. Thus the whole created effect in the lowest degree is held together by their united willing, for their counsel and their thought are one, even to the completion of the process.

---

> - ¶6 [excise]: Insertion of administrative numeration and territorial distribution into a functional conflict line. The core ‘fighters begin from the Living Spirit’ is correspondential; the clause about distributing
> - ¶7-¶8 [none]: Clean correspondential pair: building and bearing/support are taught by function (architect/builder; porter/pillar). No governance vocabulary; the spiritual reading proceeds without strain.

**Changes:** Applied the critic’s excision recommendations in three places: (1) ¶3 removed the administrative addendum (“king… realm… steward and ordainer…”) per Finding 2 to preserve the correspondential maxim. (2) ¶6 removed the deployment clause about distributing “five sons… from place to place” per Finding 5, leaving the functional source-line intact. (3) ¶9 removed the identifying/glossing expansion (“Jesus the Splendour]; or the one who delivers,”) per Finding 7; the remaining sentence was minimally smoothed only as needed after excision to avoid a dangling connector while keeping the deliverance clause unchanged in substance.

**Spiritual narrative:** Every opening of worship and every motion of turning toward the Divine begins from the wisdom-principle that proceeds first in holiness, because without wisdom no blessing is formed and no prayer is received. Every beginning of sound counsel and every true ordering of what is useful begins from the governing truth that administers the lower realm of reception under the Divine source of all illumination. Every beginning of genuine reverence and every act of honoring begins from love united with wisdom, for love is what makes the Divine precious and truly acknowledged. Every beginning of the power that searches, detects, and exposes what is hidden begins from the first engagement of good in human form against evil, by which what is harmful is tracked and brought into judgment. Every beginning of the power that contends in spiritual conflict begins from living Divine operation, the origin of vitality, which has established a sufficient complement of operative powers in distinct functions throughout the whole field of reception. Every beginning of the power that designs and builds stable forms for life begins from the Divine ordering that shapes every true framework of thought and use in glory. Every beginning of the strength that bears what is weighty in the ultimate degree begins from the steadfast ultimate support by which higher things stand in the lowest plane and are held together. Every beginning of deliverance begins from Divine truth made manifest in clarity, which frees whatever is capable of salvation from falsities that bind, dissolving them by its own utterance and presence. Every beginning of wisdom that is truly of truth begins from pure receptivity for illumination without self-claim, through which truth is conceived and kept untainted for life. Every beginning of the most honored states within the domains of the mind begins from enlightened understanding, which awakens those in obscurity and gathers back into order those whose interior things have been scattered.

---

**Spiritual narrative:** They turn their understanding toward the inmost Divine, confessing it as Life itself and the prior source of all things. They also confess that this inmost cannot be seized by any created intelligence nor fixed by any defining thought, so that its quality is beyond every name and beyond the grasp of those who receive life from it.

They then acknowledge a distinct proceeding from that same inmost: a living mediation by which life is brought forth and made present to what is below. This proceeding bears the character of its source and thus acts as a begetting principle with respect to everything that comes from it, yet it remains subsequent and subordinate to the inmost Divine, which alone is the first and unoriginated Life.

---

**¶3** The second resurrector is [the Living Spirit].

**¶5** The fourth resurrector is the Light Mind, because [he enlightens the lives and turns them toward their likeness]. ⟨p.46⟩

**¶6** The fifth resurrector to the good [lives] is the Father, the First established one [in his firmness]. [He binds] all the powers, and he stands firm [in the root of his stability]. He shall unveil the [veil] over them [that was spread], and he draws them to him [by the attraction of his light], his will [is their measure], and he [establishes] them, and he stands them up [in their rank] there, and they remain until [the completion].

> **Textual criticism:**
> - ¶2 [none]: Fragmentary start of a numbered correspondential sequence; the reader stumbles because the first item (the ‘first resurrector’) and its subject are missing, leaving only the tail of a functional claus
> - ¶3-¶6 [none]: Clean correspondential substrate: a coherent sequence of restorative operations described by what each agent DOES (gathering by ‘word’, enlightening, binding/ordering, unveiling, drawing). The only ‘s
> - ¶6 [none]: Apparent ‘rank/order’ language is not an administrative insertion here; it is integrated into the correspondential mechanics of restoration (re-establishing powers into their proper functional order).

**Spiritual narrative:** In that operation, living goods and truths are set within the forms prepared to receive them.

A second restoring operation is the Divine vitality that proceeds to reawaken what has fallen dormant.

A third restoring operation is the Divine Itself in first permanence, because by Divine truth all living goods and truths that have been dispersed are gathered back into unity and returned to the Divine; then they receive the form proper to their renewed state and enter peace.

A fourth restoring operation is enlightened understanding, because it illumines living goods and truths and turns them back toward the image of their own intended quality.

A fifth restoring operation for those who are in good is again the Divine Itself in first permanence and stability. It holds all subordinate powers bound together in one steadfast order, and remains unshaken in the inmost ground of permanence. It removes the covering that has been laid over them, and draws them into conjunction by the inward compulsion of Divine wisdom. Divine love becomes the measure of their willing, and by that measure they are re-established, raised into their proper order and function, and kept there until the process is brought to its completion.

---

> - ¶3 [none]: Clean correspondential substrate with typical Manichaean mythic naming, but the teaching still proceeds by function (initiation, dwelling, sharing light) rather than by territorial administration. No

**Spiritual narrative:** When the power that recollects what has been dispersed returns into rule within the full cycle of a person’s states, it draws the whole outward life into unity, subordinates the cravings that cling to external things, and restrains bodily impulse under what is just. In this ordering, the whole composite of faculties is set into a central support of Divine radiance fixed in a higher degree, because the lower powers are upheld by the descent of higher life, and the entire person is lifted toward fuller reception.

The one held in honor is the initiating good that first meets evil and begins restoration. It abides in the outermost knowledges made living by genuine truth flowing through them, and within that outermost it dwells in the wisdom of the first arising of spiritual life. It is therefore called initiation, because those who recognize it are given a glorious share of that illumination.

The one that enlightens discernment is the clarity by which truth is distinguished from falsity, and by that clarity the whole domain of the mind’s life is arranged as it must be in the greater spiritual state. The ordered truths and the living recipients of them are extended through the entire system of thought and affection, and this arrangement is fixed within the interior degrees and within the living forms by which they are received and held.

---

No other changes: Findings 3–4 recommended none, and Finding 5 recommended annotation only (no textual modification).

**Spiritual narrative:** For the third completion, when denial rose again into conscious activity, it set the lowest obscurity in motion and drew it into a single operative mass; and those who were immersed in the most external and buried falsities were stirred within the first established state of their opposition.

After a long interval, because there was no peace from any reception of living good and truth, what lay shut up within that denial forced itself outward from its inward recesses. A sufficient series of primal corruptions issued from the first intention of spiritual death: obscured understanding, self‑inflaming love, unstable persuasion, falsified truth, and active negation of good. These surged forth again with renewed insistence, and they were fixed into determinate habits and settled modes of life, until spiritual death stood established within them as their ruling principle.

From this there followed a further completion: works wholly evil, brought forth through a sufficient set of organized distorted perceptions, each operating within its own general domain of falsity.

Then denial extended itself into all derivative lives throughout those general domains—lives fashioned and propagated from those evil works. This became the full array of merely bodily life and the total system of falsities in which spiritual death was made to dwell. And it divided into a complete duality, sufficient in the active form and sufficient in the receptive form, so that corruption might operate through both.

And as the lowest falsity, by its ruling affection and dominant persuasion, sustains those primal corruptions, so also this complete duality fed the burning of self‑love and the craving of merely bodily delight. By binding the active and receptive together and yoking them into one force, they were set fast in opposition to the first good that directly confronts evil. In this is the terminal state of such denial: it was judged and held outside heavenly order without end.

Along with this there came forth, within that denial, a hidden fullness of falsities organized as a complete set, all established against the first good that confronts evil. This fullness manifested as an entire series of ruling errors—total persuasions of falsity—arising from a complete framework of outward knowledges perverted into governing influences that mislead.

---

## Chapter 16: [Concerning the Five] Greatnesses who [went forth] against the Darkness.

**¶1** “[When the battle was raised against] the darkness, it was defeated by five greatnesses.

**¶2** The first greatness is the Father of Greatness who exists in calm and hiddenness; as he is established in his light-earth, in his own essence.

**¶3** [*] Four greatnesses were manifested and came forth of him.

**¶4** The second greatness poured out and was manifested. It was revealed from the first greatness. It is the Mother of Life, the Great Spirit, together with the First Man and his five sons; these are the second greatness.

**¶5** ⟨p.50⟩
The third greatness is the Beloved of the Lights, the Great Builder, the Living Spirit, and the five sons of the Living Spirit. These are the third greatness.

**¶6** The fourth greatness is the Third Ambassador, and the Pillar of Glory, and all the light powers who were revealed from him. These are the fourth greatness.

**¶7** The fifth greatness who came from the Father is Jesus the Splendour, and all his powers whom he summoned, his emanations who were [revealed in the elements] They poured out and were manifested from him.

**¶8** [The advent] of the First Man who came [from the heights to the depths] belonging to [his own household] existing in a kingdom [and is found] esteem before they who belong to his household [and honour] his enemies [but] and suffering by their wickedness; and they take from him [come upon him] and a richness; and they imprison [him in] captivity and take from him their companies (?). Yet, he [his servants] by his humility; and he takes heart and restrains his enemy [endures] in silence.

**¶9** Just as with this man, this noble son whose enemies surround him, this also is how the advent of the First Man happened, [among the] rulers, the enemies of the light.

**¶10** In contrast, the advent of the Living Spirit and the commandment that he received, whereby he came upon all the rulers, it differs from that of the First Man. His advent [is not like] the advent of the First Man.

**¶11** ⟨p.51⟩
The advent of the Living Spirit is like this, when he was sent from the greatness that he might bring up the First Man. He resembles a judge the king would send to overturn the violence to that man, the noble son against whom his enemies sinned. And he, this judge, [is firm] hard and cruel [against those who are] and he gives the means [means of salvation] by his righteousness and his strength; and he also judges all who are condemned according to their rebellious heart, and repays each according to his deserts. They who are worthy of blows he strikes with hard leather whips. Again, these who are worthy of cutting off their limbs, or indeed their [their ears] and he repays them accordingly. [Again, those who are worthy of imprisonment] and he binds and fetters them, and hangs by [their feet] and thus he does to them with a righteous judgement. And he kills them to flay their skin [and flay their flesh] so that they will be killed by a death [that is strange and bitter] until they die. Again, [they for] whom it is fitting [to cast out] to banish from their dwellings he may remove according to a righteous judgement; just as they had faulted and sinned against the noble son.

**¶12** Also, the associates in the mystery and the assistants of this judge, these who belong to him in great strength; in an instant and haste they shall do the will of their master.

**¶13** Now, just as this genuine judge, who was sent from the king to overturn the violence to this man, the noble son: this is also the case with the Living Spirit. He also was sent from the King of the lights to overturn the vengeance and violence to the First Man, against whom the King of the realms of Darkness had sinned. He faulted and sinned against him, and also his rebellious powers. Therefore, when (the Living Spirit) came, he bore up the First Man.

**¶14** Also, all the rulers, the powers of sin who had faulted and sinned against the sons of the First Man, he has judged them according to a righteous judgement. He has bound them in heaven and earth. He put each one to the place fitting for him; he weighed each of them according to his cruelty and oppression. While some of them he enclosed in the prison, others he hung head down. Some [among them] are crucified, others are sat down all the time. Some among them are fixed beneath their associates, restricted by a strong chain; others he made the holders of authority over their companions beneath, so that they may do their pleasure amongst these below them.

**¶15** ⟨p.52⟩
Happen you know this [thing,] that when the Father of Life established the judgements [and established] He was revealed to all the powers of darkness. He judged them as a true giver of judgement [in righteousness.] This is the first judgement that the Living Spirit gave the rulers [in respect of the] First Man. Indeed, he gave victory to the First Man corresponding to his victory; but he condemned the King of the realms of Darkness corresponding to that by which he had erred.

**¶16** [Thus] this chain, of this sort, became [fixed] the earth till the end time when the universe will be dissolved; and it perishes in the great fire and is separated. While the light goes to its country, the darkness remains in the chain and fetter for ever.

**¶17** The advent also of the Third Ambassador, when he came to inspect they who are imprisoned in the zone: he is like a great king who comes and inspects craftsmen [and soldiers] and [fortifications] and [guards]

**¶18** Also, they who are good, of the craftsmen and architects and artisans whom he has there in each place: he shall see that they have worked well, and have fallen to his works in their fashion. He shall rejoice with great joy [in their presence] He shall thank the artisans who have constructed his works [and completed] his orders.

**¶19** Now, just as this great king who comes and inspects his works and fortifications, so also is the advent of the Third Ambassador. For, when he too came, he saw [his works] established and prepared and [arranged] series of the rulers prepared and established there, above and below, outside and within, in the great pattern that his brothers had made. They had come before him to the contest, they who had fixed and created the universe before he came. He saw the gods and the angels established and controlling the fortifications [in a] guarding the entire ruling-power [of the universe] which his first powers had done. They are the First Man and the Living Spirit and the Great Spirit, the Mother of Life.

**¶20** Therefore, when he saw them, that they had created everything with great wisdom, [he revealed the hidden things] as they looked he plundered (?) [to them] in his advent, by which he came, he was unveiled to them. He says to the Living Spirit: “The judgement that you have [done is] good, what you uttered [to each one,] you gave to them correspondingly; so that they were moved and raised up to that of which they were not worthy”.

**¶21** ⟨p.53⟩
The advent also of Jesus the Splendour, the time when he came to all [the powers below,] being just like a man if he is sent to pull out a [root] burn a difficult fig-garden with fire. Therefore, again, when he has first cut the bad trees with his axe and has pulled out [their roots,] and their body with his fire, so that they should not return to sprout from this time, nor bear fruit bad to eat [from them.]

**¶22** Afterwards, he also planted good plants: the tree of life that will make good fruit.

**¶23** Again, this is like this simile of the advent of Jesus the Splendour. It happened like this; because he too set out [to the zone,] his power and his wisdom, in his [compassion]-ness [as] a king and a saviour [and as a healer;] in his glorious wisdom he destroyed them. He bound and crushed them like the fire that shall burn and destroy trees. This is also how he performed his will in the zone, among the many powers, until he reached the form of the flesh of Adam and Eve, the first humans. [He perfected] his will in Eve, he [purified her.] He also gave hope to Adam, and the good news [that he might live;] glorious [that he would be saved,] He went up to the heights [in the glory] of light.

**¶24** ⟨p.54⟩
Then the summons and the obedience, the great counsel that came to the elements, which are set in conjunction: it mixed with them, it was established in silence. It bears up [it] until the end time when it can arise and stand firm in the great fire. It will gather to it its own soul, and sculpt it in the Last Statue. You will also find it sweeps out and casts from it the pollution that is foreign to it. However, the life and the light that are in all things it gathers in to it, and builds upon its body.

**¶25** The time when this Last Statue will be perfect in all its limbs, then it can become free and ascend from that great struggle through the Living Spirit its father, the one who comes and brings a [each] limb. **¶26** Now, this counsel, that summons and obedience, resembles thus cooled butter. If a person will take and set it in hot milk, it melts down and soaks in with this hot milk. It melts in another time in silence and quiet to the midst [of it,] yet also, of the power and the taste of this milk [that] is drawn to (the butter), and (the milk) sets and becomes cold [and] in [again,] and the [whey] this milk it shall clarify and discard. However, the oil and the grease and the ‘beauty’ of the milk it shall gather in to it.

This is the one that at the end can arise and stand firm, and will gather in to it all the lives, and the light that has remained behind in everything. It can build them upon its body; it will gather them in to it [in calm] in its image for ever and ever [in its form] from this time.

**Changes:** Executed the critic’s excision recommendations only: (1) Finding 3—removed from ¶3 the numeric administrative clause “They were appointed to ten parts, and established in total twelve,” leaving a coherent emanation statement. (2) Finding 5—excised ¶8 in full (fivefold spatial deployment seam-expansion). (3) Finding 6—excised ¶9 in full (twelve-parts + summons/obedience succession/governance seam-expansion). All other paragraphs unchanged; Finding 4 was ‘annotate’ only and required no textual modification.

**Spiritual narrative:** When opposition from active falsity rose up out of obscurity, it was subdued by a sufficient series of Divine operations.

The first operation is the inmost Divine Itself, abiding in peace and unsearchable interiority, remaining within its own wisdom in the purity of its own being.

From that inmost Divine, derivative operations came forth into manifestation. They were disposed into complete conjunction as to the working powers in the outer degree, and they were established as an entire organized body in fullness.

The second operation proceeded and came into manifestation, revealed from the first. It is the wisdom-principle in living activity, together with the good that first engages evil in order to restore order, and with a sufficient complement of subordinate operative powers acting from that good.

The third operation is love for all truths and goods ordering and forming them for use: the living proceeding Divine that builds and disposes, together with a sufficient complement of subordinate powers proceeding from it for executing its uses.

The fourth operation is mediation in its third degree, together with the upholding Divine that sustains what has been arranged, and with all the luminous powers of understanding and use brought forth through it.

The fifth operation proceeding from the inmost Divine is truth in splendour as it reaches the outermost, together with all the powers it calls forth for operation—its derivations that pour forth and come into manifestation from it.

This was arranged so that the Divine operations might be extended in sufficient series: one sufficiency as the subordinate powers of the first engaging good distributed into full function and support, and another sufficiency as the subordinate powers of the living proceeding Divine extended into a full set of positions and uses, sustaining the ordered whole.

Thus the whole was made complete in organized fullness. To that fullness two were added—reception and compliance—so that responsive conjunction might exist. These were appointed as a succeeding principle in place of the Divine splendour in the lower degree, to govern the outermost plane in which all things are held together.

The descent of the first engaging good occurred as when noble truth from a higher state is present among those of its own spiritual household, yet is assailed by opposing falsities and suffers from their evil. They strip from it what is serviceable and abundant, confine it under their dominion, and attempt to deprive it of its attendant powers; yet it endures by humility, gathers courage, and restrains the opposing force by withholding consent, remaining in interior peace.

As noble truth is surrounded by adversaries, so this descent took place among dominant falsities, the adversaries of illumination.

But the descent of the living proceeding Divine and the mandate it received, by which it came upon the dominant falsities, differs from the first descent.

This descent of the living proceeding Divine is that it was sent from the Divine operations to lift up the first engaging good. It is like authoritative judgment from the highest order overturning the violence done to noble truth by falsities that violated order. That judgment is executed with unwavering firmness against what is cruel and perverted; it provides means of restoration through righteousness and strength; it evaluates and condemns all that is condemned by rebellious intention, repaying each according to its quality. What requires sharp correction it chastises; what must be deprived of ability to harm it renders powerless in the faculties by which it injured; what must be held fast it binds in lasting constraint, fixing it in inverted states; what is wholly against good it extinguishes as to power of acting, so that the destructive tendency is brought to an end; what must be removed from former influence it casts out from the spheres in which it dwelt, according to just order.

The associate powers within this hidden working, belonging to that judgment in great strength, instantly execute the will of the governing Divine.

As authoritative judgment was sent from the highest order to overturn violence done to noble truth, so it was with the living proceeding Divine. It was sent from the Divine of illumination to overturn vengeance and oppression against the first engaging good, which the ruling falsity of the obscured domain had committed with its rebellious derivatives; therefore, when the living proceeding Divine came, it raised up the first engaging good.

It also judged all dominant falsities and evils that had violated order against the subordinate operative powers of that first engaging good. It fixed them in constraint in higher and lower degrees, assigning each to the state fitting to it and weighing each by its cruelty and oppression: some it shut within interior restraints; others it turned into complete inversion; some it fixed in grievous exposure and immobilization; others it forced into perpetual passivity. Some it placed beneath the influence of their like, limited by strong restriction; others it allowed to hold delegated dominance over those beneath them, so that their delight might be spent upon those in similar disorder.

Know, then, that when the Divine of life set order by these judgments, it was made manifest even to the powers of obscurity. It judged them as genuine judgment itself. This was the first judgment the living proceeding Divine executed upon the dominant falsities in respect to the first engaging good: it gave victory to that good according to the nature of its victory, and it condemned the ruling falsity of the obscured domain according to the manner of its perversion.

Thus the fixed constraint remained in the outermost degree until the end, when the entire formed system is dissolved: evil perishes by final ardour brought to consummation and separation is made. What is of illumination returns to its proper state; what is of obscurity remains in everlasting restraint.

The descent of the third mediating Divine, when it came to examine those held bound in the intermediate region, is like governing providence inspecting those performing uses and maintaining order.

It discerns those who are good in their functions—those who form, arrange, and execute within each assigned position—and sees that they have worked well and have entered the uses of the whole according to their proper form. It rejoices and confirms those who carried out the ordered work and fulfilled what was commanded.

As governing providence inspects its ordered works and the protections of order, so is this descent. When it came, it saw the ordered structures established and prepared, and it saw the series of dominant falsities disposed and fixed in their places above and below, outwardly and inwardly, according to the great pattern the preceding operations had made. It saw the heavenly and spiritual ministrations established, administering the protections of order and guarding the whole government of states—those first operations: the first engaging good, the living proceeding Divine, and the wisdom-principle in living activity.

Seeing that all had been formed and ordered with great wisdom, it brought what was hidden to disclosure and by its presence made itself manifest to them. It affirmed the living proceeding Divine’s execution of judgment as being according to order, so that each received what fit its state, though some were moved and lifted into states beyond what they merited.

The descent of truth in splendour to the states below is like decisive purification sent to uproot what is obstinately evil and to consume a difficult network of perverted perceptions by ardent love. It first removes hardened falsities by the cutting power of truth, and it extirpates root-intentions, consuming their persuasions by purifying ardour so that they do not revive or bring forth destructive works.

Afterward it implants living good and living truth that bring forth beneficial works.

So it was with truth in splendour: it went forth in power and wisdom in the lower degree, and by glorious wisdom destroyed resisting evils and falsities. It bound and crushed them as consuming ardour destroys what is perverted in perception and its resulting works. Thus it carried out its will in the intermediate region among many powers, until it reached the outermost human state in its first formed condition, where the first recipient stood ready. It completed its work in the receptive affection and purified it; it gave hope to the understanding and announced restoration and illumination; then it elevated that state toward higher reception of light.

Then responsive conjunction—reception and compliance—proceeding into the ultimate constituents set in mutual bond, entered into them and remained held in interior peace. It sustains the ordered process until the end, when it can arise and stand firm in the final ardour that makes separation. It gathers to itself what is its own life, forms it into the final unified human form of order, and purges away whatever is impure and alien to it. But the life and illumination dispersed through all states it gathers into itself and builds into its own organized fullness.

When this final unified form is perfected in all its powers and functions, it becomes free and ascends out of the great contention through the living proceeding Divine as its originating support, which comes and brings each power into its place, lifting it out from the midst of the collecting and separating process in which all things are brought to their end.

This counsel of responsive conjunction is like gentle good held in a calm state: when introduced into living truth warmed by love, it yields and permeates that truth. It is received quietly into the inmost of that truth; then whatever is vital and delightful in that truth is drawn into the conjunction. Afterward what is cold, inert, and unsuitable is clarified and cast away, but what is pure, rich, and beautiful is gathered into the conjunction.

So also is the descent of that living counsel—responsive conjunction—into the ultimate constituents: at the end it arises and stands firm, gathering into itself all lives and the illumination that remained hidden throughout every state, building them into its own organized form so that they abide in its image to eternity from that time.

---

## Chapter 17: The Chapter of the Three Seasons

**¶1** From the time when the light came forth and was crucified in the darkness, to the season when the Statue will ascend and the entire light, which was conjoined with the darkness, will be purified and cleansed. From that time on, from that time to the season of the end totals three seasons.

**¶2** Three seasons: from the time when the First Man went down to the abyss, and waged the war and the struggle against the King of the realms of Darkness and his other powers. He was victorious, while his sons remain behind with them in the worlds [and in the heavens] in the [midst] until he shall catch all of them [worlds of darkness] he bound them all up, so that not one of them escaped his hand. He won a great victory [in his net] The First Man spent human lifetimes and generations in the land of darkness, until he prevailed [afterwards] ⟨p.56⟩ he fulfilled the will of the greatness that was commanded of him.

**¶4** The second season is from the time when the First Man ascended. The Living Spirit came, and the Mother of Life. They set in order the things of the universe, they set up [the firmaments] the Father of Life and the Mother of the living set in order [all] the universe, up to the moment when they go out [from it, until it was completed] it was reckoned to the second season.

**¶5** [Afterwards] the universe [was established,] the Third Ambassador spread [his light] that is in the [universe of the] living; he [gathered it;] he separated beauty from all the powers and scooped it up to the heights, he swept the waste to the abyss, and he [set a boundary between them] Now, from the moment when the Ambassador revealed his image he purified the light. Sin spurted up towards him. He hid his image. Sin came [up] from the darkness. It sculpted the trees and the abortions fell down. Afterwards they sculpted Adam and Eve in the flesh. Until the moment when Jesus was sent and he [again] as he performed there his will. He gave the hope to Adam, and ascended to the heights.

**¶6** Now, from the time when the Ambassador displayed his image until the season when the Statue will ascend at the end, shall be reckoned to the third season.

**¶8** [The first season] lasts long in the time that the First Man spent in the world of darkness. It is a very great count of time, more than the (other) two seasons!

**¶10** [He replies to them]: If you should say that [the intermediate season] it is small, if you should [consult someone] to him [again] and you consult [someone else] you will tell him: Twenty times! It is greater again in this other one that you will consult: Thirty times! Less also is this other one [in another reckoning,] people will receive the {} of the trees [fruits] the years and the generations [they will carry up from the] earth to the heights. This is [...

**¶11** This is the third period, from the time when the Ambassador revealed his image until the Statue will ascend from the lump. It again, that time, is greater than the intermediate; but it does not come equal to the time of the First Man. For this intermediate season, in which the Father of Life and the Mother of Life constructed the universe with its orders, is shorter than that of the First Man.

**¶12** Great is the period of the First Man! It lasts longer than the season in which the Ambassador displayed his image, until the dissolution of the world and the Statue ascends. From the time too when the Ambassador displayed his image, until the dissolution of the universe [it is greater than the season] when the Father of Life and the Mother of Life shaped the universe.

**¶13** These are the [only] three periods of time that occur. ⟨p.58⟩

**Spiritual narrative:** From the first emergence of wisdom into manifestation, and its being subjected to falsity and denial, until the final elevation of the ultimate recipient in which the whole measure of wisdom that had been entangled with falsity is separated, purified, and restored—between those two bounds there unfold three distinct processes.

The first process runs from the point at which the primal good that engages evil directly was let down into the lowest obscurity, where it entered combat against the ruling love of evil and the allied falsities that proceed from it. It overcame; yet the operative powers that belong to it were still held detained within those mixed lower states, amid the opposing forces, until the primal good gathered the dispersed elements back under order and constrained them so that none could escape its governance. It thus achieved a great victory. It endured long successions of states, as if through many lifetimes and generations, within that obscurity and opposition, until it prevailed and carried out the Divine intention laid upon it.

After this, a restoring Divine operation was sent forth, proceeding from sustaining good. By that operation the primal good was lifted out of conflict and brought back into the state of manifested wisdom and peace. This entire span—from the descent into the lowest obscurity to the ascent into illumination—constitutes the first process.

The second process begins from that ascent. Restoring power again went forth, together with the wisdom-principle that gives life. By their conjoint work the whole ordered structure of degrees and their containing forms was arranged and established, until the connected system stood completed. This span is reckoned as the second process.

Once that order was established, a further Divine manifestation extended living truth into the formed system for reception. Through it, whatever could be purified as beauty from the mixed powers was separated and gathered upward into higher states, and whatever was mere refuse of evil and falsity was driven back into the lowest obscurity, with a fixed distinction between what could be raised and what must be cast down. From the moment this manifestation was made perceptible, the purification of wisdom advanced; but evil surged upward in resistance, and the manifestation was withdrawn from open perception. Then falsity shaped systems of perverted perception, and from these issued works without genuine spiritual birth, which fell out of order. Afterward there was fashioned in the outermost degree a merely external semblance of the human—will and understanding bound to sensuous life. This continued until a saving influx of Divine truth was sent into those states; it accomplished its use there, implanted hope in the first formed human condition, and was then elevated again into higher illumination.

From the first perceptible appearance of that Divine manifestation until the final elevation of the ultimate recipient at the completion is reckoned as the third process.

The spans of these processes are unequal. The first—because it consists of sustained combat in obscurity and opposition—extends through an exceedingly great succession of states, far exceeding the other two.

If one attempts to measure the intermediate span, its magnitude varies by the standpoint of comparison: in one reckoning it seems multiplied many times, in another greater still, and in other reckonings less; for the ways lower states are received and carried on among many can prolong succession, while the elevation of what can be raised into higher illumination hastens conclusion. 

The third process—from the first perceptible manifestation until the final elevation of the ultimate recipient out of indistinct mixture—is greater than the intermediate process, yet it does not reach the vastness of the first. The intermediate process—in which restoring power together with life-giving wisdom disposed the ordered structure and its uses—is shorter than the first.

Great, therefore, is the duration of the first process: it exceeds the duration that runs from the perceptible Divine manifestation to the dissolution of the established system and the final elevation. And the duration from that manifestation until dissolution likewise exceeds the duration of the intermediate ordering by which restoring power and life-giving wisdom formed and arranged the connected system.

These alone are the three processes by which the whole course is measured.

## Chapter 18: [Concerning the Five] War[s that the] Sons of [Li]ght waged with the Sons] of Darkness.

**¶2** The first war is that of the First living Man, which he waged against the King of the realms of Darkness, and all the rulers that had come forth from the five worlds of Darkness. He hunted them with his net, which is the living soul. He bound them in the snare and sealed them the face of the Virgin of Light his virgin, the living fire shone forth, so that they did not find how to escape his net because he caught them like fish in the sea of smoke the five counsels of the five elements and seized. He cut and dug out the root of the five trees, root of the evil tree, like an axe.

**¶3** The second war is that of the Living Spirit which he waged against the rulers and the firmaments. He has constructed the things and the worlds of light; he spread out the rulers and chained them in the middle of the firmament the stars in all the earths and he set them in the three vessels. For the rulers rebelled and were set against the Living Spirit. They wished to flee since they knew and realised that he might bind and fetter them with a strong chain. Yet he, the Living Spirit, appeared to them with many aspects, with numerous powers, with many hands and great cruelties. He was revealed to them, he imprisoned them with much skill. Not one of them was able to save himself, and he did not prevail. Now, since he all of them he trapped them had foreseen their rebellion by their own devices.

**¶4** The third war is that of the Third Ambassador who has displayed his image in the height what is below and he gathered up scooped and he all light and the property of the rulers the ones that are consumed from the entire ruling-power and he uncovered pride of the light and the extending it over the whole. A person comes, who is powerful against it; he takes his riches and he stripped him, silver gold, his pride and wantonness.

**¶5** This is what the ruling-power is like: it is like a thief, all riches that it takes as they travel through the paths light by borrowed. He cleansed and purified it.

**¶6** [*] The fourth war is this that he waged against all the rebels who had rebelled above and below. When the light was purified from them by the image of the Third Ambassador, their chains loosened and they rose up. The glorious one in the fourth war; he came and brought and he restrained a rebellion of the entire ruling-power. He girded himself with the armour of truth all of it another time. He constructed the whole structure and he made and he bound a great contest; he humiliated therein. it firm he fulfilled the will of the greatness.

**¶7** The fifth war is this that the counsel of life waged against the persuasion which is the summons and the obedience and the great counsel great its power and it placed its seal upon the earth and the depth the bonds of death, which clung with it. And it was victorious over the persuasion for ever, and it gave the kingdom of life and of light.

**Spiritual narrative:** The first conflict is the first direct engagement of living good against the central dominion of evil and all the subordinate evils that had issued from a full sufficiency of fundamental falsities. Living good pursued and enclosed them by the living inward faculty that discerns and binds, so that their evils and falsities were held fast within a fixed restraint. Pure and uncorrupted love stood openly against them, and the living ardor of that love made their escape impossible; for the whole sufficiency of their contrivances, rising from the full set of elemental falsities, was seized and shut in. Then living good struck at their origin, removing the beginning-source of their organized perversions, and cut off the root-principle of evil with decisive force.

The second conflict is that of Divine operation in living power, which formed an ordered heaven and established the arrangement of goods and truths in their proper degrees. It disposed the rebellious dominations into their assigned states and bound them in an intermediate region, fixing their influences within a complete ordering of cognitions and ultimates, held inside the full structure of the three degrees of life. For those dominations rose up in resistance, wishing to evade restraint because they understood that Divine power can bind by an unbreakable connection. Yet Divine operation confronted them by many modes of presence, by a fullness of ordered powers, by abundant means of action, and by severe counteraction against their violence; and by wisdom in application it shut them in. Not one of them was able to rescue itself, and all their attempted evasions were brought to nothing; therefore, because their resistance was foreseen and met, they were taken in their own devices and held fast.

The third conflict is that of mediating Divine truth making itself visible and effective in the lower degree. It gathered up into itself all remaining good and truth, together with whatever spiritual quality had been carried off into what consumes, reclaiming it from the entire pretended dominion; and it laid bare that dominion’s exaltation, by which it had gloried in the pride of seeming illumination and spread its influence outward. Then a power came against it that was able to overcome, and it stripped that dominion of everything it boasted as its own—its accumulated cognitions, its glittering falsifications, and its arrogance and wanton delight in ruling.

Such is the nature of that pretended dominion: it steals every abundance, claiming as its own whatever goods and truths pass through the mind, and it lives by borrowing appearances as it moves along the ways of thought. Therefore what had been taken was cleansed and purified, with the living separated from the defiled and made clean.

The fourth conflict is that of Divine truth fully present in the human, waging against all rebellious impulses in the inner and outer degrees. When the reclaimed good and truth had been purified from those impulses through mediating revelation, the restraints that held the disorder in check began to loosen and the rebellion rose up again. Then the glorified Divine met the uprising of the whole pretended dominion. It invested itself anew with the full order of truth for combat and bound the disorder again; it rebuilt the whole framework of spiritual life and fixed a great contest in which the rebellious power was humbled and held firm. In this it fulfilled the will of the supreme Divine, and it gave hope to the first human state, offering preservation and conveyance for whatever remnant of living good and truth could still be saved.

The fifth conflict is that of the living counsel that flows from the Divine, which is the inward call toward good together with the reception that obeys. This counsel entered with great force into the ultimate and lowest degree and set a firm confirmation against the bonds of spiritual death that cling there. It prevailed over the persuasive compulsion toward spiritual death forever, and it established the reign of life and light.

---

## Chapter 19: Concerning the Five Releases: what they are

**¶2** [The first loo]sening and saving is that of [the] First Man, since when the First M[a]n was freed fro[m the prison of] the rulers by the Living Spirit, who had come [to the rescue] of the First Man, much light and [much] power was saved from the entire ruling-power. They ascended with the First Man to the aeons [of] greatn[ess]. This [is the first] release.

> - ¶7 [excise]: Expansion at a seam: the chapter promised ‘five releases’ as five salvific events/operations (¶2, ¶5, ¶6), but ¶7 redefines the fifth as an abstract institutional mechanism (“great counsel … summons a

**Changes:** Applied the critic’s excision recommendations only: (1) In ¶6, removed the institutional/mechanism insertion “He gave [the] summons and the obedience to the elements, he formed [J]esus the Youth” per Finding 4 (excise), and minimally repaired the resulting flow by joining directly to the ascent/rest conclusion. (2) Excised all of ¶7 per Finding 5 (excise), leaving the paragraph empty rather than introducing any editorial substitute. No changes were made to ¶8 or ¶9–10 because their recommendations were annotate only (Findings 6–7). All other paragraphs unchanged (Findings 1–3, 8: none).

**Spiritual narrative:** The first unbinding and preservation is the liberation of the primal good that had entered combat with evil. When Divine truth in living operation came to its rescue and broke the dominion of the organized falsities and evils that had confined it, much wisdom and much strength were recovered from that whole tyranny. What was recovered was raised with that primal good into higher degrees of life and into the greater Divine state.

The second preservation is the work of Divine truth in living operation itself. When the inmost Divine good together with the wisdom-principle brought forth the ordered framework of spiritual degrees and their outward extensions, setting each level in its place with the governing forces proper to it, immeasurable wisdom was withdrawn from the lower dominations that had seized and consumed what belonged to the primal good, and that reclaimed wisdom was gathered up and kept safe from further profanation; at this point the sequence becomes damaged and the narrative turns fragmentary.

What follows, though broken, continues the account as a series of complete spiritual combats, carried through a full sequence, until the victorious ordering power was established above the whole created arrangement, ruling from a higher degree over what had rebelled.

The third restoration is the work of the mediating Divine proceeding that follows after the first ordering. It made Divine quality visible in a form accommodated to those in lower states, set that manifestation directly before the organized ruling falsity, drew out what could be rescued from their grasp, and established it securely in a higher reception.

The fourth preservation is Divine truth revealed in glory. When it was made manifest in the intermediate region between higher and lower states, it presented the Divine openly before the fixed outer structures of heaven and purified the wisdom that remained above from admixture. It instituted the first genuine righteousness in the midst. It took on an accommodated appearance, making itself like heavenly affections and thoughts in the higher externals, until it could go on to descend even to the ultimate natural state where truth is clothed in the lowest form. Then it arranged and stabilized all lower domains and their fixed connections, loosened what had been bound, and released immeasurable wisdom throughout the whole ordered system. It sent forth a directed influx and produced responsive obedience in the most external knowledges and forces, and it brought into being a new operative form of truth for renewal and instruction. After this it rose again and came to rest in the state of pure wisdom and peace.

The fifth unbinding and preservation is the great inward counsel of providence itself—the directed influx and the responsive reception—by which the human mind is also drawn upward into the higher state so that restoration may proceed in order. 

Again the account returns to Divine truth in living operation as the giver of release to each operative power, describing successive stages: an ascent and strengthening through received wisdom and its ordering; further establishment of wisdom within the fixed outward forms; and then a final stage in which the whole is sealed into an ultimate recipient and set in order until it can bear a formed image of light in the last and lowest composite. 

These five great operative powers were made manifest and brought into effective use within the five unbindings, so that they were set upon their foundations within the five saving acts. Then the primal good, after returning from combat, was able at its renewed advent to arrange and build the whole system into a coherent order. Thus all operative powers stand, each in its own function and place. The closing fragment ascribes this stability to the wisdom that pressed onward from the mediating Divine intervention, though the precise linkage is obscured by the break.

## Chapter 20: The Chapter of the Name of the Fathers

**¶3** Now, the Father, who is the first established thing, shall be called ‘the Father of Greatness’. His greatness is the great earth where he lives, he being established in it [as the great earth, and his root is in it; and] the essence of the light that swathes all the rich gods and the angels and the dwellings [of this greatness; and the boundary of the light] is set over it.

**¶4** [*] His greatness is the light ship of living fire wherein he lives, he being established in it.

**¶5** [*] His greatness is the ship of living waters where he lives, he being established in it.

> **Textual criticism:**
> - ¶3 [none]: Clean correspondential substrate: the teaching proceeds by function (what “greatness/earth/light” does) and builds an organic cosmology (plane → indwelling → encompassing → boundary) without administr
> - ¶4 [excise]: Insertion of administrative titulature into an otherwise functional pattern: “Third Ambassador” is an office-name from Mani’s administrative cosmology, and it interrupts the otherwise purely descripti
> - ¶5 [excise]: Expansion at a seam via christological identification: the historical/theological proper name “Jesus the Splendour” is a Mani-layer identification that recasts a functional figure into a named authori

**Changes:** ¶4: Excised the naming clause “Furthermore, they shall call the Third Ambassador ‘father’.” per Finding 2 (administrative titulature interrupting the functional template). ¶5: Excised the naming clause “Once again, Jesus the Splendour shall himself also be called ‘father’.” per Finding 3 (proper-name christological identification as a seam expansion). ¶3 unchanged per Finding 1 (recommendation: none).

**Spiritual narrative:** The Divine as first made present and fixed for reception is acknowledged as the originating Love in unmeasured power. That power is the whole ultimate plane in which this Love abides as stable being; within it, wisdom in its pure essence shines forth and encompasses every abundance of heavenly goods and truths, together with all angelic states and all ordered communities of reception, and the outermost determination of that wisdom’s shining is set as what governs the whole.

Also, the mediating Divine that proceeds as a third in the order of manifestation is likewise acknowledged as an originating source. Its unmeasured power is the active conveyance in which love is alive within wisdom and wisdom is alive within love, and within that living union it dwells as what is fixed for use.

Again, the Divine Human made manifest as saving wisdom is likewise acknowledged as an originating source. Its unmeasured power is the living conveyance of truth as it proceeds from love into lower degrees for restoration, and within that living truth it dwells as what is established.

**Spiritual narrative:** The state of wisdom is immeasurably great for all who are willing to receive it and to understand it, because it is from the first Divine source of all greatness. That Divine is eternally in being and fixed in a sufficient fullness of luminous powers, within which lie infinite interiors containing every quality without boundary or measure: the capacities of intending, discerning, perceiving, judging, and attending; and also the complete organization of truths in their fullness, which are the total forms of wisdom in that Divine.

Within each of those sufficient powers there is again a sufficient fullness, and from that fullness a complete proceeding goes forth as living acknowledgment and strength, so that in every degree there is an ordered outflow.

The first proceeding is the Divine wisdom itself, which gives illumination to all and flows into every heavenly degree.

The second proceeding is the delight of love that makes wisdom sweet, and it permeates every state of greatness.

The third proceeding is living communication of praise, by which what is from the Divine is universally perceived and all the states are held together.

The fourth proceeding is the Divine firmness in good, which goes forth as steadfast power and extends outward so that even the outermost states may be held in order.

The fifth proceeding is the Divine glory in full manifestation, eternally revealed and without end, by which all things are sustained and held together. It is brought into determinate form within all the heavenly recipients at once and fixed in their inmost understanding, made permanent in them so that, as they transmit what they receive into lower degrees, they are also nourished and renewed from that same living communication. From it they receive immediate joy and are set up anew again and again. From this glory there is an unceasing outflow, and their states are enriched and made beautiful forever with the presentation of living wisdom, so that their whole outward expression becomes an adornment of life and they abide in the living order to eternity.

**Spiritual narrative:** Within the Divine realm of wisdom, the Divine established the whole order of heaven. I was in a supreme state of that realm, and from it I will disclose to you the realities that are interior and prior. In that interior state, perception is alive because it is borne and actuated by the living proceeding from the Divine. From wisdom’s shining and from the Divine presence there flows an outgo that makes what is within manifest; and within the organized heavens there is the present fullness of greatness, sending forth the acknowledgment of worship in living delight. A sufficient set of operative powers is conjoined into one, so that together they appear as a single spiritual form, and within that form the common atmosphere of life and the inmost vital activity are both living. As that sufficient set is cleansed from every opposition and striving that belongs to the establishing of greatness, it is delivered into concord, and a beautiful unity is formed through the whole complex of faculties, so that the entire spiritual person stands as a likeness of the Divine Human within the realm of wisdom, and life is in each part. Just as every heavenly and spiritual community, every abundance of good, and every ministering function that is called into use proceeds from the Divine, so also every elevation of state, every rising of interior truth, and every perception bearing uses proceeds from that same source and continually flows forth; great is the wonder that everything you perceive lives from that proceeding, and nothing is life from itself. These are ordered realities established in their proper bounds, though the intended sequence breaks where the words are missing. There is also a further degree in which wisdom in a third discrete level and the living common atmosphere of heaven are the dwelling-place of the higher states of greatness, fixed there as forms; and in it are ministering powers in stable function, and the stored gifts and endowments that the Divine bestows within the organized heavens of wisdom. Blessed is the one who comes into knowledge of these things.

**Spiritual narrative:** No finite mind can recount the inner being of the contrary principle, nor unveil the mode of its enduring, for it stands outside the order that makes spiritual life intelligible. Yet its universal powers and the universal dominations within it disclose enough to grasp its measure: a perpetuity of opposition, persisting without heavenly order, whose so‑called beginning and end are only the fixed boundaries of what is permitted.

Therefore the state of spiritual death is named as a distinct domain: a reigning quality of inverted life that holds together by conjunctions of evil with falsity, and of falsity with evil, so that it exists in a unity that is itself hostility.

No one can comprehend how such a dual opposition endures from itself, because nothing higher is made manifest within it that could speak from within it about its origin. Yet when it first roused itself into active resistance against wisdom, it desired not only separation but dominion over those who are in living good and truth.

Then the first going‑forth of saving power met it and restrained it, keeping it outside the community of life. From the time when wisdom was permitted to be entangled with falsity, and living good to be bound within what is dead, there arose a regulated measure by which the first recipient powers and those who teach afterward could speak from revelation about the conflict: how contempt and assault came to be, how wisdom was woven into falsity, and how life was held as if imprisoned within death.

After it stirred within its own essential quality, it turned its whole intention back into itself and drew its domain together into one concentrated endeavor, seeking to plunge whatever was of wisdom into the lowest obscurity so that no light would appear. This was sustained by a persuasion within that state that evil is strength and falsity is firmness, so that it seemed unbreakable, as though its prevailing power could not be resisted.

From the willing of spiritual death arose envious delight, which justified wickedness and sharpened the impulse to injure the opposed domain of life.

It then hastened from obscurity toward the outermost limits of life, gathering its operative powers into a single thrust; and the more deeply it pressed toward what is living, the more firmly its persuasions and delights held together within those powers.

It spread itself into a sufficient series of fundamental falsities: obscured understanding, burning self‑love, unstable persuasion, perverted truth, and active denial of good. From these falsities arose organized systems of perverted perception; and within those systems it generated many derivative compulsions and tendencies, unlike one another. In the interior depths of its lowest mind it produced abundant hellish affections and thoughts—destroying impulses, devouring persuasions, brutal cupidities—and it was fixed and strengthened in hideous spiritual forms. Because conjunction there is only by common hatred, their community became mutual slaughter and continual conflict.

Then the deliberation within the ruling love humbled and bent its whole external order and all its subordinate powers toward one aim. It perceived living good and wisdom above itself, and conspired to wage conflict against the exalted kingdom of heaven and against the ordered goods and truths there.

But the Divine Source of love and wisdom also perceived the permitted assault and its aim to disturb the heavenly state. Therefore, from mercy united with wisdom, it provided a mode of presence suited to the lower degree, setting in order a form of resistance by which what rose from below would be met and restrained without violation of what is higher.

At that time there was a commanded going‑forth of Divine operation against the uprising opposition.

The contrary principle poured itself outward into its fundamental falsities, rising from the lowest obscurity and establishing itself within perverted perceptions as fixed bases for opposition.

And when those perverted perceptions were in place, it brought forth works from them, holding its own life as if set within those works; and by these it summoned a sufficient complement of operative powers belonging to the first saving combatant, fitted for action in the outermost degree.

When, through those works, it generated further offspring of evil and falsity, these derivations descended into the natural degree and drew strength from the fundamental falsities, being nourished by them. Thus wickedness together with cruelty was gathered into the ruling love, the leader of all hellish forces. It bent its external orders and powers, drew them into one dominating evil, and made itself ready for conflict against the heavenly state in ultimates and against the Divine Kingship present within it.

At that very moment the first saving power drew near to its invested operative forces—the shining goods and truths fitted for combat. It spoke with them to entrust what must be done, revealing and teaching all that was necessary for action. It took them into itself, arranged and conjoined them into a single form of resistance, disposing external truths upon inner truths and fixing them throughout the whole capacity for use, so that it might meet the impending onrush with all its powers, though the full measure of the closing description cannot be completely unfolded from the fragmentary wording.

---
