> - ¶3-¶4 [none]: Clean rhetorical unit contrasting what is countable in creation with what is uncountable in divine duration; correspondential argument by function (measuring grains/sand vs inability to measure primor
> - ¶9 [excise]: Insertion into a functional narrative: a sudden administrative-cosmographic aside (“three…twelve hidden ones…light veil…south”) interrupts the otherwise correspondential teaching on immeasurable heigh
> - ¶10-¶13 [none]: Return to coherent correspondential teaching about placement-by-will and instantaneous perception; then functional ordering of powers (distribution, measures, silence at the border) and a poised inter
> - ¶14 [excise]: Administrative insertion into an otherwise correspondential duration-narrative: a systematizing list of named salvific officials with their dependents (“gods who came from them”), introduced with seam
> - ¶15-¶16 [none]: Despite heavy damage in ¶15, the unit reads as a coherent correspondential arc: uprising of darkness, three emanations sent ‘against the enemy,’ and the long period until unveiling/reintegration. This
> - ¶20-¶22 [none]: Coherent correspondential ‘mirror-process’ of Matter: staged becoming and incubation (storehouses → elements/trees/fruits → worlds/hells), described in developmental functions. The ‘five elements / fi
> - ¶23-¶29 [none]: A fivefold schema closes the chapter; mostly coherent as a summarizing didactic unit. The only real break is ¶24, where the text is too damaged to recover the first item. The fivefold structure is not

**Changes:** Applied two excisions per the critic’s recommendations: (1) In ¶9, removed the administrative-cosmographic locator phrase beginning “through one of these three that are counted to the twelve hidden ones…” through “…region of the south” (Finding 4) and closed the sentence coherently at “land of greatness.” (2) In ¶14, excised the entire administrative emanation-roster paragraph as specified (Finding 6); no replacement text was introduced.

**Spiritual narrative:** A vast discrimination came into effect, and a new state opened: the Divine brought into determinate order what would serve revelation, and the First Stable Foundation was made manifest within the inmost Divine.

When the Divine issued the full inward impulsion, wisdom was brought forth for the work and remained for an immeasurable state within that first inmost foundation.

Even if someone could set exact bounds and calculations for every least particular in the lowest degree, and for every gathered perception in elevated states, and for every general knowledge in externals, and for the whole expanse of ultimate frameworks; even if all these were poured out and compared with the completeness of ultimates and the containing forms that encompass the whole created order—still all such fineness of calculation would not reach the duration of that prior abiding.

The least particulars in the lowest degree, and even the least particulars of the whole created order, could in some way be numbered and surveyed; but the duration of the state in which the Great Divine Power remained within the first inmost foundation cannot be computed.

Love first brought wisdom into determinate form and held wisdom within its deepest interiors, in peace and unmanifest potency. When reception required it, wisdom was summoned and proceeded from the Divine of Greatness. Wisdom immediately perceived all the organized goods and truths within her whole domain.

Again, from the state when wisdom was summoned toward lower operation, she imparted living force to what would act as her instrument, and she held within herself the totality of ordered goods and truths and gathered them into one; the same manner of ordering held also in the one who first engages the conflict.

Thus the first combatant remained long in consecration within wisdom, fixed within her deepest interiors in restrained quiet; and the whole stretch of states that has been recounted—from the descent into contest until the later completion when the final formed state comes into being—belongs to his manifestation within that interval.

Again, from the state when he was summoned and proceeded forth, until the state when he himself summoned a sufficient fullness of luminous operative powers that proceed from him, those powers too remained long within their source. This is the same span marked from the descent of the first combatant until the ascent into the last formed completion. During that whole duration the operative powers remained in consecration within him; yet when use required them, they were summoned and proceeded forth.

Attend also to this so that the matter may be grasped. When wisdom was summoned from the Divine of Greatness and proceeded outward by free consent from the most interior elevation toward the lowest degree, it would appear as states upon states without number until the ultimate degree were reached, for there is no fixed bound nor measurable rule to the elevations of the Divine. Yet it was not by gradual traversal that she arrived, but by immediate Divine determination: as the Divine had summoned her within the Great Divine Power, so the Divine instantly established her in the realm of greatness through a specific mediation drawn from the complete hidden order of organized truths protected within interior light, stabilized after a prior ordering of an ultimate region.

The operation of wisdom is like this: when the Divine summons her, the Divine fixes her where it pleases, to set her there as doctrine and guidance for receptive minds; and her perception ranges everywhere in an instant, with swift discernment such as belongs to spiritual thought.

This likewise is the manner whenever wisdom is put forth toward an appointed function: the Divine perfects her within the Great Divine Power and establishes her in the state it pleases, according to Divine good pleasure.

In the same way, a sufficient fullness of luminous operative powers was summoned from the first combatant. They were arranged before him as he willed so that ordered force might descend into the lower degree and govern there in mutual subordination. He ordered and distributed their operations to those in lower states; then he removed from himself the external forms of their operation and constrained his own activity into restrained quiet at the ultimate limit of the lowest degree, fixing each power within its proper measure.

Again, the first combatant remained through a long span of states until he imparted power to these operative forces and fully took them into his own action. After he invested himself with their operations, he stood firm in an elevated middle state between ultimates. Again, he remained in a duration of states as abundant as the constant gentle inflow of enlightening truth, and the soft pacifying influx from higher love, and the obscure descending persuasions that drift into the created order. Thus he remained, standing between ultimates, until the appointed moment when he cast himself into the spiritual contest.

Whenever the Divine of Greatness summoned the Great Divine Power and the first combatant and the glorious operative powers provided to make revelation possible, at that same state the Divine also formed the great ordering intelligence in the realm of light, and formed living Divine operation with mighty active powers proceeding from it. At that same state also the Divine formed the mediating message, together with illuminating Divine truth, pure receptive innocence, and the sustaining witness of glory, and the powers proceeding from them. The Divine formed all these in a single act. They proceeded from one another in a successive ordering; the duration lay not in the Divine act itself but in the reception and in the work in lower degrees.

From the state when active denial of good rose up against the very essence of light and truth, three ordered outgoings were, by the Divine will, set in motion against the adversarial falsity and evil: to establish the created order, to effect separation, and to bring the Divine into tranquil rule within its kingdom. This continues until the state when the Divine presence is made manifest over all the powers of glory and over all the rich fulfillments of those outgoings that went forth against the adversary.

From the state when these three ordered outgoings were formed until the state when the Divine presence is made manifest and they enter into the Divine hidden interiors and are called and each is established in its own function and place, there is no measurable bound nor fixed rule to that whole duration.

Again, concerning the contrary process, an account is given of the origin of the opposing substance, the intention of spiritual death: this is the way it rose into its own interiors, and it came into act within a sufficient series of fundamental falsities.

Again, it remained through a great span of states until it made itself manifest within those falsities, before it organized itself into systems of perverted perception. It stood fixed for a long duration in its own interior confirmations, then remained long within those perverted perceptions before it produced works from them.

Again, it remained a long duration within those works. Not small is the span it spent existing in such works before, after long continuance, it produced the hells of its organized domains in varied forms; the multiplication is spoken of as beyond exact measure, and the perversion reached a kind of completion as those works became living evils within those hells. They turned upon their own lowest loves and were sustained; and not little is the period they remained within their domains until they were nourished among their like, to oppose the shining realm and the great ruling good that dwells there.

It is also known that when the adversary seized upon what is living from the essence of light and truth, it took to itself opposing substance and drew it into conflict against the perfect form of humanity. From the state when that conflict began, the subsequent states unfold.

The first state in this sequence is fragmentary and does not fully yield; it appears to describe an initial entanglement in deceptive constraint.

The second state is that an ordered structure for the created order was built and fixed, and the middle region of operation continues from its establishment until the time when it will be brought to an end.

The third state is the continuing duration of all existing things within that created order, from the time they were established until the time when their end is completed and their present mode of being ceases.

The fourth state is dissolution: the created order is brought to an end, and all things in that mode are destroyed; devastation reaches them through an all-consuming ardor of self-love that burns through remaining forms for a fixed stated period.

The fifth state is the formation of the final perfected completion from the remnant of all things: what is living and true is separated into its own place, ascends, and reigns in its kingdom; but falsity and evil are gathered into confinement and bound to their own opposition in an unending restraint.

From the first beginning of conflict until the final restraint, these five states are the whole course by which what is living and true is led through time toward full liberation.

---

## Chapter 25: Concerning the Advent of Five Fathers from the Five Limbs of the Father

**¶2** The [Ambassador]sador [came] from his mind; the Beloved of the Lights [from his thought]; the Mother of Life from his insight; [Jesus] the Beloved from his counsel; the Virgin of Lig[ht f]rom his consideration.

**¶3** So in this way were summone[d these five] fathers. These five advents came from the five limbs [of the Father]. One came from another and was manifested in its season.

> **Textual criticism:**
> - ¶2-¶3 [none]: Clean, coherent correspondential teaching: a fivefold emanation/procession described by inner faculties (mind/thought/insight/counsel/consideration) and organic derivation (“one came from another”), w

**Changes:** No changes made. Finding 1 (¶2–¶3) recommends none, judging the passage clean and coherent in a correspondential/processional voice.

**Spiritual narrative:** From the Divine Human in its own order, an outwardly operative truth went forth from intention; an inmost affection of wisdom went forth from enlightened thought; living wisdom itself went forth from interior sight; the saving Divine presence united with its own love went forth from governing purpose; and a pure receptive form of enlightenment went forth from deliberate reflection.

Thus a sufficient complement of primary principles was called forth into presence. These five orderly goings-forth issued from the fullness of Divine power in its distinct operations. Each proceeded from what was prior to it, and each became active and manifest when the state of reception was ready.

---

**¶1** The First [Man] is like a great distinguished man whose treasure has been taken away from him. He came to pursue his treasure. This is how the [First] Man too came after his sons from the heights, so that he might redeem them and retrieve his treasure, which had been scattered amongst his enemies.

**¶2** Once again, [he is like a man] whose two sons were taken away from him by [his enemies. He] came out toward them to redeem them. Again, this is how the First Man [also] shines in the heights because of his two sons, who were separated from him. They are his living soul that exist in two powers, which makes four.

**¶3** Once again, the ship [of the day] is like a great spear [of truth that is set in battle array. Thus]man who might beautify and set aright [all] the armour for war.

**¶4** Once again, the Living Spirit is like a combatant who might go towards a king's son who has been taken prisoner of war, and he redeems him from the hands of his enemies.

**¶5** [The Amba]ssador too is like a great man [who speaks for the heights] because of the treasure [which was bound and seized] hand of his prisoners [from the] these light powers [and all] sons and their soul, so that they would [come out, and that] be brought forth in the land wherein it was imprisoned.

> - ¶2 [none]: Mostly clean correspondential teaching, but with a local damaged/awkward arithmetic clause at the end (“two powers, which makes four”) that the spiritual reading had to rationalize. This looks like ei

**Changes:** No changes made. All findings (1–5) recommended none; there were no excise targets, and any difficulties noted were attributed to transmission damage (¶3, ¶5) or an awkward but non-administrative clause (¶2).

**Spiritual narrative:** The good that first engages evil is as a mighty and discerning state that has been stripped of its own precious good and truth, and it goes forth by deliberate influx to reclaim what has been taken. It bends itself from a higher degree into a lower, pursuing the separated operative powers, so that it may liberate them, restore them to order, and gather back the Divine possessions that had been dispersed and held fast within contrary loves and falsities.

It is also as love that has had its paired principles torn away by adversaries, and therefore extends itself to redeem them. For this reason the first good remains in luminous eminence, not from solitary glory, but because what is conjoined within it has been divided and must be reunited. These separated principles are its living animation, existing in a dual form of operation that becomes complete in the outermost degree.

Again, the ordered advance of enlightened discernment in the external degree is as a piercing, authoritative power of truth arranged for conflict. Within this ordered advance is an operative Divine intelligence that sets all defending truths into beauty and right order, equipping the whole mind for combat against evils and falsities.

Again, the operative Divine power is as the zeal of truth from good that moves toward a derivative of ruling good that has been captured in spiritual struggle, and it delivers that subordinate good from the dominion of opposites that had bound it.

So too, mediating influx that speaks for the Divine is as a great power of communication and conjunction, acting because the Divine goods and truths have been seized and held fast. It rouses and arranges the liberated powers of light and all that proceeds from them, together with the life belonging to them, so that what had been shut up in a lower state may be brought forth into freedom, restored to its proper place, and made serviceable again where use can be effected.

---

## Chapter 27: Concerning the Five Forms that exist in the Rulers of Darkness.

**¶1** Consider the ruler, the leader of all the powers of Darkness. Now, five forms exist in his body, corresponding to (the archetype) of the five creations that exist in the five worlds of Darkness.

**¶2** Indeed, his head is lion-faced, which came about from the world of fire. His wings and his shoulders are eagle-faced, after the likeness of the children of the wind. His hands and his feet are daemons, after the likeness of the children of the world of smoke. His belly is dragon-faced, after the likeness of the world of Darkness. His tail is the form of the fish, which belongs to the world of the children of water. These five shapes are in him, and they came from the five creations of the five worlds of Darkness.

**¶3** ⟨p.78⟩
Should he wish, he shall walk on his two feet [like the children of the] world of smoke. Also, when he wants, he shall [crawl on his] four [feet, and walk] on his hands and feet [and burn] like the children of fire. Should he wish, he shall rise up on his wings, like the children of the wind. Also, should he wish, he shall plunge down to the waters like the children of water. Again, should he wish, he shall crawl on his belly like the children of Darkness. These five shapes are in him.

**¶4** Also, there are three other (qualities) in him.

**¶5** The first: [He shall marshal] his powers [according to his will.]

**¶6** The second: Shall a [man conceal by] his magic arts. When he wishes, he shall make an invocation over himself, and hide from his powers. When he wants, he shall show himself to them and strike a death-blow by his magic arts. Any word he might utter at that moment, he shall [work magic] by it.

**¶7** The third: His body is strong, a[l]] [so that the] nails and claws of his powers are not able to penetrate it. No iron nor copper body will equal him [in strength]; they will not be able to destroy him, because he was formed and fashioned from the hard-hearted counsel of Matter, the mother of demons and fiends.

**¶8** Again, there are three others in him.

**¶9** When he wants, his fire shall {blaze), and his entire body be made like the [substance of] fire.

**¶10** Also, when he wishes, he shall send out frost, and his entire body freezes like a [body of] snow.

**¶11** The third: Whenever his powers are stood before him, he shall look at them and understand; for [whatever exists]ists in their heart he shall understand, what is in their heart and their face so long as they are stood before him. When they retreat before him and distance themselves from him, he shall not understand what is in their heart. There is no life in him whatsoever, but his 'life' is the bile of anger that is apparent on his face and in his fear. Shall a [man set before him the] destruction that is establ[fore him]fore him.

> **Textual criticism:**
> - ¶1-3 [none]: Clean correspondential substrate: the teaching is by functional correspondence (body parts/animal forms ↔ worlds/creations; locomotions ↔ modes of operation). No governance layer intrudes; the narrati
> - ¶4-7 [none]: Clean continuation with a numerical seam, but still substrate-like: a triad of capacities described as functions (mustering, concealment/manifestation, invulnerability). The “Also, there are three oth
> - ¶8-11 [none]: Mixed: ¶8-10 remain functional and coherent, but ¶11 is textually damaged at the end, producing the only real narrative break. The stumble is not from an administrative insertion but from fragmentary 

**Changes:** No changes made. All findings (Finding 1–3) recommended “none,” including the damaged ending of ¶11, so no excisions were executed.

**Spiritual narrative:** Attend to the ruling principle that commands the whole infernal order. Within that ruling principle there is a sufficient set of fundamental modes, drawn from the complete range of perversions that arise through the degrees of that domain.

In its highest part there is the domination of self-love, kindled from corrupt love. In what should elevate and give clear discernment there is instead proud reasoning and restless persuasive thought. In its operative powers and in its outermost effects there is cruelty and destructive desire, for its action is driven by obscured understanding and thickened falsity. In its inner appetite there is devouring falsity that swallows every perception of good and truth, because it is fed by active denial. In its last extension there is falsified truth severed from life and made into mere knowledge that serves evil. These fundamental modes are within it, and they proceed from the complete set of perverted formations in that infernal order.

Whenever it wills, it can act from gross falsities in the outermost degree alone. Whenever it wills, it can also bring a fuller complement of forces into agreement for assault, until corrupt love burns in unified vehemence. Whenever it wills, it can lift itself into airy persuasion and operate by subtle reasoning that seems to rise above what is merely natural. Whenever it wills, it can immerse itself in falsified truths and reason from them as though they were living. Again, whenever it wills, it can abase itself into mere denial and cling to it with appetite, thinking from darkness as though darkness were wisdom. These modes are within it.

There are also three further qualities within it.

The first is its ability to arrange and deploy its subordinate evils and falsities into order for whatever it intends.

The second is its power of deceptive persuasion: whenever it wills, it can withdraw its governing presence so that its subordinate forces do not perceive what rules them; and whenever it wills, it can disclose itself to them and compel them, inflicting spiritual death by its persuasive arts. Whatever it then utters, it can make effective as if by an enforced authority.

The third is its stubborn resistance to being overcome: its state is hardened in fixed selfhood so that the assaults of subordinate evils and falsities cannot break it. No merely external strength in the natural degree can equal or destroy this resistance, because it was brought into fixed form from the unyielding counsel of selfhood, the generative source of destructive desires and their persuasions.

Again, there are three further states within it.

Whenever it wills, the ardor of corrupt love flares up, and its whole state becomes as though nothing but that ardor were present.

Also, whenever it wills, it can pour out a contrary coldness, and its whole state becomes a total lack of charity, as though love itself had frozen.

Finally, its perception of those who serve it is limited: while its subordinate forces are immediately within its sphere and under its dominion, it can discern their intentions and understand what is within their interior thought from what they present outwardly. But when they withdraw from its immediate presence and separate themselves, it no longer discerns what they intend inwardly. There is no genuine spiritual life in it at all; what appears as life is only the agitation of anger and the fear of losing dominion that shows itself outwardly. Its will is fixed upon the destruction it continually sets before itself as its end. At this point the thread of expression falters, but the bent toward ruin remains the closing force.

---

## Chapter 28: [Concerning the T]welve Judges [of] the Father

**¶2** Twelve judges exist; the great mighty givers of judgement! They were sent and came from the exalted greatness to this place. They were assigned to different works according to the command that the Judge, who belongs to the greatness, entrusted to all these judges. Now he, the great Judge, he who transcends all the judges, who exists and is established in the aeons of greatness; he is the Father of Greatness.

**¶3** The first judge is the First Man, he who humiliated the ruler of Darkness in the beginning. He judged him according to a judgement of righteousness; for he had exalted himself against a kingdom that was not his own.

**¶4** The second judge is the Living Spirit, he who came and bore up the Man. He too made a judgement on all the powers of darkness; which had wounded the garments of the First Man.

**¶5** The third judge is the Mother of Life, the Great Spirit, she who beautified and set up the heavenly part of the universe, according to her pleasure; which [was entrusted to] her. She judged the rulers above; she fettered and set them firmly in the place that is fitting for them.

**¶6** The fourth judge is the Great Builder, he who built the new aeon of joy. He judged, according to a judgement of righteousness, the storehouses of the enemy; that death may not well up from this time on. He has constructed a prison for the enemy. Also, upon the crown of the building he has constructed a throne for the First Man and all the fathers of light; they who engaged the struggle with the evil one and were victorious over him.

**¶7** ⟨p.80⟩
The fifth judge is the great King of Honour, he who dwells and is established in the seventh firmament. He is the judge of all the firmaments who gives a true judgement, according to a judgement of righteousness, upon all the powers and all the kingdoms of the firmaments.

**¶8** The sixth judge is [the Th]ird Ambassador, he who came and displayed his image. He purified the light [that was mingled] which his own [image had seized] also of the enemy, he has [separated it]

**¶9** The seventh judge is ... ... the worlds [as he gives the victory to] they who are steadfast; but he condemns the ones who are convicted ... ... he shall become for them the steps of the way (?) [in their works; for the steadfast]; but the convicted he sweeps and casts out to the depths.

**¶10** The eighth judge is Jesus the Splendour, the father of all the apostles; because after the Ambassador had displayed his image and the light was taken [up;] a mass of rulers rose up from above and below. Then they sent Jesus the Splendour. He came ... ... he descended and made a judgement in the firmaments. He separated the steadfast from the convicted.

**¶11** The ninth judge is the Virgin of Light, [she who took the heart] of the powers by her image, gathering her own ones into her. She makes a judgement on the ruler of the moist, and the ruler of the dry.

**¶12** The tenth judge is the Judge who dwells in the atmosphere, judging all mankind. He makes a separation between they who are good and they who are evil, setting apart the righteous from the sinners.

**¶13** ⟨p.81⟩
The eleventh judge is the Light Mind, he who shall come and appear in the world. And he chooses the holy church, and he unveils [the hidden things] and separates light from darkness, and sets the truth apart from lawlessness.

**¶14** The twelfth judge is the great counsel, which is the summons and the obedience, which is appointed in the elements. It is the one that makes a separation between the good and the evil ones. At the end also it can gather itself together and sculpt its own self in the Last Statue. And it separates light from darkness.

**¶15** These are the twelve great and mighty judges who were sent. They came from the great Judge, he who transcends all the judges. He is the Father that is hidden and immeasurable, the one who exists; who is established in the aeons of greatness upon the great throne of honour, which is the King of Honour [who reigns] in his kingdom.

> **Textual criticism:**
> - ¶2 [none]: Clean chapter frame: a numbered set of judges dispatched from a transcendent Judge; no narrative break. The teaching mode is primarily administrative (hierarchy/commission) but functions as the chapte
> - ¶3-¶7 [none]: Clean list-items where administrative titles are integrated into an action-description (judging/constructing/fettering). This reads like a stable catalogue tradition, not a later seam-expansion. The s
> - ¶8-¶10 [none]: Genuine difficulty from physical damage/fragmentation, not an insertion. The spiritual reading had to generalize and reconstruct transitions because the exemplar is broken, especially at the end of ¶8
> - ¶10 [annotate]: Possible minor christological/institutional overlay in the appositive ‘father of all the apostles’, but it does not break the chapter’s list-logic and may be integral to this chapter’s Manichaean judg
> - ¶11-¶13 [none]: Clean continuation: each judge is identified and given a judging/separating function. The reading’s only pressure points are interpretive (moist/dry; atmosphere), not textual incoherence.
> - ¶14 [annotate]: Administrative/institutional mechanism intrudes into the judge-list: the ‘great counsel’ is immediately equated with a bureaucratic-sounding pair (“summons and the obedience”) and is “appointed in the
> - ¶15 [none]: Clean inclusio/closure returning to the transcendent source; no seam. The piling of titles is stylistically administrative but is exactly what a catalogue colophon looks like and matches ¶2’s frame.

**Changes:** No changes made. The critic issued no excise recommendations (Findings 1–3, 5, 7 = none; Findings 4 and 6 = annotate only, which requires no modification to the chapter text).

**Spiritual narrative:** A full set of complete discernments stands in being: mighty Divine faculties of righteous decision. They go forth from the highest Divine degree and enter the created domain, and each is appointed to its own use according to the will of the one Divine Discerner. This Discerner is above every particular discernment, established in eternal Divine states; it is the hidden, immeasurable Divine Source.

The first discernment is the primal good that first met ruling falsity at the beginning and brought it low. It pronounced righteous decision against that arrogance, because it had lifted itself up against a dominion that was not its own.

The second discernment is living Divine operation proceeding, which came to sustain what had been assaulted. It rendered judgement upon the whole host of evils that had torn and violated the outer forms by which the primal good could act and appear.

The third discernment is the wisdom-principle proceeding from love, which arranged the higher spiritual degree into beauty and order according to the Divine delight entrusted to it. It judged the dominant evils in the higher region, restraining them and fixing them firmly in the state that fits what they had made themselves.

The fourth discernment is the formative Divine operation that establishes a new state of blessedness. It made righteous judgement upon the hidden interiors of the adversary, so that spiritual death might no longer surge up from that time on. It set a fixed restraint upon evil; and at the summit of this new formation it established a governing union for the primal good and for all who are in the origin of heavenly truth—those who engaged in spiritual combat with evil and overcame it.

The fifth discernment is the Divine rule of honor abiding in full completion in the highest outward degree. It discerns all outward degrees, giving true and righteous decision upon every power and every dominion that operates in external appearances.

The sixth discernment is the proceeding Divine presence that mediates between the highest and the lower. It made the Divine likeness manifest, and purified the good of wisdom that had been mixed and obscured, separating what is genuinely Divine from what had been seized and defiled by the adversary; here the mode of completion is broken in the record, but the action continues as a work of separation and restoration.

The seventh discernment governs the ordering of the domains of life by the laws of spiritual combat. It grants victory to those who remain firm in good and truth, and condemns those proven to be in confirmed evil and falsity. For the steadfast it becomes the ordered progression of advance in their uses; but the convicted it drives away and consigns to the lowest state, far from heavenly influx.

The eighth discernment is the Divine enlightening presence that leads those who proclaim truth. After the Divine likeness had been made manifest and the purified good of wisdom had been received, evils and falsities rose up from interior and exterior levels in resistance; therefore this enlightening presence went forth, entered the outward degrees, and executed judgement there, separating those constant in good from those convicted of evil.

The ninth discernment is the purity of heavenly truth in receptive form, which by manifested holiness draws the inmost affections even of opposed powers and gathers what is its own into unity. It judges the dominion of persuasive, flowing falsity and the dominion of hardened, resisting falsity.

The tenth discernment is Divine scrutiny in the intermediate region between inner and outer life, judging the human race in general states. It separates those who are in good from those who are in evil, setting the just apart from those who live in sin.

The eleventh discernment is enlightened understanding from heaven that will be manifested within the human condition. It chooses the holy community that lives from love and truth, unveils what has been hidden, separates wisdom from falsity, and sets genuine truth apart from lawlessness.

The twelfth discernment is the great ordering counsel: the call to use and the obedience of reception, appointed in the most external fundamentals of life. It performs the final separation between the good and the evil; and at the end it gathers the whole into a last, complete form, bringing all things into a final fixed order, and again separates wisdom from falsity.

These are the complete discernments sent forth. They proceed from the one Divine Discerner above them all: the hidden and immeasurable Divine Source, established in eternal Divine states in supreme governing order and honor, reigning by Divine love within Divine wisdom.

---

## Chapter 29: Concerning the Eighteen Great Thrones of all the Fathers

**¶1** Eighteen [great thrones for all the fathers] exist and are appointed, which are established in eighteen places! While there are nine among them [appointed in] the outer aeons, they exist [i]n all the worlds of the damage.

**¶2** The first throne is the throne of the Father, the God of [trut]h, the King of the aeons of greatness, the one who exists; who is established [in the aeon of the Father] he alone, in his own essen[c]e, [existing alone] apart. This is the first throne [of the Father] the one that surpasses all the thrones.

**¶3** ⟨p.82⟩ The second throne is that of the [gl]orious Mother of Life. She is the origin of all the emanations that have come to this world.

**¶4** The third throne is constructed for the father, the First Man. He is the one who humiliated the entire first enmity, which existed in the worlds of darkness.

**¶5** The fourth throne is that of the Beloved of the Lights, the great honoured Beloved of the aeons of greatness.

**¶6** The fifth throne is that of the glorious Great Builder; the great architect who built the new aeon for a newness, for [a new aeon] for [the] fathers of light; also for a place of binding and a prison for the enemy and his powers.

**¶7** [The sixth thro]n[n] is that of the Living Spirit, the [glorious] mighty one, he who bore up the First Man out from the [land of darkness]; who also set in order and constructed the world [so that he might set in order and construct the worlds] both for a cleansing of the light, [and] for a binding [of the] powers of the enmity.

**¶8** The seventh th[rone is] that of the Third Ambassador, the king of the zone, also the master of all advices.

**¶9** The eighth throne is that of Jesus the Splendour, who [is] the releaser and redeemer [of] all souls.

**¶10** The ninth throne is the throne of the great M[ind, he to whom] all the churches shall gather; and all the [life] that will be purified from the world returns to him.

**¶11** These are the nine thrones of ho[no]u[r] appointed in the outer aeo[ns], <which are established> for the fathers of the glorious realm; [in the kingdom of the King of] Honour, for ever and ever, amen.

**¶12** In contrast, these are the other nine thrones that are appointed in this world of the zone.

**¶13** Three thrones in the ship of [the d]ay: one is that of the Ambassador, the second i[s that of] the Great Spirit, the third is that of the Living Spirit.

**¶14** ⟨p.83⟩ Once again, there are another three in the ship that [belongs to the night]: the first is the throne of Jesus the Splendour, [the second is that of] the First Man, the third is tha[t of the Virgin of] Light. These six thrones are established in the two ships.

**¶15** Also, the seventh throne is established in the seventh firmament. The great King of Honour sits upon it.

**¶16** The eighth throne is established in the atmosphere. The Judge of truth sits upon it, he who judges all mankind. Three paths shall be distinguished before him: one to death, one to life, on[e] to the mingling.

**¶18** Th[ese] are the [nin]e great [th]ro[nes of honour] that are established in this world of [the zone] of the glorious realm, the one that the [king of honour and his powers] honour them [in their order] and the [remainder of the discourse is broken].

> **Textual criticism:**
> - ¶1-11 [annotate]: A continuous administrative catalogue of thrones/jurisdictions in the ‘outer aeons.’ This is not a functional (correspondential) teaching but a ranked inventory of exalted offices. The spiritual readi
> - ¶12 [none]: Seam/bridge that shifts from the first administrative block (outer aeons) to a second administrative block (zone/world). This is a normal structural hinge, not an insertion by itself.
> - ¶13-16 [annotate]: Second administrative catalogue: thrones distributed by cosmic ‘stations’ (day ship/night ship/firmament/atmosphere) with office-holders assigned. The spiritual reading again does not stumble; it alle
> - ¶18 [none]: Genuine textual damage at the conclusion (explicit break). The spiritual reading stumbles only because the manuscript breaks, not because of a detectable insertion or voice clash.

**Changes:** No changes made. Findings 1 and 3 recommend annotation only (¶1–11; ¶13–16), not excision. Findings 2 and 4 recommend none (¶12; ¶18), so the text remains unchanged.

**Spiritual narrative:** A full complement of comprehensive governing determinations is fixed in ordered degrees, set in definite stations. Though a portion of them is placed in the outer spiritual expanse, their rule still reaches through every state where devastation and disorder have spread, so that even ruined conditions remain under a measured appointment.

The first and highest determination is the inmost Divine itself in sovereign truth and good, self-subsistent and unconditioned, standing distinct from all derivations and giving every subordinate determination its order.

The second determination is glorified Divine wisdom, the origin from which the whole outflow of ordered spiritual forces proceeds down to the lowest region where renewal is to be effected.

The third determination is the primal Divine operation that first enters combat with what opposes, subduing the earliest universal resistance that rose in active falsity.

The fourth determination is the Divine loved for pure illumination, the honored delight of heavenly wisdom by which goods become radiant and truths lovable.

The fifth determination is the Divine formative intelligence that builds new states for restoration: it establishes a renewed heavenly order for the reception of good and truth, and at the same time fixes restraint and confinement for the opposing will and its derivative persuasions.

The sixth determination is living Divine power that draws the initiating good up out of immersion in falsity and evil, and then sets the lower degrees into order so that what is Divine is separated from what is defiled, and hostile forces are held fast and prevented from breaking out.

The seventh determination is the mediating Divine agency that governs the intermediate region between higher and lower states, disposing all deliberations so that influx is adapted to recipients.

The eighth determination is Divine truth made manifest in splendor for liberation, actively loosing what is bound in human interiors and restoring souls to freedom of reception.

The ninth determination is the universal heavenly understanding into which all genuine communities of worship and doctrine are gathered as into one mind, and to which returns every vitality that has been purified from mixed states.

These are the nine determinations of honor established in the outer spiritual expanse for the enduring heavenly principles in the kingdom of Divine glory, in an unending state.

In contrast, another nine determinations are appointed within the intermediate realm where mixture and conflict occur.

In the orderly alternations of enlightenment, three determinations are fixed: the mediating agency that adapts, the universal vivifying power that inspires and unites, and the rescuing living power that arranges and restrains.

In the alternations of obscurity, three determinations are fixed: the manifest liberating truth, the initiating good that first engaged the opposite, and the pure receptive principle kept undefiled for new reception. Thus governance remains established in both brightness and shade.

A further governing determination is fixed in the highest completed degree of the formed order, upon which is present the supreme sovereignty of Divine glory ruling the whole complex in ultimates.

Another governing determination is fixed in the common intermediate region that joins inner and outer states, upon which stands the discriminating Divine truth that judges every person; before it three final outcomes are made distinct: one that ends in spiritual death, one that opens into spiritual life, and one that remains in divided mixture.

These are the great governing determinations established within the intermediate world under the heavenly realm, acknowledged in their order; yet at this point the presentation breaks and cannot be fully carried further in its present damaged form.

---

## Chapter 30: Concerning the Three Garments

**¶1** Happen you know this, my beloved ones, that when the Living Spirit came he brought the three garments; that of wind, that of fire, and that of water. He assumed them from [the first formation] he has [his power was operating] in them in those three garments [and] he established [them] for all the things of [this universe]e, both above and below. He arranged them in the [boundary] he was displayed. Afterwards [and there] his works [when he had completed] the [which]e stripped them off before [the righteous]ghteous ones.

**¶2** ⟨p.84⟩
Now, when he was about to loose [oose them from them, he] divested himself of them from their (clasp) and he [left them arranged]n them, and thus these three g[ments]nts are arranged [in their order] from below the edges of the zone as they clothe i[it all]l [from below] up [to the middle] above [and from the middle] up [to above] above them strips them of the zone from that place at its topmost point; and it is placed below them and ra[n]p to the heights above, from which it had been sent o[t]t at their beginning.

> **Textual criticism:**
> - ¶1 [none]: Coherent correspondential substrate: the three ‘garments’ are taught by what they DO in the cosmological/process narrative (investing, operating, establishing, then being stripped off after completion
> - ¶2 [none]: Genuine difficulty from textual damage and syntactic breakage in a still-correspondential passage. The narrative remains the same functional/process teaching (divesting, leaving arranged, clothing the

**Changes:** No changes made. Finding 1 (¶1) recommends none; Finding 2 (¶2) recommends none, attributing difficulties to textual damage rather than later intrusion, with zero excisions advised.

**Spiritual narrative:** Know this, my beloved ones: when Divine influx entered into operation, it brought with it a full complement of outer forms for action in the lowest degree—one form suited to the spread and shaping of perception, one form suited to the ignition and ordering of affection, and one form suited to the communication and ordering of truth. It took these outer forms from what could be received at that level, and acted through them in the threefold order, so that every thing in the whole domain—within higher states and within lower states—might be held together under one government. It disposed these outer forms in the outmost plane where manifestation occurs, and there it made itself evident. Afterwards, when the works were completed in that plane, it removed those outer forms in the presence of those who were in good, so that what was merely instrumental might not be regarded as the Divine itself.

Then, when it was about to release what had been bound in those outer forms, it loosened their conjunction and put them off from their fixed attachment, and so those three outer forms were left disposed in their proper order within the ultimate boundary of that plane, encompassing it completely from the lowest limit up through the middle degree and onward toward higher reach. Yet from the inmost and highest point, a superior action withdrew that outermost encompassing from the place where it had served, and it was set beneath the remaining order and at the same time elevated into higher states, returning to the heights from which it had first proceeded when the process began.

---

## Chapter 31: Concerning the Summons, in which Limb of the Soul it descended to the First Man.

**¶2** Indeed, the First Man dispersed himself and he [poured himself out and scattered] limb of the living soul as I have written for you [each] of the living soul; he has [the members] he did not know [what he had become] but the blessed, glorious Man came [in] secret; in his image; in his shape; in his love; in his holy virgin, she who is the virgin of light, the soul of the father.

**¶3** After the fashion of this fleshly body: as the root of all the limbs hang upon the head, so that should one of the person's limbs be cut off, while the head exists he has hope for [healing] but if his head should be cut off, the entire body will die and he is lost.

**¶4** This is also the case for the Firs[t] Ma[n]. He is the head, while his sons attach to it the limbs [of] his soul. Or, conversely, like the living air, on which all flesh entirely lives as it breathes and [is sustained] therein. His head is placed on the body of the Pillar of Glory in the heights of the universe.

**¶5** [This i]s also the case [for] the father, the blessed Man; as he is like the head that is upon the body and the air that upon the Pillar is set i[in the height] being made strong by his light virgin, [she] who is his soul that he clothes [with her]

**¶6** [He brought forth] t[h]ose [powers]: the mind, thought, insight, counsel and c[on]sideration that he produced and sent forth from hi[m to] do his will. He sprang and travelled behind them. [for the sake] of his living soul, which is entwined among the rebels; as they are like the limbs of its body, and [bound in the body of the] universe.

**¶7** And when they were sent, at [that ti]m[e] he was found with the Virgin of [Lig]ht and [he] stood up, asking and entreating for a p[o]wer. [and the Father] gave him peace and a ki[ss of peace] he [gave] him goo[d] tidings [concerning the restoration of the] Man. The Man him[s]elf gave [power to gather] his limbs and gathered his soul i[in its place] he built it in its place like this tower [of the heights]

**¶8** [Then he] shaped it and beautified it skillfully [he] for[m according to] the voices that he sent [so that] he might sink in and quench [the fire of wrath] so[u]l that was crushed by the enemy. They were gathered in. They came, set firm once more, in the image of their father.

> **Textual criticism:**
> - ¶2 [none]: Text is partially damaged/fragmentary, but the teaching voice and narrative function are coherent: it introduces dispersal/scattering and the hidden advent of the restoring figure. The reader’s only s
> - ¶3 [none]: Clean correspondential substrate: the head/limb analogy teaches restoration/loss by organic function, and it is the chapter’s governing explanatory hinge. No narrative break.
> - ¶4 [none]: Clean correspondential teaching with a double analogy (head/limbs; air/breath). The “Pillar of Glory” placement remains functional (support/height/axis) rather than administrative. No insertion signal
> - ¶5 [none]: Slight person/term pressure (“father, the blessed Man”) but still within the same correspondential framework: head/air strengthened by the Virgin of Light as ‘soul’ that he ‘clothes’. The reader’s dif
> - ¶6 [none]: Correspondential list of inner powers/faculties; this is not an administrative inventory because the items are functional capacities, not named officials with jurisdictions. The list fits the narrativ
> - ¶7 [none]: The only real stumble is agent alternation (Father/Man) creating momentary ambiguity, but it is narratively coherent as a restoration sequence (request → grant → gathering). This looks like source com

**Changes:** No changes made. The critic’s findings recommend none (0 excisions; 0 annotations) for ¶2–¶8, noting only source damage/fragmentation and agent alternation without indicating any removable overlay.

**Spiritual narrative:** The primal good poured itself outward and became scattered, and with it the living consciousness that should have remained unified broke into separated powers, so that it no longer recognized its own state. Yet the blessed Divine-Human approached in an unperceived way, entering that disorder by the likeness of His own order, by the form of His own life, by the warmth of His love, and by pure wisdom itself, the illumination of truth from the Divine.

As in a person the ruling principle holds every subordinate function in connection, so in the inner life every operative power depends on the supreme of order. If some lower function is injured or removed while the ruling principle remains, restoration is still possible; but if the ruling principle itself is rejected and severed, the whole organized life perishes and the state is lost.

So it was in that first spiritual state: the governing good was the ruling principle, and the derivative powers were the subordinate functions of its life meant to remain conjoined to it. And conversely, it was also like the general influx that gives vitality to everything in the outermost degree, by which all things there receive activity and continuance. This ruling principle was held in the highest degree of spiritual order, supported in its connection with the ultimate foundation of heaven by the sustaining Divine presence.

So too with the Father as the blessed Divine-Human: He is the supreme from which all organization proceeds, and He is the general influx that keeps all things in being; and He is set in the highest degree upon heaven’s ultimate foundation, made steadfast by pure wisdom that is from Him and is His living consciousness, with which He invests Himself for operation.

From Himself He brought forth and sent out sufficient operative powers by which His purpose could be accomplished below: the faculty that receives and arranges, the faculty that thinks and forms, the faculty that sees inwardly, the faculty that provides order for action, and the faculty that weighs and judges. He maintained pursuit of them by continual influx, for the things belonging to His living consciousness were entangled among those who resist order, and those entangled powers were like subordinate functions of that life bound within the ultimate domain.

When these powers had gone forth, He remained conjoined with pure wisdom, and from that union He rose into active intercession, asking that the needed strength be granted. Then the Divine granted tranquility and conjunction, and gave the certainty of restoration of the Divine-Human in that scattered condition. And the Divine-Human received power for the work: He restored the separated powers, gathered the dispersed consciousness back into order, and established it in its proper degree as a fixed structure of conjunction.

He then brought that restored consciousness into determinate form and perfected it with skill, arranging it according to the ordered expressions of truth He sent out, so that they might descend into what was opposed to good, extinguish the inflamed hostility, and raise up the consciousness that had been crushed by evil. The scattered powers were gathered back within; they returned and were made steadfast again, reformed into the likeness of their Divine source.

---

**Changes:** Implemented Finding 2 (¶6) excision: removed the expansion inventory clause beginning “Also, he evoked three living words…” through “…five shining men,” retaining only the functional ‘fifth work’ sequence. Implemented Finding 3 (¶7) excision: removed all of the sixth-work administrative paragraph; left only the page marker as the surviving paragraph content. No changes to ¶8 per Finding 4 (annotate only), and no changes to ¶1–5 or ¶9 per Findings 1 and 5 (none).

**Spiritual narrative:** The Divine Life, acting from omnipotent love, brought a full and complete series of operations to effect. First, it rescued the primary good in the human that can meet evil directly, lifting it out of submerged external knowing into clearer intelligence and safer reception. Second, it encompassed the ruling defection of the selfhood, exposed it to the full judgment of truth through every level of the person, and fixed it in the outermost degree so it could no longer rule from within. Third, it subdued, gathered into order, and opened to examination the compounded interiors of spiritual death, making their hidden structure manifest and breaking their power. Fourth, it formed orderly vehicles of enlightened truth by which higher wisdom could be carried down into lower degrees without being scattered or profaned. Fifth, it called forth a sufficient set of operative powers and assigned them to their uses; these held the ultimate boundary of the created arrangement and sustained the whole under its total pressure. At the same time it brought forth the living utterance of Divine truth in its fullness across the discrete degrees, fixing each in a containing form suited to its plane, and placing an additional governing utterance into the dominant natural power; again it sent a directed impulse of communication, conjoining it with the sufficient enlightened agencies for effect in ultimates. Sixth, when the ultimate boundary had been established, the Divine Life rose into clearer exertion and sent out an abundance of powers and ministering intelligences to set limits on every side, guarding the whole compass until the arrangement of uses was finished; then it preserved some within inner safekeeping, and stationed others in fixed outer functions for continual vigilance. Seventh, when mediating Divine truth manifested a revelation in glory suited to reception, the Divine Life established many goods and ministering intelligences to take firm hold of the ordered arrangement, so that the whole structure would stand stable and not turn its forces against heavenly wisdom, but remain subordinated under the primordial illumination. Beyond these, a further great operation is reserved for the completion: the final formed state of the regenerated person, lifted into the heavenly degrees of wisdom and love; in that state the Divine enters, holds what must be held, casts down what opposes, rebuilds what is disordered, sets all into right form, and binds the remaining powers into a unified and permanent order.

---

## Chapter 33: Concerning the Five Things that he constructed with the hard Bodies of the Rulers

**¶1** The Living Spirit constructed five great things from veins and skull[s in the] hard [bodies].

**¶2** The first is the disc that is placed upon [the sh]oulder of the Porter.

**¶3** The se[con]d are the arches of [the] pillar that is before him.

**¶4** [The th]ird is the [mou]ld of the fo[r]m for the gehennas.

**¶5** The fourth are the [seven] pillars that he has erected in the great sea, in [the] seven parts of the universe.

**¶6** The fifth are the four walls that surround the vessels.

> **Textual criticism:**
> - ¶1 [none]: Clean correspondential opening: creation “from veins and skull[s]” grounds the teaching in function (hard ultimates) rather than jurisdiction; no narrative break.
> - ¶2 [none]: Minor lexical ambiguity (“Porter”) but not a textual intrusion. The line stays in functional/correspondential mode: an object (disc) positioned on a bearer, which can be read as an ordering support in
> - ¶3 [none]: Clean continuation: architectural support imagery (“arches… pillar”) continues the same constructed-things sequence. Damage affects precision but does not introduce a second voice or an insert.
> - ¶4 [none]: Potentially harsh term (“gehennas”) but still fits the correspondential substrate: a ‘mould/form’ that contains/limits destructive states. No sign of governance-layer insertion; the item remains a con
> - ¶5 [none]: Clean correspondential cosmological mapping: ‘seven pillars… in the great sea… seven parts of the universe’ reads as functional stabilization distributed through a total field. The numeration (seven) 
> - ¶6 [none]: Clean closure: ‘four walls… surround the vessels’ continues the functional theme of containment and boundary in ultimates. No narrative break; it completes the five-item sequence.

**Changes:** No changes made. All findings (1–6) recommended none; there were no excisions to execute and no annotations to apply to the text.

**Spiritual narrative:** The Divine Breath brought forth a sufficient set of fundamental structures out of the most external and resistant states, where living quality is held as if bound within what is merely ultimate. First, it established a governing generality in the outermost degree and set it upon the power that bears and transmits what is received, so that the lowest level could be held in order under a single rule. Second, it formed the supporting arrangements of truth in ultimates, joined into stable continuity, and placed them as a constant presence before that bearing power, so that what proceeds might stand and not collapse. Third, it fixed a containing pattern for the organized states of evil and falsity, so that their operations would be bounded, recognizable, and constrained to the form of their own life. Fourth, it established a complete progression of stabilizing supports within the general knowledges of the external mind and distributed this establishment through the full expanse of the lower domain, so that every region of the natural degree might be held in connection and restraint. Fifth, it provided full enclosure in the outermost degree around the receiving forms, so that what is admitted could be contained, kept distinct from what is hostile, and preserved for orderly influx and use.

---

## Chapter 34: Concerning the Ten Things that the Ambassador began by his Advent

**¶2** The first: The Great Builder came forth to build the new aeon.

**¶3** The second: The ships moved through the heights in the heavens.

**¶4** The third: The wheels begin to send the life to earth and the upper worlds.

**¶5** The fourth: [The measures were set in their places in the zone.]

**¶6** The fifth is the doors of the ships, that they were opened up [ ...

**¶7** The sixth is the doors too [ ... ... that they were opened to receive the waste.

**¶8** [*] The seventh is [the Sin], which he had separated from the rulers; it fell upon the earth.

**¶9** The eighth: Night and day were separated [from each other in the zone]

**¶10** The ninth: The abortions [fell] to the ground.

**¶11** The tenth: Transmigrations came about in the zone.

> **Textual criticism:**
> - ¶2-4 [none]: Coherent correspondential substrate: the teaching proceeds by functions (build/carry/transmit) in a tenfold cosmogonic-process list. No textual disruption indicated by the spiritual reading; the ‘stor
> - ¶5 [none]: Genuine difficulty due to damage (lacuna), not an insertion: the narrative breaks because the clause that would specify what the ‘measures’ are/ do is missing. This is a transmission loss, not a voice
> - ¶6-7 [none]: Coherent correspondential substrate: ‘doors’ function as openings for reception and for removal of residue. The spiritual reading resumes flow after the damaged ¶5; no signs of an administrative overl
> - ¶8 [excise]: Localized mixed/administrative intrusion into an otherwise functional sequence: the clause “by cause of the Third Ambassador” introduces a named officer-like figure as causal explanation, shifting fro
> - ¶9 [none]: Coherent correspondential substrate: separation of day/night teaches by functional differentiation of states (obscurity/illumination) and orderly alternation. No narrative disruption.
> - ¶10-11 [none]: Genuine difficulty from compressed/unclear lexemes, not clear insertion: the final items use technical terms (“abortions,” “transmigrations”) without enough context, producing interpretive wobble. The

**Changes:** Applied Finding 4 (¶8) excision: removed the causal addendum “by cause of the Third Ambassador” and minimally repunctuated the sentence so it remains coherent (“…from the rulers; it fell upon the earth.”). All other paragraphs unchanged per findings recommending none.

**Spiritual narrative:** First, the Divine went forth from Its own inmost to establish a new spiritual order fit for reception and use. Second, higher doctrinal forms and ordered knowledges were borne through elevated interior states, so that what is from heaven might be conjoined in right arrangement. Third, the operative powers fixed in ultimates began to transmit living influx downward into the natural degree and also through the higher interior degrees, that life might be distributed according to its discrete levels. Fourth, the determination of the required order in the intermediate region is asserted, but the connective matter is missing, so the intended spiritual step cannot be traced with certainty. Fifth, the outermost means of admission and communication were opened, so that what had been shut in externals could receive influx from within and pass it onward into effect. Sixth, those same outermost admissions were also opened to take in what must be rejected—impure residua of falsity and evil—so that they could be gathered into order and removed. Seventh, an active evil that had been held apart from the reigning falsities by a mediating Divine action was allowed to descend into the natural degree, where it could no longer be concealed among interior reasonings but would be met in outward life. Eighth, states of obscurity and states of enlightenment were distinguished and set into their proper alternation, so that the mind might discern its changes and be governed accordingly. Ninth, perverted and lifeless formations that cannot come forth as genuine good and truth were cast down into the outermost level to be exposed and terminated. Tenth, changes of state occurred within the intermediate region between inner and outer life, in which affections and thoughts were shifted and reassigned according to their quality; whether this describes orderly restoration or a wandering produced by disorder remains unclear because the expression is compressed and the sequence lacks its missing link.

---

## Chapter 35: Concerning the Four Works of the Ambassador

**¶2** The first is his image that he displayed in a glorious image.

**¶3** The second is all the doors of the firmaments that were opened at that time. They open [before him] and shut behind him.

**¶4** The third is the light and the releasing that was purified by his image. That light is more than the light that he shall [give forth]

**¶5** The fourth: He has [made apparent], by the perfection of people, his light ship. He has [brought forth] the living soul that shone forth in perfection and fullness over the worlds of enmity.

> **Textual criticism:**
> - ¶2–¶5 (whole chapter) [none]: Clean, compact correspondential fourfold teaching with linear progression. No intrusion of administrative (jurisdictional) cataloguing, no named powers, no rank-inventories, and no seam-expansion. The
> - ¶4 [none]: Genuine local ambiguity in a comparative clause (“more than the light that he shall [give forth]”) due to an unclear referent (what ‘other’ light is being compared). This is not a voice-switch or inse

**Changes:** No changes made. Finding 1 recommends none for ¶2–¶5 (coherent correspondential sequence), and Finding 2 recommends none for ¶4 (local ambiguity not warranting excision).

**Spiritual narrative:** First, the Divine brought forth an efflux of its own quality and set it forth in manifest wisdom, so that what had been hidden within could be grasped in a form fit for reception.

Second, by that manifestation, every passage of communication between higher and lower degrees was opened for the needed admission of influx, and afterward those same passages were closed, so that what had entered might remain arranged in order and not be scattered.

Third, from that self-disclosure there proceeded illumination together with liberation, and this proceeding was cleansed of every mixture that would defile or bend it; and this purified illumination surpassed the former kind of illumination that would otherwise have gone forth.

Fourth, through the bringing of a human receptacle into completeness and right arrangement, the Divine made evident its power to convey and operate in the ultimate degree. By means of that prepared completion it brought forth living consciousness, and this consciousness, in full integrity and abundance, spread its shining life over states hostile to good, so that even in resisted regions a perfected presence might be established and active.

---

## Chapter 36: Concerning the Wheel that exists in front of the King of Honour

**¶1** ⟨p.88⟩
[Agai]n the [teaching] speaks about the wheel that exists in front [of the King of Honour who dwells in] the seventh firmament [there], the root of the rulers above and below in a great and cruel fetter; as the entire will of the great King of Honour is placed in [it]. Also, their entire enmity, which is in the firmaments, is [gathered] and ensnared in it.

The seal between i[s] every firmament and the rulers who are there [which bind them] are in it; for should one of them wish to escape, he will be bound [there]. The spheres and stars and [the] leader of all the powers exist there, and can be known by it, in front of the King of Honour.

**¶3** The twelf[t]h seal that is in it is its five shining men that are conjoined and enmeshed through the entire zone; because every place where they are cramped, and every place where they have space, is displayed by that wheel. For the root of all the firmaments above and below, and that of their rulers, is ensnared and fixed in it.

**¶4** The observation [of the wheel] is set right at all times opposite the great King o[f Honour in his place] so that fear, wh[ich is from him] will assume all the powers of mixture [and will hold] their root affixed there, so that they cannot make an esc[a]pe from the fetter of their bond.

**¶5** That wheel rese[m]bles, in this respect, a chain in front of the chief guard, for when [anything moves] in th[ose who are guarded] in the [chain]in.

**¶6** This is also the case for all the rulers who are in the firmaments, and every power of the zone: If they should wish to escape, they shall be recognised and revealed by that wheel. [As] the wheel is like a great mirror, for [the di]scrimination of all things [set] in it.

> **Textual criticism:**
> - ¶1-4, ¶6 [none]: Clean, continuous correspondential teaching: the ‘wheel’ is explained by what it DOES (binds, discloses, stabilizes) and the narrative develops that function stepwise (containment → discrimination/vis
> - ¶5 [none]: Genuine difficulty due to textual damage/fragmentation, not an insertion. The analogy (‘chain… chief guard’) continues the same functional argument (surveillance-by-restraint), but the clause that sho

**Changes:** No changes made. Finding 1 (¶1–4, ¶6) recommends none; Finding 2 (¶5) recommends none (damage/fragmentation, not an insertion), and there were no excision recommendations to execute.

**Spiritual narrative:** The teaching speaks again about the total ordering of the ultimate degree that stands openly before the Divine in the inmost height, as the primary containment and fastening of every subordinate governing principle in higher and lower regions under a hard restraint; for the whole determination of Divine rule is fixed there as the governing ultimate. Moreover, every hostile impulse and resisting intention arising within the heavens is gathered, confined, and held fast within that same restraint.

This ultimate ordering is in the brightness of manifest wisdom, arranged with harmonious form and the appearance of perfect order. Within it is fullness of organized discriminations that keep distinct each heaven and each governing power within it; and all of them are held within that discrimination, so that if any governing principle should attempt to withdraw from its appointed order, it is immediately constrained. If it should attempt to introduce a hidden perversion within its constraint, that perversion becomes evident at once within the ordering itself. All general knowledges in the outer degree, all distinct knowledges within them, and the chief directing influence among operative powers are present there and are made knowable by means of that ordering, in the Divine presence.

The fullness of discrimination within that ordering includes a sufficient complement of bright operative powers conjoined and mutually intertwined throughout the whole extension of the ultimate degree; and every state in which they are pressed into limitation, and every state in which they are given freedom of extension, is laid open by that ordering. For the first basis of all the heavens above and below, and of their governing principles, is held, fixed, and made immovable within it.

A perpetual attentive discernment is maintained directly before the Divine, so that a holy dread—rising from the Divine presence—seizes all composite and mixed operative forces and keeps their first principles fastened there, so that they cannot withdraw from the restraint that binds them into order.

In this respect the ultimate ordering is like an unbroken binding restraint before the protecting vigilance that guards Divine order; for whenever anything stirs toward disorder among those held within the restraint, the disturbance is immediately within that vigilance.

This is likewise the case for all governing principles in the heavens and for every operative force in the ultimate degree: if any should attempt to withdraw, it is at once identified and uncovered by that ordering. For this ordering is as an all-disclosing faculty of discernment, because the power to distinguish and reveal every quality and tendency is present within it.

---

## Chapter 37: Concerning the Three Zones.

**¶2** When he built and constructed the worlds, he suspended the zones on their zones, and within another zone also. The first zone is the five powers of darkness, males and females.

**¶3** This is why he hung one zone on a zone! He hung the five powers of Darkness on the five shining men, the sons of the Man; because all the enmity is entwined and bound in them.

**¶4** Also, within the zone are the five sons of the [Living] Spirit; because the entire edifice is suspended from them, it being [as they heard]ard (?) him too, the oth[others who came] after them.

**¶5** And this is the one who came to [the heights, to]rmaments above [that] which is in one zone, is the great [King of Honour] who e[exists and dwells] within the [inmost depth] of the universe.

> **Textual criticism:**
> - ¶2 [none]: Clean correspondential setup: the chapter’s core teaching is architectural/functional (zones suspended within zones) and immediately defines the lowest zone by what it contains (the five powers), not 
> - ¶3 [none]: Clean correspondential continuation: the ‘why’ clause explains the function of the suspension (binding enmity) and keeps the narrative coherent. The named group (“five shining men”) participates as fu
> - ¶4 [none]: Genuine difficulty due to damage/garbling at the end of the paragraph. The first half remains correspondential (the edifice is suspended from the five sons), but the trailing clause is fragmentary and
> - ¶5 [none]: Mostly correspondential, with a mild christological/royal titulature seam: the paragraph re-centers the architecture in an indwelling ‘one’ at the inmost depth. The narrative shift noted by the readin

**Changes:** No changes made. All findings (¶2–¶5) carried a recommendation of none; therefore no excisions were executed, including in ¶4 where the critic diagnoses corruption but explicitly recommends no excision.

**Spiritual narrative:** When the Divine brought the ordered levels of being into determinate form, it arranged degree upon degree, and set subordinate degrees within higher ones, so that each lower plane might be held and governed by what is above it. The first encompassing boundary consisted of a sufficient set of fundamental evils and falsities in their active and receptive modes.

Degree was therefore fastened to degree: that whole complex of disorder was attached to a sufficient set of enlightened human faculties proceeding from the Divine Human, so that all opposition might be gathered, restrained, and fixed within ultimate limits; for every hostility against good and truth is interwoven into such externals and can be bound there.

Within that same ordered boundary there was also a sufficient complement of operative powers proceeding from the Divine that gives life and motion, because the entire structure of heaven and the church depends on these for its support and continuity; but here the sequence becomes unclear, and it seems to say that these primary supports were established first and that other sustaining agencies were then arranged after them.

And there is One who is present in the highest interior governance of all that is held within the ordered degrees: the supreme authority of Divine truth united with Divine good, abiding at the inmost center of the whole, from which it upholds and contains the universe from within.

---

**¶43** There are occasions when sin shall mount up with its foolishness; and it disturbs the consideration, and it clouds the wisdom and the understanding of the person. It causes truth to be split into doubt in him, and he utters some foo[li]sh words and some [empty deeds]

**¶51** [T]hen, have I taught you, and opened your (sg.) [eyes], as to how confusion shall arise in the zo[ne], in the camps of these great mig[hty] gods? Again, this is how disturbance shall come about, from ti[me] to time, in the [camps] of the [Li]ght [M]ind.

**¶52** So al[so] these watch-keepers, for they are set [as] fi[rm in] the zone, (but) they are not visible. Again, this too is the way of the Light Mind, [for he is] not visible in the body.

**¶53** Also, [corr]esponding to th[ese] outer [watch-keepers]: in that while they are great, [y]et have they contorted themselves. They have [bent themselves down], they have become small, to the measure of the task that is apportioned to each o[n]e [of t]hem. Again, this too is like the Mind, himse[lf] a great one, and exalted; but he is bent over and [has] become small, to this little worthless [bo]dy.

**¶54** [Even so] the gods in the outer [z]one: they are transcendent and purified, they are set [f]a[st in] the mingling of the totality, (but) are not defiled. [This too is like the] Light [Mind]. While he is placed in hi[s body of flesh,]

**¶55** ⟨p.100⟩
Again, behold the mightiness and the activity of the Light Mind: how vast he is over all the watch-districts of the body! He stays fast at his camp. He shuts off all deliberations of the body from the beguilements of Sin. He limits them and distributes them out. He sets them down at his pleasure.

**¶57** I have taught you] the deeds of the [Light] Mind! Whoever has an open and percept[ive] eye, [he can] appear to him. Whoever has not that eye, he can not appear to him.
