**Changes:** Applied excisions exactly as recommended by the critic. (Finding 4) In ¶13, excised the abrupt roster tail beginning “also] the Living Spirit and the Ambassador [yet] the five sleepless guardians,” leaving the functional conclusion intact. (Finding 5) Excised all of ¶14 (the full appointment/jurisdiction catalogue). (Finding 6) Excised ¶15–18 in full (the administrative expansion concerning intellectuals, sons lists, and camp/watch-district ordinances). Per rules, ¶19–29 were not altered (Finding 7: annotate only). All other paragraphs unchanged.

**Spiritual narrative:** You asked about Divine wisdom that is to enter those being made holy, and you were troubled that it is spoken of as though it were one power among many, and that it comes into the natural person and restrains the former ruling self by a sufficient set of governing faculties fixed in the outermost life. You therefore asked where that wisdom is, since the former self seems restrained in the natural mind and yet rebellions still break out there at intervals. You asked how what is infinite and unchanging can be present within what is finite and narrow, and how what is pure can be joined to what is unclean without being stained. You asked why, if wisdom is within the holy, its own likeness is not manifest as it is in itself; and why those commissioned to teach are still oppressed by the world, so that their authority seems doubtful, even though much had already been disclosed openly.

The answer was given that if you persist in these matters until they become living within you, you will understand what you seek. For the Divine opens interior perception in those who can receive it, and causes interior truth to rise and overflow for those who long for it, so that they may receive understanding and live from it.

First you must know that every organized falsity the adversary of the lights builds is arranged in the form of a person, because the human form is the form of all order. Thus the whole structure of perverted worship and perverted thought imitates the arrangement of truths, but turns them into coverings and constraints. The highest governing falsity is the beginning of outward coverings; what follows from it is their continuation; and the inner seat of cravings is a sufficient complex of unfolded persuasions that supports those coverings. The fixed general frameworks that hold that error together are the planes by which it is kept distinct and upheld. The intermediate ordering of cognitions and their appearances is the arrangement of knowledges and their measured cycles; and what extends from that intermediate ordering into the extremes is the outspreading of influences into the full compass of external life. The lower ruling impulses are the distinct levels of the merely natural mind resting upon the ultimate sustaining faculty that holds externals in place, and the last extension into the outermost life is the whole boundary of that system in its ultimate zone.

Within that inverted form, the central love of that system is the human race as it is grasped by that error. The varieties of affections that feed it appear as the lower affections of life: the more bodily loves, the airy and changeable thoughts, the slippery cognitions that move in general knowledges, and the lowest sensuous impulses that cling to external things. Its outermost general truth is a boundary surrounding violent self-love and its consuming desire; its intermediate containants are the containing forms of that consuming desire; its inward denial is active falsity; its bitter ruling motive is the sharpest lust; its broad extension is the diffusion of influence through external societies and states; and the channels of its circulation are all the sources and pathways by which falsities and cravings are distributed. At points the description becomes textually broken and uncertain, yet the sense continues to present a single inverted organism of evil order.

Thus each external system is held in a kind of equilibrium and coherence. A sufficient number of dominant falsities are fixed within it and act as its life. In such a form the human being is seen as a transgressor, and yet within the Divine government there is also living power and Divine mediation, together with a sufficient guarding in ultimates.

Over these structures the Divine provides higher oversight: an interior enlightening fixed in the higher understanding; a universal governing thought standing in the higher generals of truth; a hard firmness of truth that serves as discerning insight; an ordering counsel that holds steady through the threefold progression of life; and an ultimate sustaining faculty that fixes and supports the lowest plane. Also there is a refinement and ordering of living intellectual powers for use, and the establishing of hearing and obedience in the lower mind, so that conjunction may be made between living powers from the Divine life and combatting powers that engage evil in the outermost degree.

Then a higher presence is set within these ordered things: Divine wisdom itself as the central support of all that is true, the perfected human form within the mind. And within the same order there is placed Divine truth accommodated to reception, as the formed expression of the living Word and as the capacity to hear and to obey.

These arrangements are strengthened in both higher and lower degrees so that each faculty remains secure in its appointed province and neither revolt nor treachery should break out within it.

Yet you were shown that even under Divine ordering, disturbances arise where evil is permitted to act so that it may be exposed, limited, and judged. For even where interior enlightening stands above states of restraint and dispels the gloom of spiritual death, a turning-away can still arise. Sin produces disorder and entangles itself with the soul, mixing itself with good that it has driven outward toward the appearance of Divine mediation. It insinuates itself into a higher region of thought above guarding perception, and from there again entangles itself with good. Then it is separated and sinks into divided states of life: into what is without truth and into what is with truth but not yet living. From that separation it forms fixed systems of perverted perception in what is without truth, and in general knowledges it at once takes form and raises great agitation in externals.

Likewise in the province of higher governing thought, disturbance can arise, and zeal without charity can turn into wrath. Then treachery and anger occur in that domain. Loftier reasonings that should have remained in order descend into external life in that province and commit profanations; they teach crafts of self-intelligence and unveil lofty things in a profaned way. From this comes disturbance and ruin in the external mind.

And in the province of hard firmness of truth, which ought to be fulfillment and stability, treachery can occur when what is only apparent good falls down into externals. Then the natural self is formed together with its companion love, and these are begotten so that evils may reign through them in life. Every object of craving is then produced in externals, and the whole external life is filled with lust.

Again, in the province of ordering counsel that governs the threefold progression of life, disturbance and affliction can arise, and the lower degrees are pained and oppressed. When Divine mediation is displayed to the mind, passages for ascent are closed by falsity and the rising of the three essentials of life is impeded: the rising of love, the rising of truth, and the rising of their activity into use.

And again, in the province of the ultimate sustaining faculty, revolt can arise from the depths beneath: the lower supports are bent, the bindings underneath are loosened, and the foundation in externals is shaken.

Therefore, when such a shaking occurs in the province of interior enlightening, a higher support comes forth: the perfected human form within the mind manifests as a helper, bearing all burdens so that the lower degrees do not collapse.

And when treachery and revolt arise in the province of higher governing thought—when loftier reasonings descend and profane—Divine truths in fullness are brought to bear against them. They restrain those reasonings by an unbreakable limitation within the obscured states that belong to them, and they remove the derivative falsities they have spawned from the external mind.

And when merely apparent goods descend again in the province of hard firmness and beget the natural self and its companion love for the sake of ruling, Divine truth accommodated to the lower mind is sent in response to the prayers of a sufficient set of remaining goods. It takes up what can be rescued from those apparent goods and fixes the deceits beneath the external reasoning of the natural self.

And because the lower degrees were shaken and the passages of ascent were hindered, and the sources of love, truth, and use were impeded, Divine truth accommodated to reception descends further. It takes up the companion love of the natural self, straightens the courses by which love, truth, and use must rise, opens their sources, and sets the ascent into order.

And because the ultimate foundation beneath the sustaining faculty was loosened from its bindings, Divine truth descends even into that lowest place, sets the lowest bonds in order, strengthens them, and then rises again into rest.

From all this you may see that even in the ordered system of the mind, where higher powers preside over their provinces, disturbances and treacheries may occur from time to time. These are permitted with deep humiliation of what is proud, until hostile powers are subdued.

So it is also with the individual person. A mighty power can dwell within what is small: Divine wisdom lives within the natural mind though that mind is narrow. Nevertheless sin also dwells there, and the former ruling self lodged in it is cruel and cunning, until wisdom finds how to humble the natural life and lead it according to Divine pleasure. And just as disturbances occur at intervals in the outer provinces of the greater order, so disturbances occur in the province of the individual mind: sin raises agitations from time to time in natural life.

Understand, then, that the powers of light are good; they see beginnings and ends and act from right judgment. For this reason they may permit evil to initiate disorder and do its pleasure for a brief time; then they seize it and set it under judgment, because they act first from justice.

As to your question how infinite wisdom can enter and take up what is finite: consider that even higher powers, though great, are each held within the particular function and degree assigned to them, compressed into the bounds of their office, and fixed there in a rooted endurance. In the same way Divine wisdom, though exalted above all, can be present within a finite recipient by accommodation to function and degree.

You were then shown more plainly how sin holds the soul: it binds the soul’s understanding in the hardness of ultimate falsity; it binds the soul’s thought in the tough persistence of habit; it binds the soul’s discerning insight in the circulating channels of persuasion; it binds the soul’s counsel in the cravings of the fleshly will; and it binds the soul’s consideration in the outermost sensuous life. It fixes a sufficient set of its own powers upon the soul’s corresponding faculties, setting falsified understanding over understanding, perverted thought over thought, deceptive insight over insight, cunning counsel over counsel, and sensuous calculation over consideration. It establishes dominating falsities and evils upon the soul’s operative faculties now held in the natural life, and these continually urge the soul into every evil: into lusts, into profanations that worship what is not Divine, into false opinions, and into the humiliation of slavery under the lower self, so that the soul bows to external loves that are unclean and distorted.

In that state the soul takes on falsity and forgetfulness. It forgets its origin, its quality, and its conjunctions, and it does not know the way to turn upward in prayer or to call for help. It becomes hostile to its own source and turns against the remaining good within itself, dimming and nearly extinguishing the light it still possesses.

Then Divine wisdom comes and finds the soul in its restraints and takes it up within the reception of its own ordering light. It becomes for the soul the power that deals with the bonds in the natural life. It loosens the soul’s understanding from ultimate falsity and instead binds the falsified understanding there. It releases the soul’s thought from stubborn habit and binds the perverted thought there. It releases the soul’s insight from persuasive channels and binds the deceptive insight there. It loosens the soul’s counsel from fleshly craving and binds the cunning counsel there. It releases the soul’s consideration from sensuous outermost life and binds the sensuous calculation there. Thus the soul’s faculties are freed, and the once-loose faculties of sin are restrained.

Then wisdom sets the soul’s faculties in right order, gives them form, purifies them, and builds from them a new self, a child of righteousness. When it has formed and purified this new self, it brings forth from the purified faculties a sufficient set of living powers and places them within the new self: love within the new understanding, faith within the new thought, completeness within the new insight, patience within the new counsel, and wisdom within the new consideration. It purifies the formed expression of truth from the falsity that had spoken within it and adds its own living expression, so that what is received becomes nourishment and strengthening.

When this ordering is carried through to fullness in all organized truths, the person’s interior life is made righteous. Formerly the person ran in the track of disorder; now the person runs in order and the path of ascent is opened. The mind is raised into higher states and more interior communities of good and truth. Thus the former ruling self is restrained in the outer life, and its lust, craft, and folly are held within the bound faculties, and the dark spirit that acted through them is shut up with them in severe misery.

Then the new self governs by love, faith, completeness, patience, and wisdom. Yet the true king within that kingdom is Divine wisdom itself, ruling the whole according to Divine pleasure. And although sin is restrained as the faculties are held in order, some affliction may still arise in the natural mind from time to time. For at times sin rises again with its folly; it disturbs the outermost consideration, clouds wisdom and understanding, splits truth into doubt, and brings forth foolish speech and empty deeds.

In this way you were taught how disturbances can arise within ordered systems even under higher guardianship, and likewise how disturbances can arise at intervals within the province where Divine wisdom governs the natural person. Higher guardians are fixed in their functions and are not visible to bodily senses; so too Divine wisdom is not visible in the natural mind as an object, though it is present as life and order. And just as higher powers, though great, accommodate themselves to the use assigned and become as small as that use requires, so Divine wisdom bends itself by accommodation to the finite recipient. And just as higher powers remain pure and elevated within the mingled complexity of the whole and yet are not defiled, so Divine wisdom, while present within the natural person, is not defiled.

Consider also the might and activity of Divine wisdom: how widely it governs all the provinces of the natural mind. It remains in its own interior station, shuts off deliberations from the seductions of sin, limits and distributes them, and sets them in order according to Divine pleasure. These are the works of Divine wisdom. Whoever has opened interior perception can perceive its presence; whoever lacks that perception cannot.

---

## Chapter 39: Concerning the Three Days and the Two Deaths

**¶4** The first day is our father, the Man. From the time when the First Man went down to the abyss of darkness and drowned in it; and the Living Spirit was sent, who went and bore him up. After the Man had ascended, the Father of Life and the Great Spirit set things in order, and the worlds above and below.

**¶5** Furthermore, the glorious Ambassador came and dwelt in the ship of the day. He displayed his glorious image, and he purified the light out of all things. He walked in the heights of the heavens. Till the time when the world will be dissolved, all things destroyed, the great fire released, and the Last Statue ascends. These three deeds were begun on this first day, when they sent the First Man with his five sons.

**¶6** ⟨p.103⟩ In contrast, [the first death] is the darkness that mixed with the five sons of the First Man.

**¶7** The second day is the time when the fathers of light, who were victorious in the struggle, will sit on their thrones in the new aeon and dwell on the new earth. And they reign in the new aeon till the Father unveils his image above them.

**¶8** The {length of the sojourn} in the new aeon equals the measure of the first day spent in affliction. They will reign like this and rejoice in the new aeon.

**¶9** The great third day [is the time] when the Father will unveil over them his image ... ... and he raises them up [above them his image] and he receives them into his hidden treasury ... and he gives [to the life] his soul. He can pour out upon them ... his ambrosia and his sweet perfume, which takes away all affliction that they have seen with their eyes. [to them of] fullness and a joy eternal! When his grace would sate them, he gives to them his [of his own] and they are made anew in them.

**¶10** Also, he can summon them once more in peace and silence, and perfect their stature in same measure as his first aeons. And he sets each power in its aeon, in its dwelling-place. They will dwell in the new aeon in their aeons!

**¶11** Now, when they dwell in their aeons, they will become rich beyond measure, for ever, beyond time. From then on they will not count that season amongst them; nor the number of the days, nor the hours, generation after generation, for ever and ever. The Father will not be hidden from them, from this time on. Rather, he has accomplished (his) unveiling to them [once;] he being revealed to them for ever.

**¶12** These are the three great days, [which the Father of Greatness has given number to in the reckoning].

**¶13** In contrast, the two deaths that I have proclaimed to you are these.

**¶14** ⟨p.104⟩ The first death is from the time when the light fell to the darkness, and was mixed in with the rulers of darkness; until the time when the light will become pure, and be separated from the darkness in that great fire. The remainder left behind there can build and add to that Last Statue.

**¶15** Now, the second death is the death in which the souls of sinful men shall die; when [they will] be stripped of the shining light that illuminates the world. And also they are separated from the living air, from which they receive [living] breath; and they are deprived of this living soul, which [had been placed] in the world; and they are separated and swept away from this Last Statue [and they are seized] and bound on [their own bonds] the female will become [female of lust] their torture and their affliction; for their deeds [have set them in] those three places;

**¶16** These are the two deaths. The first death is temporal; [but] the second death is eternal. It is the sec[ond deat]h!

> **Textual criticism:**

> - ¶6 [none]: Minor seam/label intrusion but not a disruptive insertion: an abrupt rubric-like contrast line (“In contrast…”) introduces ‘first death’ early, before the later formal “two deaths” section. This is li
> - ¶7–12 [none]: Clean correspondential substrate: the ‘second day’ and ‘third day’ are taught as states of enthronement, unveiling, satisfaction, perfection, and timelessness—i.e., by experiential/functional transiti
> - ¶12 [none]: Clean but slightly editorialized closure: the bracketed clause looks like a scribe’s clarifying rubric (“given number… in the reckoning”), reinforcing the chapter’s ‘days’ as a counted schema. It does
> - ¶13–14 [none]: Clean correspondential ‘opposite sense’ transition: the narrative pivots from three ‘days’ to two ‘deaths’ without importing administrative material. The first death is temporally bounded (mixture → p
> - ¶15 [none]: Genuine difficulty from damage and a mythic image, not an administrative insertion: the paragraph is syntactically broken (multiple brackets, interrupted clauses), and includes an opaque figure (“the 
> - ¶16 [none]: Clean closure with emphatic repetition, likely a rhetorical flourish rather than a later overlay. It restates the twofold distinction in the same functional mode (temporal vs eternal) and seals the se

**Changes:** No changes made. The critic’s findings (1–7) recommended no excisions; all paragraphs were retained as restored, including ¶6’s anticipatory label (Finding 2) and the damaged/opaque phrasing in ¶15 (Finding 6).

**Spiritual narrative:** The first state is the primal origin of all in the human form: the first good that is called forth to engage what is opposed. When that first good descended into the lowest denial and was immersed in it, a saving influx from the Divine was sent, which entered into that immersed good and lifted it up. After this good was raised again, the Divine as love together with the Divine as wisdom arranged all things into right order, both in the higher degrees and in the lower degrees.

Moreover, a glorious mediating presence came forth and took its station in the outwardly manifest reception of wisdom. It made the Divine likeness visible, and it separated genuine wisdom from every admixture. It acted from elevated states of understanding, and this continues until the time when the present arrangement of external states is brought to its end: all disordered forms are dissolved, the vast intensity of self-love is released for its final work, and the final collective form of what has been gathered into order is raised into permanence. These operations had their beginning in that first state, when the primal good was sent forth together with a sufficient fullness of operative powers.

In the opposite sense, the first perishing is the active falsity that entered into those operative powers and confused them.

The second state is the time when those who belong to wisdom and remained faithful through conflict are established in governing truths within a new spiritual condition, and dwell in a renewed ultimate plane of life. They continue in that new condition until the Divine reveals the visible presence of its own likeness above them.

The duration of their abiding in that new condition equals the measure of the first state’s endurance under distress; thus they are established and rejoice within the renewed condition.

The great third state is when the Divine reveals above them the manifest presence of its own likeness, raises them into higher reception, receives them into an interior safeguarding of good and truth, and imparts to them what is of its own life. Then heavenly delight and the gentle sweetness of peace flow into them, taking away every distress they had perceived; there is fullness and unending joy. When Divine mercy satisfies them, it grants them what further perfects their reception, and within that gift they are renewed.

It also gathers them again into a state of peace held in potency, brings their spiritual form to completion in the same fullness as the first ordered states, and establishes each distinct power within its own state and proper reception. They dwell in the renewed condition within their own ordered states.

And when they dwell in their ordered states, they come into immeasurable abundance forever, beyond time. From then on they do not measure life by successions, nor by the reckoning of states and their least distinctions, age after age forever. The Divine is not hidden from them from that time onward; the unveiling has been accomplished to them, and the Divine is disclosed to them forever.

These are the three great states which the Divine has set in order as a complete process.

In the opposite sense, there are two perishing states.

The first perishing is from the time when wisdom descended into denial and was mingled with dominant falsities, until the time when wisdom is made pure and separated from denial in the final vast ignition of self-love for judgment and purification. What remains after that separation can still be formed and added into the final collective order that is raised into permanence.

The second perishing is the perishing in which the interior lives of those who are in evil die: they are deprived of outwardly shining truths that give understanding in the natural degree; cut off from living influx by which spiritual vitality is received; deprived of the very faculty of life that had been present for reception in the world; separated and cast away from the final collective order and held fast in their own bonds. Then the corrupted will becomes their torment and their distress, because their works have fixed them into the full series of infernal states.

These are the two perishing states: the first is within time, and the second is without end—the eternal perishing.

---

## Chapter 40: Concerning the Three Things that were established by the Light

**¶2** Three things would be fashioned by the Light through necessity. For there can be no other way nor other result, to allow them to stay without coming about. For the darkness, the enemy, must be bound by them; he who stood up against the Light from the beginning. Due to this, these things have no (other) result, to allow them to stay without coming about.

**¶3** Now, the first thing is the new luminous earth, which was set and built on top of this earth [and no one will be able to build upon] it, namely that alien body [which will not be able to be] built upon it.

**¶4** The second thing is this: They will [shut him up] and they [will bind him in] the new earth, the coffin [and the prison] ⟨p.105⟩ of the dark fire, the companion of the enemy.

**¶5** The third thing is the souls of all the sinners who have been condemned by their deeds. They were [judged, and there is] over them woe; they have been condemned according to the wicked deeds they had committed, and due to this they will be crucified on the enemy, he who had raised himself up against the greatness.

**¶6** They will seal up this final lump when all the likenesses and images of every shape will be nailed in it. Also, those will be bound by this last fetter for eternity, and be placed as a footstool and a mat and a rag for this coffin [and prison] which is the prison of the enemy. It will be placed, being a boundary-marker and a great void between the two, between the darkness and the enemy, so that they cannot reach one another, nor can they hear their voices; rather they will become sterile as they are separated from each other for ever and ever.

> **Textual criticism:**
> - ¶2 [none]: Clean correspondential setup: the chapter frames a necessary threefold functional provision (things that must come about because they perform the binding). No jurisdictional listing, no named administ
> - ¶3 [none]: Mostly clean correspondential mapping (a new ‘earth’ as an ultimate boundary/plane), with mild lexical/translation strain caused by damaged/uncertain wording around the bracketed clauses and the phras
> - ¶4 [none]: Clean correspondential confinement motif: the ‘new earth’ functions as container/prison for ‘dark fire’ and the enemy. The reading’s slight forcing (“companion” → attendant falsity) is interpretive bu
> - ¶5 [none]: Clean moral-teleological third member of the triad (fate of sinners) integrated with the binding theme. The vivid image “crucified on the enemy” is stark but still functions within the same restraint 
> - ¶6 [none]: Rhetorically intensified but still coherent correspondential ‘sealing/boundary’ conclusion. The only stumble is stylistic multiplication (“footstool… mat… rag”), which reads as emphatic synonymy, not 

**Changes:** No changes made. The textual criticism findings recommend none for ¶2–¶6 (Findings 1–5), with no excisions or annotations required.

**Spiritual narrative:** By the inevitable order of Divine wisdom proceeding from Divine love, a complete provision must be brought into fixed being; it cannot fail, because without it the primal opposition to the Divine would not be finally restrained. That opposing falsity must be held fast by what is thus established, since from the beginning it set itself against what is from heaven, and therefore the Divine order necessarily brings forth the means of its binding.

First, a new ultimate plane must be established: an external degree made luminous with truth and stable for the reception of good. It is set above the former ultimate, and founded as a higher, purified external in which what is foreign to heaven can find no footing, nor form a supporting base for itself.

Second, the ruling infernal love that burns to destroy truth, together with the falsity that cleaves to it, must be completely shut up within that new ultimate. The ultimate itself becomes the fixed containing form of restraint, so that this evil and its attendant persuasion cannot flow out, act, or extend itself.

Third, all who by their own works have united themselves with evil and falsity must come into a final state according to what they have willed and done. Over them is grief, because they have confirmed themselves in what is against good; and by the opposition they chose, they are fixed in conjunction with that dominant falsity which exalted itself against the Divine greatness.

Then the last restraint is made complete when every counterfeit good and every fabricated appearance of truth, in whatever variety, is immobilized within that final containment, so that it cannot shift form, disguise itself, or escape. By this permanent bond, all such states are held for ever in abasement and impotence, made to serve only as the lowest and most contemptible support of that enclosing confinement. And this restraint stands as an impassable limit and a vast separation between infernal falsity and infernal evil, so that they cannot reach each other, cannot communicate, and cannot even take in one another’s persuasions; thus they become barren of further production, because they are severed from one another to eternity.

---

## Chapter 41: Concerning the Three Blows that befell the Enemy because of the Light.

**¶1** The Darkness itself, the Enemy, has received three bad blows with three wars; and worse dangers from the Light in those three wars.

**¶2** The first: (The enemy) was brought out to the middle and separated from his dark earth, whence he had departed. He was vanquished in that first war, caught and [bound] by the living soul.

**¶3** The second blow is the time when he will be dissolved and melted, and he perishes in this great fire. He is destroyed and overthrown, from the images that are all by which he exists. And he will be gathered in [to the] fetter corresponding to his archetype, and be made like he was from the first beginning.

**¶4** The third blow that will befall the Enemy is the 'rolling back' [at the] end when everything will be separated, and male will be divided [from] female. Now, the male will be bound in the lump, but the female [will be thrown] in to the tomb. For it will be divided [and be divided apart, and be set apart] stone in their midst, for generation upon generation, for ever and ever.

**¶5** ⟨p.106⟩ This is the way that the binding of the enemy will come about, in a fetter that is burdensome and strong, one from which there is never escape; because [they have achieved] his binding and bound him for ever. And they have achieved his being set apart, and he has been set apart for ever.

> **Textual criticism:**
> - ¶1 [none]: Clean opening in correspondential teaching: sets a three-stage process of defeat with escalating intensity. No intrusive jurisdictions, lists, or rank-language; the chapter’s story is a single functio
> - ¶2 [none]: Clean correspondential substrate: the first ‘blow’ is described as extraction, separation, defeat, and binding—an ordered sequence of actions. The only potential snag (‘middle’, ‘living soul’) is sema
> - ¶3 [none]: Clean correspondential substrate: deepening of the process via dissolution/melting, destruction of sustaining ‘images,’ then re-collection into a fitting fetter “corresponding to his archetype.” This 
> - ¶4 [none]: Genuine difficulty inside the same correspondential narrative: gendered terms (‘male/female’), ‘lump/tomb,’ and especially the clause about “stone in their midst” are abrupt images, but they still ser
> - ¶5 [none]: Clean concluding seal in correspondential voice: recapitulates the effect (“binding… set apart… for ever”) without introducing new actors or administrative taxonomy. Repetition functions as closure, n

**Changes:** No changes made. All findings (1–5) recommended none (excise 0; annotate 0), and no paragraphs were targeted for excision.

**Spiritual narrative:** The very principle of denial that opposes the Divine has been struck by a complete series of defeats through a full course of spiritual combat, and in each stage it has met more severe peril from wisdom and its power.

First, it was drawn out from concealed and hardened falsity into a state of open spiritual exposure, and it was cut off from the ultimate ground in which its denial had rooted itself. In that first combat it was overcome, restrained, and held fast by the living reception of good and truth.

Second comes the state when its coherence is undone: its hardened persuasions are loosened, its fixed form is dispersed, and it perishes by the consuming presence of pure love that cannot be borne by self-love. It is ruined and cast down because the fabricated appearances that once supported its seeming life are taken away. Then it is drawn together into a lasting restraint that matches its own inmost pattern, and it is reduced to the same powerless condition it had at its first emergence.

Third comes the final reversal at completion, when every element is discriminated and set in order, and what is active and ruling in evil is separated from what is receptive and fostering within it. Then the ruling falsity is confined in a hardened, undivided compulsion, while the receptive element is shut up in utter spiritual death. Between them an unremovable separation is fixed, an immutable barrier established in the midst, through successive states without end.

In this way the perpetual restraint of that resisting principle is effected: by a confinement heavy with its own evil and made firm by Divine order, from which there is no release, because its restraint is fully accomplished and confirmed to eternity. Its removal from all mingling with what is good is likewise fully accomplished, and that separation stands to eternity.

## Chapter 42: Concerning the Three Vessels

**¶2** Happen you know, my beloved ones, that when these vessels were discharged, they were discharged in [great] slaughter, but those [took out] these vessels from them.

**¶3** ⟨p.107⟩
They are like the form of a man who has fallen into a purulent sickness. Other wounds and diseases are in his body, and even bile and other poisons are in his inner limbs. He achieves good health by a wise doctor, who gives him one remedy to drink that causes his purulent sickness to break out above, and gives him another medicine to drink that causes his sickness to discharge below, and he gives him yet another medicine to drink that cures his wounds for him [and makes him rest] calmly and silently.

**¶4** Through the skill of a wise doctor, with his effective medicines to consume that take away all sicknesses in this manner and dissolve all magic, the doctors shall [discover] they work cures through the aroma of the medicine, other wounds [remove] what is hidden, they at last tear them out from [the inner limbs] manifested above and below [and manifested above and below, by the] the aroma of edible medicines that were placed upon them.

**¶5** This is also what these vessels of water, of darkness and of fire are like, which are hidden in the body of all the rulers above; while the Living Spirit is like the wise doctor. These three healing drugs are the three garments that the Living Spirit assumes upon his body: of wind, of water, and of fire. Through them he has set in order the things below. For by those three garments he has been displayed to the rulers who are above, with hard-heartedness, with shaking and fear, with derangement; from them he discharged the three vessels, those of water, of darkness and of fire.

**¶6** The case of these vessels is just like a child who is in his mother's womb and is swathed by seven garments. The first is the marrow, the second is the bones, the third is the tendons, the fourth is the flesh, the fifth the veins, the sixth the blood, the seventh the skin of the body that covers him. The other garment also [that is upon him] this child. The marrow and the bones, the sinews and flesh, the veins and blood and skin, together with the garment that she wears and the linen that swathes her, total nine. Together with the nine of the child, these and the others, they shall complete and make eighteen garments. Now, you shall find the soul of this child is entwined within all these.

**¶7** ⟨p.108⟩
And look see: When the smell of the action of the demon, which brings about the stench of birth, will reveal the child; immediately [the seven garments] shall [be loosened] all [these things] and it emerges from a crack that has become visible, while no tear nor wound occurs in the body of the child. In the same way as this child opens up the womb of his mother, so is it also with these vessels of water, of darkness and of fire. The actions that open up his mother to the child, and she shakes him out and reveals him; these are the three living garments that the Living Spirit assumed on his body.

**¶8** For by the aspect of the garment of water [he drew out the vessel of water] from the rulers; he moved [from them,] they are the poison and death with which their veins and entrails are filled.

**¶9** By the aspect of the garment of wind, in which he was revealed [to the rulers;] he has swept away and erased the entire destructive shadow and dirt, discharged it down to the earth. Afterwards he swept it out and confined it in the vessel of darkness that surrounds the universe.

**¶10** Also, by the garment of living fire, through its splendour and vigour, he discharged the fire of the darkness from all the rulers and threw it down upon this earth. He swept it another time from the earth and confined it in the vessel that surrounds all the worlds, and they call it the wall of great fire.

**¶11** Now, those three garments, through which he established these three things, he did not peel them off all at once; but he stripped them off one by one at the appropriate time [when he had cast them] down to the earth [and set each one in its vessel.]

**¶12** In this way the universe of the [lower world] his vessels [was guarded through] these vessels like [fetters and measures.]

**¶13** [*] Afterwards, they were swept from the earth and pushed outside to the edges of the worlds and to the {extremity}.

**¶14** The first deed is this: So that evil-doing does not increase in the universe, neither in the creations nor in the trees [because]tself up in three things.

**¶15** The [The first is lust.]

**¶16** The second is error [in the rulers above.] Indeed, about this I have spoken to you as I [saying,] For, if it were so, if [the vessel of water] had not been swept down from above, but rather the waters existing up till now in the rulers above [the] torrents that would flow [from above;] and the droplets that would [drip] down, so lust would have sprouted [up]n the trees and in the fruits as well as all flesh [in] down [till]oday [and] you could not find a single person [holy,] in the [whole world;] a saint does [if] is master of [them.]

**¶17** Not only this, but if the vessel of darkness had not been swept out and thrown down to the earth, nor swept from the earth and cast outside the region of the worlds into this pit; again the drops of darkness, which will drip down from it, and the torrents that will continue to flow down from above to the earth and assume the trees and all flesh, would have multiplied, and the forgetfulness that is in a thousand people today you would have found in just one person. There is not one among mankind [who can keep] him [that in] the thought and the knowledge of the [God.]

**¶18** Furthermore, if the fire were in the rulers of the worlds of the heavens until today, and had not been swept out and cast down to the earth, and swept from the earth and shut up inside the vessel of fire that surrounds the worlds; [the fury of] this fire [would pour down] above through the torrent [and would surge up] below;

**¶19** ⟨p.110⟩
and the dark fire would have multiplied in the tree and in fruit and in all flesh. The power of the fire that is now in a thousand people, you would have found it occurring in a single person today.

**¶20** Behold, I have taught you about this single evil that was purified by three archetypes [three seals.]

**¶21** The second deed: Al[l] the heavens [were purified] of the sediment and the pollution [that remained in them,] so that [the Light] surpasses in them [and not the darkness.]

**¶22** [The third]: The heavens and the firmaments [b]urden [and the firmaments were] relieved from the weight-carriers [and the yokes,] and it ceased being heavy. This rich one [that were set over them from] above.

**¶23** The fourth: That they might purify and cleanse their dwelling places [The fourth:]

**¶24** The fifth: Because the greatness and the richness that dwells in these ships, as it [is gathered there,] you might find them living in these ships, purified and holy.

**¶25** The sixth: That you might find the mixed, incomplete, and unconfined temples above at rest and purified; since rest will reach them and pass through them and purify them.

**¶26** The seventh: [The seventh: That the] nature alone of the vessels, which will advance and extend to their places and sites [shall not pass beyond] in [their boundaries.]

**¶27** The eighth: That great enclosures and walls would be made, and confine everything.

**¶29** The tenth deed: That they would become releasers and purifiers of everything at the end of the universe like the fire [that] burns [all filth.]

**¶30** The eleventh deed: [The eleventh: That they] will make a crossing [for the souls,] and the entire ruling-power [of the darkness be cast]

**¶31** ⟨p.111⟩
to the outer land, for they were not envious of this [as] a man who is richer than them; because the aeons of light are outside [the boundary of the worlds,] the place [is] of peace, the place of honour and grace, the place where the joy of the householders who dwell in it shall never pass away nor recede.

**¶32** The twelfth: That you might find all the deeds of loss hidden from the aeons of light, because of the wall of fire that surrounds the entire building; and these unclean things are displayed (only) in its [outermost border.]

**¶33** [*] There was a need for these twelve great deeds because the vessels that were discharged and swept out from the firmaments to the earth, and were also swept from the earth and to the edges of the worlds and to the {extremity}. because of this, they are firmly set thus in these fetters, in the way that I have told you.

**Changes:** Implemented the critic’s two excisions only. (1) ¶13: excised the seam sentence introducing the enumerative addendum (“The use of the matter, and the management… twelve great deeds”), per Finding 6 scope; retained the subsequent connective time-sentence. (2) ¶33: excised the corrupt watcher-appointment clause (“…the three words of life watch over them… were appointed… at the last time”), per Finding 9 scope; minimally adjusted the remaining sentence for grammatical continuity while keeping the original connectives and sense.

**Spiritual narrative:** Know, beloved, that when the corrupted receptacles were forced open and emptied, the separation was accomplished through a violent upheaval in the lowest degree; yet what was expelled was taken out from within the corrupted powers themselves.

It is as with a person whose spiritual form has been seized by a foul corruption: many disordered evils and falsities spread through the outward life, and even more poisonous persuasions infest the inmost faculties. That person is restored by wisdom acting with skill: one influx is given that brings the hidden corruption up into open manifestation, another influx is given that drives what must be cast out down and away into the lowest level, and yet another influx is given that heals the remaining disorders and settles the life into quiet order.

By such intelligent government, living operations remove evils in this way and dissolve compulsions of deceptive persuasion; the restoring powers work even by a gentle perception of what is salutary. What was concealed is uncovered; the rooted falsities are finally torn out when they have been brought into outer visibility above and below through an inwardly received healing influence.

So it is with the three chief corruptions concealed within the interior life of the dominant falsifying powers in higher states. The Divine saving operation is present as wisdom itself, and it applies a threefold mode of healing by assuming accommodated outer forms for reception: clarifying discernment, living truth, and purifying love. By these, the lower realm was arranged into order. Through this accommodated manifestation the dominant powers were thrown into hardness, trembling, fear, and derangement, and by that exposure the three corruptions were expelled from them: falsified truth, active denial, and perverted love.

The state of these corruptions is like nascent spiritual life enclosed within successive coverings: first an inmost support of life, then a fixed framework of principles, then connective powers, then the general substance of acquired life, then channels of communication, then circulating vitality, and finally an outermost boundary that contains and protects. Beyond these are further encompassing supports from the surrounding matrix that carries what is forming. In all these ultimates together the living essence is interwoven and held.

When the foul pressure of infernal operation that induces the anguish of emergence makes what is within ready to be revealed, the restraints yield in ordered fashion, and the new life comes forth by an opening made perceptible, without injury to its form. As nascent life thus opens what contained it and is brought forth, so it was with those three corruptions: the operations that opened their containment and shook them out into manifestation were the same three living accommodated forms assumed by the Divine saving operation.

By the manifestation of living truth, the deadly falsification was drawn out from the dominant powers—those poisonous persuasions with which their communications and inner affections had been filled.

By the manifestation of clarifying discernment, the whole destructive obscurity and defilement was swept away and erased, driven down into the lowest natural degree; afterwards it was gathered from that level and confined in a fixed general boundary of denial and obscurity that encompasses the external order.

By the manifestation of living love, through its brightness and power, the infernal ardor of perverted love was expelled from the dominant powers and cast down into the lowest degree; then it was gathered again from that level and confined in an encompassing ultimate restraint around all states, experienced as a severe boundary of consuming lust.

These three accommodated forms, by which the separations were effected, were not withdrawn all at once; they were removed successively in due order and at the fitting time, as each lower arrangement was secured in its place.

Thus the organized totality of the lower realm was preserved, and the expelled corruptions were held in fixed limits like bonds and measures.

The governance of these expelled corruptions—driven out from higher states into the lowest degree, and afterwards gathered from the lowest and thrust outward to the extremity—produced a fullness of great uses.

The first use was that evil-doing might not increase without measure in the whole system, neither in formed communities nor in the perceptions and works that grow within them, because the tendency to evil had set itself up in a triad of corrupt forces.

The first of these is the lust itself.

The second is pervasive error among the dominant powers: if falsified truth had not been cast down, but the falsities in those powers had remained, continual streams of persuasive falsity would have flowed downward and subtle insinuations would have descended, and lust would have sprung up in perverted perceptions, in the works that proceed from them, and in every affection of embodied life. In such a state scarcely any holiness could be preserved in anyone in the whole order, nor could anyone become master over these assaults.

And further: if active denial and obscurity had not been expelled and first driven into the lowest degree and then thrust outside the ordered region into its fixed abyss, continual insinuations of darkness would have descended and streams of denial would have kept flowing down into the lowest life, seizing perverted perceptions and every embodied affection. Forgetfulness of the Divine would have multiplied so greatly that what is now scattered among many would be found concentrated in one, and scarcely anyone would retain the thought and knowledge of the Divine.

Moreover: if perverted love had remained in the dominant powers of the higher states until now, and had not been expelled, cast down, and then shut up within the encompassing restraint that surrounds all ordered states, its fury would have poured down from above and surged up from below.

Then the infernal ardor of that love would have multiplied in perverted perception, in the works born of it, and in every embodied affection, so that the intensity now distributed among many would rage in a single person.

See, you have been taught about this single compound evil that was purified by a triad of governing confirmations.

The second use was that the higher states were cleansed of remaining dregs and defilements, so that what is pure might prevail there.

The third use was that the intermediate ordered structures joining higher and lower were relieved from oppressive burdens and enslaving weights, so that their function ceased to be heavy.

The fourth use was that the dwellings of higher life might be purified and cleansed.

The fifth use was that the abundance and richness of capacities gathered into the serving forms of conveyance might be found living there in a purified and holy condition.

The sixth use was that the mixed, incomplete, and unbounded sanctuaries in higher states might come into rest and be purified, for peace would reach them, pass through them, and cleanse them.

The seventh use was that the restrained corruptions, by their own nature pressing toward their allotted places, should not pass beyond the fixed limits set for them.

The eighth use was that strong encompassing boundaries might be established, and everything be held in restraint.

The tenth use was that at the completion of the age they would become means of release and purification for all things that must be separated, by a final consuming removal of what is unclean.

The eleventh use was that a passage would be made for the transfer of souls, and the entire dominion of darkness would be driven outward into the ultimate boundary; for the eternities of light are beyond the lower boundary, in peace, honor, grace, and abiding joy.

The twelfth use was that the works of ruin might be hidden from the eternities of light because of the encompassing ultimate restraint around the whole structure, and that the unclean things appear only in the outermost allotted domain.

These twelve uses were necessary because the expelled corruptions—driven down from higher ordering truths into the lowest degree, and then thrust outward to the extremity—had to be held under vigilant governance. Therefore living truths from the Divine were appointed over them in great power to watch over them to the last time, and for this cause they are fixed firmly in restraints, as in bonds, according to the order declared.

---

## Chapter 43: Concerning the Vessels.

**¶2** As for what you have asked me about the vessels, you must understand this, that when the Father of Life was about to discharge these three vessels he poured the three upon this earth, one by one at its (appointed) hour and season. He discharged them and cast them down to the earth.

**¶3** Then he bailed and scooped them from this earth in these three garments, they of wind and of water and of fire, which swathed his body. He poured and made them flow into the three pits [which]hich he had [es]tablished during the construc[tio]n of the worlds [and when he had shut them in,] they [yet] something remained behind on the face of the earth from these three vessels, so that you can find their mark and type displayed in the universe.

**¶4** The element that is left in water is the pungency and the salinity of this salty inner sea that surrounds the worlds.

**¶5** And the remnant that is left from the darkness is this black mountain that exists in the surroundings of the sunrise. For when the sun would shine over the world no sunlight is visible there, because the darkness that gushes from it manifests above it.

**¶6** And the remnant that remains behind from the fire, upon the earth, are all the mountains and islands from where fumes of fire gush up and are visible. For these things are the remnant of the great vessel, the vestige that was conjoined with this fire that comes up from the earth; together with the mountains and hills [and valleys, and barren] places where no fruit sprouts on the trees [and stony] greater than that place from the limb and [the root of] the trees shall not bear fruit in that place.

**¶8** Happen you know this! When the Father of Life discharged these vessels from above upon the earth, he poured them out for this reason: so that also the water and the darkness and the fire that exist in these three earths would be drawn and ascend towards they that had come down from above, and the earths would be purified and cleansed. Due to this he poured these vessels down to the earth, of water and of darkness and of fire, so that also the water and darkness and fire that exist in the earths would be drawn up towards they that had descended from above.

**¶9** Now, first of all, he discharged the water, the waste, the poison of the rulers who are above. He cleansed the firmaments of the [water of darkness] he poured down the dark waters. [Just as the] waters fell from heaven to earth, so also the waters below in the earths were drawn and came up from below towards the waters that had come from above to the earths. The waters [were] purified of the dark poison that exists in them. Then he swept them outside and made them a great vessel and a wall of sea that surrounds the universe, and on which the ships sail.

**¶10** After the water, he discharged the darkness. He purified the firmaments and dried from them the darkness with which they are weighed down. He discharged it down to the earth. Just as he had purified the firmaments, so also he purified the earths. The darkness that is in the earths was drawn up towards the darkness that had been discharged from above. He swept them both out and shut them in that [vessel] of darkness [beneath] the water.

**¶11** And he discharged the fire from heaven to earth, [so that] that time again the fire that was shut [in] the earth below was drawn and came up towards its kind. Fire was added to fire. Afterwards, he swept it out and cast it into the outer underworld, which [he had constructed for] them outside the universe.

**¶12** In the same way that he purified the firmaments above as a place for the light-givers to journey [and for the luminaries to go and come,] so also he purified the earths below and swept from them the fire and the darkness and the water [that were mixed in them.] He has purified them for the ascent of these three powers, they of the wind and the water and the fire, which ascend before the Porter and traverse the earths.

> **Textual criticism:**
> - ¶2 [none]: Clean correspondential setup: three “vessels” are taught as functional mechanisms in a purification process (descent → extraction). No jurisdictional cataloging, no named administrators. The spiritual
> - ¶3 [none]: Mostly clean correspondential teaching with minor damage/compression. The ‘three garments’ and the clause about something remaining are functional (how the descent is mediated; why marks remain). The 
> - ¶4-¶6 [none]: Clean correspondential ‘residues’ section: each remnant is justified by observable function/behavior (salinity surrounds; darkness blocks light; volcanic fire and sterility). No administrative overlay
> - ¶8-¶11 [none]: Clean, tightly structured correspondential core: three-cycle purification narrative (water → darkness → fire) is entirely process-based (like draws like; separation; confinement). The repeated ‘Just a
> - ¶12 [annotate]: Mostly correspondential conclusion with one localized mixed-voice term. The ‘Porter’ functions as a regulator in the ascent mechanism, but the title is introduced without internal definition and can f

**Changes:** No text was excised or altered. The only targeted item in the findings was Finding 5 (¶12) with a recommendation to annotate the clause “which ascend before the Porter”; per rule, annotation does not modify the chapter text.

**Spiritual narrative:** Concerning the forms that receive and contain spiritual things, understand this: when the Divine Love was about to bring into effect a full progression of the three discrete degrees, it sent them down into the lowest degree in ordered sequence, each according to its appointed state, and allowed them to descend into the outermost plane.

Then the Divine gathered those descending degrees out of the outermost plane by investing them with outward modes suited to reception and action, so they could be present there without being harmed and could act upon what was there. It directed their operation into the three ultimate repositories prepared during the ordering of the whole heaven-and-world structure; yet something of that first contact remained in the outermost plane, so that traces of those operations still appear everywhere as fixed signatures within the total arrangement.

What remains from the operation of truth in ultimates is a sharp and resistant quality within the most external knowledges that encompass lower things. What remains from the operation that dealt with falsity is a stubborn and obscuring state at the boundary of enlightenment, where attempted illumination finds no reception because denial continually rises and throws shade over what would otherwise be made clear. What remains from the operation that dealt with disordered love is the recurring eruption, in many outermost states, of heated cravings that break out into open violence, and from this come barren states of life in which no good work proceeds from perception and no living result can be brought forth.

Know, then, that the Divine sent these degrees down into the outermost plane for this purpose: that the impure things already present in the lower levels might be drawn out and lifted toward what had descended, so that the lower levels could be purified. This descent was ordered so that what was corrupt in truth, what was false in understanding, and what was disordered in love within the natural degree might be extracted, raised toward its like, and separated from what could be cleansed.

First, the Divine dealt with truth in its ultimate forms, where it had become waste and poison through domination by higher falsities. It cleared the intermediate levels of that corruption and permitted the falsified truths to descend into the outermost plane. As the falsified truths were brought down from above into the natural, the falsified truths hidden in the natural were stirred and rose up toward what had descended, so that the truths in ultimates could be purged of the dark contamination mixed into them. Then the Divine expelled that contamination and fixed it as a general enclosing boundary of merely external knowing around the whole outer domain, serving as a defensive limit and as a surface upon which lower reasoning can move.

After this it dealt with falsity itself. It cleared the intermediate levels and removed from them the oppressive obscurity by which they were burdened, and allowed that obscurity to descend into the natural plane. As those levels were purified, so the lower levels were purified: the falsity in the natural was drawn up toward the falsity that had been let down. The Divine expelled them both and confined them together into a fixed state of denial set beneath and apart from what can still serve as an enclosing boundary of external knowing.

Then it dealt with disordered love. It permitted the burning impulse to descend into the natural plane, and at that same time the heated craving shut up in the lowest level was drawn out and rose toward its own kind; disordered love was added to disordered love. Afterward the Divine expelled it and cast it into the most external hell, prepared as a place outside the ordered form of the universe for such loves to dwell in separation.

In the same way that the Divine purified the intermediate levels as a region fit for the orderly movement of illuminating truths, so it purified the lower levels and swept out from them corrupted truth, falsity, and disordered love. It purified them with a view to the ascent of these three operative powers in their orderly forms, so that they may rise before the one who admits and regulates influx and pass through the lower levels in right order.

---

## Chapter 44: Concerning the Sea Giant

**¶2** After the Living Spirit constructed things and the worlds, he came up and set himself upon this earth. He gave a sign to the King of Glory, who turned the three wheels and caused the wheels that are found [in their place] up above all the mountains; he spread forth the wind to them, he breathed on them, he stirred them up, he washed the earth with the waters. And the waste and the accretion of the three vessels, they of water and of Darkness and of fire, which had been discharged upon the earth, he gathered them and deposited them in the sea that is within the walls and the vessels; because of this sea waters are salty. For they have received salt and bitterness from the washing out and cleansing of the three vessels.

**¶3** When the Father of Life came, rising in his ascent to the heights, he discharged the wheel of the stars; he washed it out and loosened all the sediment that was in it. He cast it down to this great inner sea like [a stone] of the mountain. And that waste, the sediment of the washing out of the wheel, he cast it down and mixed it in with the sea waters. Its own thought collected it together; and the thought gathered the waste together in this way. It made it fast and fixed it [in the depths]

**¶4** [*] The sea giant had stamped upon him the seal of the seconds and the hours, the seal of the days and months and years, the impression of the stars and the signs of the zodiac [and the courses of the seven planets.] He came into existence from them and was discharged from their power and cast into the sea. Because he is the residue of them all. Consequently, each star that will shine, and each sign of the zodiac that will turn: of one he shall be its inducement, and of another its confirmation.

**¶5** He was discharged into the sea like this, the way a cloud might discharge rainwater into the midst of the sea. They compelled him totally through the action of the power and the helmsmanship of the angels. He was swept down to the mill, the place where he was constructed in the setting up of the universe; and the sea also conceived him [and took hold of him] for its part [in the work of his shaping;] it being his chief sculptor and chief craftsman. It sculpted him in a likeness, he being changed more than the other residue of the sea bodies.

**¶6** This matter is comparable with a craftsman who might take the stamped impressions of many coins and cast them on the fire, and mould them [to a single] image. These thousands of impressions and thousands of shapes [are perfected] and make a single body [and become one form.]

**¶7** This is also the case with the sea giant, who too was moulded and sculpted by the power of the lust inside him from many doctrines and counsels that belong to the residue of the wheel of the sphere.

**¶8** The archetype of the giant was constructed for three things.

**¶9** The first: That the sphere would be relieved and purified of the burden present in it. In the same way that the ten firmaments were purified and relieved of the burden of the three vessels; so was it also done to the sphere, that it too might be relieved and purified of the burden and the waste inside it.

**¶10** [*] The second: That he might add a {fetter} and a prison to the wheel and the signs of the zodiac that are bound to it, so that when they wish to go up above, you can find the sea giant dragging them back as by an iron {fetter}, and they are unable to go up; and when the powers of the wheel would take a decision on a guileful thing, and it enters their mind to come down to the earth, they are not able to descend because of this stake that is nailed to the wheel upon the {} of the firmaments.

**¶11** The third thing: That the wind and the water and the fire that gush from the circuit of the sea, and the rivers that flow upon the face of the earth discharging into the sea, and the rainwaters that come to ground, and the waste and rebirth that come down to it; that sea will be a hostel and gathering place for all of these.

**¶12** As for all the power and the life that comes down to the sea, and in the breath that the sea giant will breathe and draw it into him that he might disturb the sea [there is] fullness and a [interaction] becomes between his movement [that will] purify; but the sea [does not cease.]

> **Textual criticism:**
> - ¶2 [none]: Clean correspondential substrate: the teaching proceeds by function (washing, gathering, salting) and the spiritual reading arises smoothly from the natural operations described.
> - ¶3 [none]: Clean correspondential continuation: casting down ‘sediment’ and its consolidation is functional and narratively continuous with ¶2.
> - ¶4 [excise]: Primarily correspondential but with a systematizing-lexical accretion that increases density and blurs function: the piling up of abstract terms (‘aspects… doctrines… counsels’) reads like a schematic
> - ¶5-¶7 [none]: Clean correspondential exposition with a stable illustrative analogy (coin-impressions → single image; lust as formative force). The narrative is continuous (descent → formation → analogy → applicatio
> - ¶8-¶11 [none]: Clean correspondential ‘three uses’ structure: purposes are stated as functional roles (purge burden; restrain motion; serve as receptacle). This matches substrate-style teleology by use/function, not
> - ¶10 [excise]: Minor seam/patchwork inside an otherwise coherent paragraph: two restraint mechanisms are spliced with abrupt deictics (“This is one. However, this other one…”) causing a perceptible internal jolt. Li
> - ¶12 [none]: Genuine difficulty from damage/fragmentation at the close, not an administrative intrusion. The sentence breaks off (“but the sea [does not cease.]”) leaving the stated effect unresolved.

**Changes:** Implemented excisions per the critic’s recommendations: (1) In ¶4, removed the schematic additive string “The images and the seals and aspects and doctrines and counsels of [the wheel of the stars] were sealed upon the body of that giant,” as identified in Finding 3, while preserving the functional seal/impression framework and the inducement/confirmation conclusion; minimal grammar smoothing followed the removal. (2) In ¶10, excised the stitched addendum “This is one. However, this other one, because of its sleepless watchers that are set over it, it is turned all the time.” as specified in Finding 6. All other paragraphs unchanged (Findings 1, 2, 4, 5, 7: none/annotate-only).

**Spiritual narrative:** After the Divine life had completed the ordering of all things and their subordinate formations, it turned itself toward the outermost degree and established itself there. It gave an impulse to the Divine governance of truth, and the full arrangement of successive states was set in motion and elevated into a higher order. Then an encompassing influx of truth was extended into those ordered states; life was breathed into them; their activities were awakened; and the outermost degree was cleansed by that influx.

Then the impure remainder and hardened encrustation from a complete mixture of corrupt influences—those arising from falsified understanding, from active denial of good, and from perverted love—which had flowed down into the outermost degree, were gathered up and confined within the common repository of external knowledges, held inside fixed bounds and containing forms. Because that repository received what was harsh and acrid from the cleansing of those mixed corruptions, the knowledges there became bitter and resistant to spiritual enjoyment.

When Divine life rose into higher states, it released the ordered progression of cognitions that governs changes and seasons of state. It cleansed that progression, loosened all thickened falsity within it, and cast the loosened remainder down from an elevated state into that same common repository of externals. The remainder from that cleansing was mingled into the general knowledges. A dominant intention within that repository collected what was scattered; and that intention bound and fixed the gathered residue so it became stable in the lowest degree.

Upon this fixed composite, the measurements of successive states were impressed: lesser and greater changes, extended cycles, and the determinations of cognition with their ordered varieties. It arose from those determinations, was removed from their active government, and was confined within the general external repository. Upon it were impressed the appearances, settled patterns, persuasive teachings, deliberations, and governing aims that remained from that former progression, because it was the condensed remainder of them all. Therefore, whenever any cognition is kindled into prominence or any ordered variety turns into effect, this composite serves, for one, as an incitement and, for another, as a confirmation.

It was brought down into the general repository as a common inflow pours into what is general and is received there. It was compelled into that state entirely by the operation and governance of ministering powers. It was carried into the place of reduction and formation where the ordered whole was established in its ultimate working; and the general repository of externals also received it and cooperated in shaping it, being the principal agent in giving it determinate form. It was formed into a representative structure, and it underwent more alteration than the other residual composites stored in that general external plane.

This process is like the way innumerable distinct determinations from many sources can be gathered into one: their separate markings are dissolved, and the multitude of prior forms is brought into a single coherent whole.

So it was with this fixed composite within externals: it too was shaped and set by the force of inward craving within it, assembled from many persuasive teachings and deliberations drawn from the remaining elements of that ordered progression of cognitions.

The governing pattern for producing this composite was for three uses.

First, that the progression of cognitions might be relieved and cleansed of the oppressive remainder within it. As the full outer arrangements had been purified and lightened from the burden of the mixed corruptions, so the progression itself was also relieved and cleansed of the burden and residue held inside it.

Second, that there might be added a restraint and confinement upon that progression and its ordered varieties bound to it, so that when they strive to rise beyond their proper degree, the fixed composite within external knowledges drags them back by a hard constraint and they cannot ascend. And when the powers of that progression incline toward deceit and intend to flow down into the outermost degree, they cannot descend, because a fixed arrest is set at the boundary of the ultimate arrangements. This is one restraint; yet another restraint, by constant supervision from vigilant guardings set over it, keeps it compelled into continual regulated motion.

Third, that all general influxes of truth, together with the diverse movements of understanding and love that surge within the circuit of externals, and the streams of thought that traverse the outermost degree and return into what is general, and the descending influxes that terminate in ultimates, and the residues and renewals that come down with them—might all have in that general repository a common lodging and place of collection.

And as to all force and vitality that descends into that general repository, and as to the inbreathing by which this fixed composite draws that vitality into itself so as to agitate what is general: from its motion there arises a fullness and interaction that tends toward purification; yet the continuation is unresolved here, as though the account breaks and does not fully disclose the outcome.

---

## Chapter 45: Concerning the Vessels

**¶1** Understand also this other (matter): Before these vessels were discharged and swept down from heaven, first three ditches were fashioned and constructed for them, in which they would be bound and fettered.

**¶2** Before the sea giant was discharged from the sphere, the Father of Life constructed a place for [him in]n this great sea that is within the walls, whereon the ships sail, and where the fullness shall [be kept apart]

**¶3** Also, before the [ruler of the darkness] was displayed to the worlds [and sank to] the earth [and its thought]ell to the sea and its thought shaped it, the Father of Life constructed a place for it in [the great ditch] below the four mountains, above the mountains of the heavens above [and he dug] that [opened] a great plain it digs in Matter in the midst of the worlds. When the Adamant of Light was sent to it, he hastened after it to that place, smote it and threw it between mountains in the place made ready for it [and its body] is set fast upon it till the end of the universe.

**¶4** Before the waste and dirt of the darkness were swept out from creation, gehennas were constructed in which the waste might be covered till the dissolution of the universe.

**¶5** [*] Before the rebels rose up, they whose corps rebelled in the ruling-power above and below; for them were constructed in the great outer sea seven hostels, places whence they are thrown into the depths and the darkness [according to their measure]

**¶6** Also, before the abortions [fell down] to the earth, there was set apart [a pen]: they are gathered in and dwell therein.

**¶7** Again, before the watchers rebelled and came down from heaven, a prison was fashioned and constructed for them in the depths of the earth, below the mountains.

**¶8** [*] Before the children of the giants were born, they who had [no] knowledge of righteousness in them nor divinity, were assigned and constructed for them wherein the children of [the giants would] live; they who would come to beget from each other, they who shall spend ten hundred years alive.
