(Finding 8, ¶8) Removed the quantified expansion “thirty-six cities were assigned and constructed for them” and “they who shall spend ten hundred years alive”; made the minimal grammatical bridge to keep the claim of prepared habitation coherent.

**Spiritual narrative:** Understand also this further order: before lower receptacles were emptied of what they had taken in from higher degrees and were cast down into the natural degree, fixed states of restraint were first brought into being, so that what fell could be held and prevented from acting beyond bounds.

Before a vast ruling falsity was expelled from its former range, the Divine source of life provided for it a determined containment within the general field of external knowledges, so that it might be kept within limits under outward order, and so that the fullness of such outward knowledge could serve separation and not confusion.

Also, before a further obscured ruling power was made manifest within lower domains after it had sunk into the lowest degree and its own fantasy formed its condition, the Divine source of life set a particular confinement for it in the lowest region beneath complete natural boundaries, yet still under higher limits that stand above the natural. An emptied expanse was opened within the midst of the lower worlds so that this power might be isolated in mere materiality. When steadfast Divine truth was sent toward it, that truth immediately pursued it into the prepared confinement, struck down its force, and fixed it between opposed limiting states, held there by established order and bound fast until the consummation of the age.

Before the refuse of active falsity and the foul remains of evil were driven out from the forms of the created order, hellish restraints were made in which such refuse could be covered over and kept from contact with what is being reformed until the final unbinding of the whole process.

Before the perverse rose up—those whose interiors revolted against governing good and truth in higher and lower degrees—together with domineering loves that desired to rule amid obscured understanding, deceptive appearances, unstable persuasions, falsified reasonings, and restless agitations: for these, complete states of lodging were established in the most external general knowledge, as fixed stations from which they are cast down into deeper obscurity and denial according to order, so that their outbreaks might be regulated.

Also, before malformed products of corrupt desire and thought descended into the natural degree, a separated enclosure was prepared for them; they are gathered into it and remain there.

Again, before those entrusted with oversight of truths revolted and let themselves down from a higher state, a confinement was made and established for them in the lowest region of the natural beneath the limiting truths there, so that their misuse of what they had known might be restrained.

Before derivative falsities from those great perversions came forth—those in whom there was no perception of what is just nor any acknowledgment of the Divine—many ordered communities of life were allotted and established for them. There such derivative states would dwell; there they would generate like from like by mutual communication, and they would persist through a full conjunction of time in that state.

## Chapter 46: Concerning the Ambassador.

**¶2** Know that before night and day were differentiated in the universe, the Third [Am]bassador was summoned. And afterwards, he was sent to this void and outer waste that he might purify the light, and separate night and day from each other.

**¶3** ⟨p.117⟩ Before the light was released and freed and ascended through this zone, the Father of Life made beautiful for it the ships of light. He constructed them so that it might be purified in them, and be sculpted and fashioned in good order.

**¶4** [Bef]ore Sin had multiplied and had made a kingdom in the flesh, before the acquitted and the condemn[ed] were set apart from each other, the Judge [w]as sent forth and revealed. His throne was created and placed [i]n the living atmosphere, so that he would be a just Judge, so that there the righteous and the sinners would be set apart and tested and separated from each other.

**¶6** [*] Again, [befo]re mankind had been engendered, some were named ['rich' and] others 'poor' on earth [and the fate was set] upon them.

**¶7** Again, before the universe is dissolved, [and the] entire earth, the destroyers were appointed and instructed for its dissolution.

**¶8** And before the ne[w] aeon was [built and] made be[auti]ful and marked out, [the] Great Builder [w]as summoned, the first architect and the angels who are with him, who would build and make beautiful the new aeon.

No other paragraphs were changed per Findings 1–3 and 5–6 (recommendation: none).

**Spiritual narrative:** Before obscurity and illumination were distinguished within the whole created order, a sufficient mediating power was called forth. Afterward that mediating power was directed into the outermost emptiness and disorder, so that wisdom might be purified there and so that obscurity and illumination might be separated from each other.

Before wisdom was loosed from restraint and rose through that lowest region, the Divine source of living good prepared for it ordered receptive forms in the inner degree. These were established so that wisdom might be cleansed within them and brought into determinate form and harmonious arrangement.

Before evil had multiplied into dominion in the outer human life, and before those in good and those in evil were distinguished from one another, the principle of just discernment was sent forth and made manifest. The seat of that discernment was fixed within a living spiritual medium, so that judgment might proceed in justice, and so that the good and the evil might be examined, proved, and separated according to what they are.

Again, before the human race came into actual existence, states were already allotted, and some were foreseen as abounding in spiritual resources while others were foreseen as lacking them in the outer degree. Before the end of their worldly life, those who would remain in spiritual death were marked out by prevailing external patterns that seem to order life and by persuasive appearances that govern in the natural mind. Such determinants were assigned to them, and within them their first formations were established. Their inmost tendency was entangled with these persuasions, and they were driven by them toward an equalizing judgment according to their works and their evils.

Again, before the present ordered state is brought to its end, the forces that serve dissolution are designated and instructed for that final loosening.

And before a renewed state could be established, perfected, and set in distinct form, the governing power of formation was summoned, together with the primary ordering intelligence and the ministering powers that work with it, to build and bring that new state into beauty and order.

## Chapter 47: Concerning the Four great Things

**¶1** The wheel of the stars, which is displayed to you, is a great mighty thing! Also, it is one among the four great things; since the whole ruling-power is assigned to four parts.

**¶2** The first part is the powers who occur in the ten firmaments that exist above the wheel, and the ruling-power that is bound in them, as it is imprisoned within them in the heavens that are above.

**¶3** The second part is the eight earths that are below; the four that are composite, and the four places of darkness. The second part extends from this earth, upon which mankind walks, towards the underside.

**¶4** The third part is the walls and the vessels that surround the worlds. They are the four mountains and the three vessels; with what is in them, and the ruling-power that is trapped within them, and the powers who are masters over them.

**¶5** The fourth part is the sphere of the stars that is appointed with great diligence, as it is ordered in a [fore]any [orderings]; that of the Father of Life, [and that of the Mother of Life] the living.

**¶6** ⟨p.119⟩ Its [ordinance] very [ordered] with diligence, because of the great and cruel powers, and the titanic figures, [who are] in it; they being suspended from it. So, all the first-born and the leaders of all the rulers, they who were existing in the three parts, these were garnered. They were collected together, seized, bound and assigned on this wheel. Each was apportioned by these three parts. They were distributed, entwined, and weighted against their companions of the same size.

**¶7** Still, for its part, the wheel is equal to them in its strength; the powers of the enmity who are bound on it. Indeed, they were suspended on the circling wheel because of their cruelty; so that, due to the wind and the motion in which they go round, they comprehend not the place wherein they are set. For if they had been bound in the heavens, the heavens would not have had the strength to support them. Again, if they had been assigned on the earths, on the fixtures that are below, they also would not have had the strength to support them. Rather, they were suspended from the wheel of the circling stars; so that the power and the light that should come up from below can be received, and given to the heavenly ones above them. Conversely, whatever will fall from above, whether cold or hot, the wheel of the sphere will also take it and cast it out upon the entire earth, upon all the wood.

**¶8** Behold, the ordinance of the wheel is in this form. For the powers who are entwined on the wheel are like this: a king mastering his kingdom by this mighty legionary force of all the leaders of the kingdom; and with all the armour of the soldiery that is collected together for him; and also with the mass of the property of the kingdom; and the beautiful lustful aspect of the women who are collected together for him; together with [all the] property [besides] and those cattle [also] that have been distributed about among his retinue. Yet, if you ask about that king, he is matched with all his retinue. His retinue, for its part, is matched against his entire kingdom. Judgement shall come forth from him, vindication and condemnation, to his entire kingdom; by his wisdom, skill and strength.

**¶9** [*] Now, just as for this king, who is matched against the measure of his entire kingdom by his wisdom, skill and strength; so also is it for the powers who are bound on the sphere as compared to the ruling-power, and the powers who exist in the entire zone. Like the king towards his retinue; so too is it that this totality is gathered to the sphere. As all the cities are gathered to the king, again they gather to the leaders of the universe. All that is done in the universe above and below: the battles and the disorders and the captivity and the hunger and the lust and the property; so shall they increase and diminish through these leaders. They are the motivaters of the whole created order; the totality being gathered in to them the way that I have told you. I have opened your eyes!

**Spiritual narrative:** The ordered turning of general knowledges displayed in the outer mind is a great and mighty arrangement. It is one of four comprehensive arrangements, for the whole government of states in the created mind is distinguished into four universal domains.

The first domain is the operative forces active in the higher degrees, together with the ruling capability confined within those interiors and held as in restraint in the heights above the outward turning.

The second domain is the lower degrees beneath: the mixed and compounded planes of life, and also the obscurities where truth is absent and falsity dominates. This domain extends from the level in which the human being lives and acts in outward life down toward the lowest plane.

The third domain is the ultimate boundaries and containing forms that encompass the distinct levels of one’s spiritual world: elevated states and the full series of discrete containments that hold what is within them, together with the ruling capability restrained inside those containments and the subordinate forces that preside there.

The fourth domain is the ordered complex of general knowledges in externals, set in meticulous arrangement as a patterned whole of living love and living wisdom.

This external complex is held in exact order because great and cruel forces of perverted love and monstrous persuasive falsities are fastened within it and dependent upon it. Therefore the first outbirths and chief beginnings of all dominant evils—those that had been active through the three prior domains—were gathered together, seized, restrained, and fixed into this outward turning. Each was allotted its place according to the domain from which it arose. They were arranged in interwoven connection and made to balance against others of like measure.

This outward turning is equal to them in strength because it bears the restrained forces of enmity fixed within it. They were fastened into ceaseless change because of their cruelty, so that through restless instability they do not perceive the state in which they have been set. If they had been restrained in higher interiors, those interiors could not have borne such weight; and if they had been fixed in the lowest planes, those planes likewise could not have borne them. Instead they were suspended in the circling of outward knowledges so that power and illumination rising from lower uses might be received and communicated to the higher interiors above. Conversely, whatever influx descends from above—whether chilling denial or burning craving—this outward complex also receives it and disperses it through the whole lower life into every form of perception.

The ordering of this outward turning is therefore of this nature. The forces interwoven within it are like a governing love ruling its whole domain by means of a mighty host of leading intentions, together with all defensive falsities and persuasive appearances gathered to support it, and with the mass of claimed possessions of knowledge and delight, and the pleasing allurements of cupidities drawn together to serve it, along with other acquisitions and the scattered appetites that attend its train. Yet the governing love is equal in measure to all that serves it, and what serves it is in turn equal in effect to the whole domain it rules. From it proceed decision and assessment—both acquittal and condemnation—through the entire mind, according to the quality of its understanding, its practiced capacity, and its prevailing force.

As it is with such a governing love, so it is with the restrained forces fixed in the outward complex when compared with the ruling capability and with the forces present throughout the whole expanse of the created order. As the governing love stands to its attendants, so the whole of this outer arrangement gathers into dominant principles: into a full process and into a complete organization of truths in the natural degree. As all specific teachings and practices gather to the ruling love, so they gather to the chief determinants of the universal state. Everything done in the whole mind, above and below—conflicts, confusions, captivities, deprivation of truth, disordered craving, and the possession of knowledges—rises and falls according to these determinants. They are the movers of the entire created order, the whole being gathered into them; thus the understanding is opened.

---

## Chapter 48: Concerning the Conduits.

**¶1** There are three conduits that exist through the entire zone, from the abyss to its heights.

**¶2** The first conduit is the root of all the powers above, they that exist in all the heavens. They are cast off and fettered on the earths below; because the earth below is the ‘stripping off’, the garment and the body of the powers that are above the heavens.

**¶3** For when the Father of Life constructed the heavenly worlds, he divested them of their bodies, [he] threw them to the earths below.

**¶4** So, the upper worlds are of the soul and the spirit; but the worlds below are of the body and the carcass [of death] ⟨p.121⟩ Now as for this, he [fastened] the powers of heaven; he sealed it on their bodies and their carcasses that are on the earths, so that when life comes up from the fullness of these earths, it can be entirely drawn up to the heights in the roots of what is heavenly, that are fastened in their bodies. And also, all life can be purified in that place.

**¶5** However, the waste that will be swept from the purified one who ascends to the heights will be let fall to the ground through the conduits of these conduits, and will be poured down [to the earths] and thrown to the underside.

**¶6** This is the first conduit, which comes from the powers of the heavens to their bodies and their carcasses that are upon the earth; also, from their bodies and [their] carcasses that are on the earths to the powers of the heavens.

**¶7** The second conduit is the one that comes from the temples and dwellings and cities that exist in the heavens down to the ground, to the five shapes of tree that grow upon the earth. The life comes up from the trees to the temples and cities. Life too, and the residue of the heavenly ones, comes down to the trees in a conduit.

**¶8** And the third conduit is all the powers and householders who dwell in all the firmaments, whose roots come down from them to the five worlds of flesh that creep upon the earth, being fixed to one another. Now, the power and the life that shall be gathered in from the worlds of flesh are dispersed among them in various shapes. The powers above shall draw them to the upper side through conduits.

**¶9** [The waste]te too, and the lust and the evil-doing and the anger that will be greater in the powers of [heaven]n, shall be poured to the ground through their various conduits. They shall be discharged upon mankind and the other remaining animals. When what is heavenly will wash the waste and the stench and the poison down on the creations of the flesh below, in their turn the creations shall be greater in lust and anger and evil-doing against each other through the action of their fathers who are on high.

**¶18** Just as you have instructed us and put together this lesson on [the three conduits] you have told us about the prototype of the conduits. The first is the one that comes from the firmaments to the earths, and the one that comes from the wheel to the sea giant. That is only one. All other paragraphs unchanged per findings marked none.

**Spiritual narrative:** Within the whole span of ordered existence there are three universal pathways of communication, extending from the lowest obscurity up to the highest illumination.

The first pathway is the inmost dependence by which all higher operative forces in the ordered heavens are held in connection with their ultimates. Those higher forces are as though cast down and bound in the lowest degree, because the lowest degree is the plane where interiors are terminated in outward form, and where what is living is set into an external covering and an inert remainder.

For when the Divine vital source brought the ordered heavens into form, it provided them with an ultimate basis by casting beneath them their lowest terminations.

Thus the higher degrees are chiefly living will and living understanding; but the lowest degrees are chiefly outward effect and what is dead when separated from interior life. Therefore the Divine fixed the higher forces to their ultimates and sealed their connection there, so that when life is awakened from the fullness of the lowest plane it may be drawn up entirely into the higher origins that stand rooted in those ultimates; and so that the life rising there may be purified.

Yet whatever cannot be conjoined with the purified life, and whatever is rejected as unfit in the act of purification, is let fall again by those same general communications, sinking down into the lowest underside.

This is the first universal communication: descent from higher forces into ultimates, and return from ultimates into higher forces.

The second universal communication is the exchange between higher ordered intelligences and the formed perceptions in the lowest degree. From higher states there is influx down into the various patterns of perception that grow in the external mind, and from those perceptions a responsive life rises back into higher intelligences. Moreover, both living influence and residual byproduct from higher operations also descend again into those perceptions by a regulated channel.

The third universal communication is the exchange between the powers ruling in intermediate regions and the various bodily life-forms in the lowest degree, which are joined together in mutual dependence. The power and vitality gathered from bodily life are distributed among the intermediate rulers in many varied forms, and the higher powers draw upward whatever can be raised through ordered channels.

Yet refuse as well—craving, evil in act, and kindled hostility that increase among the higher when they fall out of order—is poured down by their several communications into the lowest plane, and is discharged into human life and into other remaining living affections there. When higher things wash impurity, stench, and poison down into the lower forms, the lower forms in turn are driven into greater craving, hostility, and mutual harm, because they are acted upon from above by those that should have governed them for good.

Attend also to the general governing system that measures and disposes outward states. It does not stand by an origin rooted in the lowest degree as its proper base; its origin is held in connection with the totality. Yet it still receives a kind of life from the power and vitality that rise from the lowest plane through ordered communications into intermediate degrees; and it also takes something from the channels that carry upward into higher heavens beyond it.

But if this governing system has no proper root in the lowest plane, from where did it acquire such authority that it rules over external perception and bodily condition? For it is observed that outward lot—gain and loss, sickness and health—seems to come to a person under the general determinations that attend one’s entry into life.

What was said remains true: it has no proper root there. It is held in place by another bond. For when the Divine vital source established the heavens and the intermediate degrees, it bound their origins into the ultimates beneath them, into the dead remainders stripped away as their supporting plane; but when it established this general governing system, it did not bind its origin into those ultimates. Instead it bound its origin by the vast most-external general receptacle of knowledge, so that its authority would stand upon generalities.

And when divine revelation came forth in living representation and brought purified light out through all higher degrees, the undivine material element present within the ruling powers spurted up against that manifestation. It rose to seize it, but could not reach it; and therefore it fell back down into the underside. After falling from higher things, it separated into three distinct portions.

One portion attached itself to that general governing system, another sank into the lowest plane itself, and another flowed into the most-external generality. By means of the portion that attached to the governing system, an induced bond was established within outward perception and bodily life, and therefore that governing system received authority and became dominant over the varieties of external perception and bodily state.

Through the quality of the undivine element that descended onto the governing system, a bond was established within outward perception; and by means of the gross rejected products that fell into the lowest plane, another bond was established within bodily life.

For this reason the determinations of that governing system obtained authority over outward perception and bodily state: through the induced bond of evil that fell down, and through the induced bond of what is malformed and rejected that came forth in the lowest degree.

But the other universal communications that the Divine fixed in the lowest plane, through which the living soul of basic principles rises into higher states, are not conjoined to that governing system. Rather, the powers dependent on that governing system seize those communications and divert them to themselves.

This lesson also sets out a general pattern: there is the communication from intermediate degrees down to the ultimates, and there is the communication by which the governing system’s origin is held in the most external generality—these together forming one complex; then there is the communication from higher intelligences to outward perceptions; and there is the communication established through rejected malformed products within bodily life, by which even the generating sources of those malformed products in higher regions remain intertwined with the bodily plane. Here the sequence becomes fragmented and does not yield a fully coherent spiritual chain.

Further, the preeminence of these communications is that much life rises from the lowest plane in its wide diversity, and the Divine draws it upward. The Divine fastens these pathways from higher powers and binds them into ultimates. They are the origins of the whole dominion by which the life of the living soul is raised, coming up from the entire natural degree in all its exterior states.

After that first great origin comes the origin of outward perceptions, conjoined to higher ordered intelligences and their established forms. For there also much life rises from formed perception and from the whole structure of deliverance and conjunction, and it is drawn upward as if a great host.

What follows concerning lesser measures and unequal distribution becomes broken and only partly intelligible. The sense that can be drawn is that the pathways pertaining to outward perceptions are spread widely through the whole lowest degree in lesser and greater measure, while the pathway pertaining to human bodily life is not equally present everywhere, but is limited to one region and sparse even there; this portion remains uncertain because the wording is damaged.

You have been instructed that these origins are bound together in ordered connections. They stir one another, and they draw life from one another by the operation of higher powers, which sets in motion the common nature dwelling within them all.

But when the undivine material element within these interconnected forms is crushed and stripped away, that same element turns upon the very members in which it was lodged, distorts their faculties, and drives them into mutual overthrow and conflict; the ending is broken, yet it points to escalating disorder among the connected members as that element acts within them.

---

## Chapter 49: Concerning the Wheel and the Conduits

**¶2** The reason that the conduits shall not be cut, for they are not cut, is because they are spiritual. Thus the conduits are like the waters through which ships sail. You shall find the prow that is in position in the front part of the ship divides the waters, casting them back and forth. So, when it parts the waters and that ship opens up the water with its cleaving prow, immediately and without delay the waters mingle with each other again behind the ship; and the path of the ship shall not be discernible in the midst of the waters.

**¶3** Just as when a bird flies up in the air, for its path is not visible in the air, and in the way of mankind, in the sun within the conduits. They also shall not be tangled up in them, and neither shall you find their path through the air known.

**¶4** This is also the case with the wheel. It turns through the conduits and the air. The wheel shall not cut the conduits, nor are the conduits themselves entangled in the wheel.

> **Textual criticism:**
> - ¶2 [none]: Clean correspondential substrate: the chapter teaches by functional analogy (water/air as media that do not retain a cut-path), and the spiritual reading arises without forcing. No sign of administrat
> - ¶3 [none]: Genuine difficulty (likely compression/translation turbulence), not an insertion: the paragraph remains in correspondential voice but contains a syntactically tangled phrase (“in the way of mankind, i
> - ¶4 [none]: Clean correspondential continuation: “wheel” functions as a turning motion moving through conduits/air without cutting or entangling them. The spiritual reading’s slight uncertainty (what exactly the 

**Changes:** No changes were made. All findings (¶2, ¶3, ¶4) recommended none; ¶3 was diagnosed as a genuine difficulty rather than an insertion, and no excision was advised.

**Spiritual narrative:** The interior channels by which truth flows are not severed, because they are living realities of spirit, not dead forms. They are like the free course of truth in the understanding: when a governing discernment moves foremost, it distinguishes and sets truths in order as it advances, seeming to part what it meets. Yet as soon as that discerning power has passed, the truths return at once into their former coherence, and no lasting rupture or scar of separation can be found in the general stream.

So too with an uplifted thought: when it rises into a more interior level, its track does not remain as a visible mark, because higher perception is not grasped as an external line. In the same way, the human in its ordinary state cannot trace how enlightenment moves within the living channels of truth; the motion is not caught and fixed as a known route, nor does it snare the channels into knots.

The same holds for the turning power that brings states into order. It passes through the flowing truths and through higher perception, yet it neither injures the living channels nor becomes bound up with them; the ordering motion remains free, and the truths remain uncut and unentangled.

## Chapter 50: Concerning these Names: God, Rich One, and Angel; who they are.

**¶3** There are these three archetypes [in the land of light, and there is no measure to] them!

**¶4** So, the ones who are called 'god', are the gods whom the Father has summoned from himself. He has established them after the likeness of his greatness.

**¶5** Conversely, these who are named 'rich [ones]', are the evocations of the first rich gods of the Father; because, when the Father had summoned (the first), they themselves summoned evocations. They called them 'rich'.

**¶6** They too, the rich ones, have summoned their evocations. They call them 'angel'. These are the three archetypes who occur in the land of light. There is no measure to apply to them!

**¶7** Once again, listen! Other persons who are named 'god' are the emanations who have come from the Father; the evocations of the Father whom he summoned forth. They came out to the contest and humiliated the enmity.

**¶8** Conversely, the 'rich ones' are the evocations of these first living words. They too, the rich ones, have come and performed and fulfilled the pleasure of the greatness, in the worlds that are above and below.

**¶10** Again, they are called 'god', all the gods who belong to the household of the Great Spirit.

**¶11** Conversely, the ones who are called 'angel', are all the angels who belong to the household of the Beloved of the Lights.

**¶12** The ones who are called 'rich', are all the rich ones who belong to the household of the glorious Ambassador.

> - ¶5 [none]: Genuine difficulty internal to the correspondential schema: a recursive, self-similar generation (“first rich gods… they themselves summoned evocations”) strains the reader, but it is still teaching b
> - ¶6 [none]: Clean completion of the triad in correspondential voice: angels arise as a third-order evocation from rich ones; restates the opening refrain about immeasurability. This is coherent with ¶3–5.
> - ¶7-8 [none]: Mixed-but-still-substrate reframing (a second pass): introduces contest/hostility and ‘living words’ as functional roles (active going-forth, humiliating enmity, fulfilling pleasure across above/below
> - ¶10-12 [annotate]: Overlay block in administrative voice inserted as a classificatory appendix: the chapter’s earlier logic defines three names by procession/archetype; here the names are reassigned by ‘household of X’ 

**Changes:** No changes made. All findings were either 'none' (¶3–8) or 'annotate' (¶10–12); per instructions, annotate recommendations do not alter the chapter text.

**Spiritual narrative:** Within the realm where wisdom is pure, there is a threefold ordering of primary forms, and their quality exceeds any finite standard. Those spoken of as possessing divinity are the first determinations of Divine love that the Divine Itself brought forth from its own essence, fixed in being as receptive forms bearing the image of infinite greatness. Those spoken of as possessing abundance are the derivative determinations that proceed from the first abundant forms; for when Divine love brought forth the first receptions, these in turn brought forth further receptions from themselves, and this derived fullness was recognized as abundance. From these abundant determinations still further receptions go forth; these are ministering powers, because they exist to serve and carry into effect what is higher. Such is the triune ordering that appears in the realm of wisdom, and its quality cannot be measured.

There is also a sense in which those called divine are the outward goings-forth from the Divine, brought forth by the Divine’s own act, which advanced into spiritual conflict and subdued what is hostile. Those called abundant are the receptions that proceed from the first living expressions of truth; these also advanced into operation and brought to completion the delight of Divine greatness across higher and lower degrees, so that what is above might be fulfilled in uses below.

Moreover, the name of divinity is applied to all the ordered forms of good and truth under the ruling Divine in its governing form; the name of ministering power is applied to all operative truths ordered under the Divine that is loved as light itself; and the name of abundance is applied to all fullness of good and truth ordered under the Divine that mediates and makes present what is highest.

**Spiritual narrative:** When the good that engages directly with evil advanced to meet the assault, it invested itself with the outward truth that carries the full force of love, and by that invested state it dried up and subdued the swelling self-exaltation of what opposed it, bringing down the enemy’s empty persuasion and vainglorious pretension.

That single invested power was sufficient for those who depended on it; yet because the primary force of love in outward action had been weakened through conflict, it therefore added a full support by surrounding that love with additional exterior forms of operation: one that gave it active impulsion, one that gave it ordered discernment through truth, one that gave it clear intelligence, and one that gave it a stable ultimate plane for action. These were given so that they might serve as helpers to the governing love, so that what is hostile could not enter that love in its outward working and pierce it, and so that destructive falsity and enmity could not sever its connection.

Although love in its external operation suffered assault and felt injury in its embodied form, still by means of that state the whole array of falsities was constrained. And because the combat required conjunction and cooperation, it was also necessary that the supporting powers as companions in that work should undergo assault and injury as well, for the enemy’s influence entered and mingled with them—except for the ultimate plane of action itself. Into that ultimate plane active falsity did not mingle, and the heated rage of the enemy was not able to pass into it.

Nevertheless even the supporting power that remained without injury—the ultimate plane of action—divested itself of its own external investment and operative strength and transferred it to its four companions, so that by means of that enlivened ultimate support they might be made firm, become effective for use, and endure in their functions. And that ultimate support became for them a continuing vitality in their labor.

**Spiritual narrative:** Consider how far the wisdom that proceeded from the first wisdom in that state extended in a determinate degree: within that reception each distinct operation of truth remained in everlasting unanimity with the others. None of those operations stepped outside the command of order, nor violated the mutual trust by which each held the use committed to it, and this concord endured without end.

But falsity set against truth was already judged and overcome; for when disharmony arose it turned inward and was divided against itself. The whole dominion of that falsity fell into internal conflict; and even the derivative powers that had abandoned their deepest obscurities and risen to join it were judged, because disunion permeated them all. Thus that falsity was brought down and its allied persuasions were humbled, because the power of the first wisdom acts by the same law of order that also governs the lowest realm.

So long as the interior consociations bound up with that arcanum remain in agreement with that falsity, its dominion stands firm for a time: it is wondered at, it is credited with glory, it is treated as the honored one, and it exalts itself as though it surpassed and were more exalted than the opposing dominion that is about to break forth against it.

Yet the dominion sustained by that interior agreement is nonetheless compelled—by the order it abuses—to condemn the other dominion; and then its assumed eminence collapses. Those falsities are humbled and made subject to the governance of the truths established in opposition to them.

## Chapter 53: Concerning the First Man.

**Spiritual narrative:** When the good that engages evil most directly was sent into spiritual combat, it was invested with the first and most immediate protection of saving love, and by that single invested power it entered the earliest receptive level of life. By that power it pursued and subdued the intention of spiritual death, together with the ruling falsities and evils and the whole series of their subordinate forces.

After this good was lifted out of the lowest obscurity, the Divine operative force that gives life and order gathered up the dominant evils and falsities that had been conquered, together with the general falsified principles belonging to denial, so that an outwardly ordered realm of effects could be formed from them in a way serviceable to the manifestation of Divine greatness in ultimates. From that time, the interiors where those perverted principles had been stored were broken up; their former arrangement was devastated, and everything that had been fixed and empowered there was stripped of its prior efficacy.

This resembled a purging of a confused mixed state by constraint: the living affections and the more interior thoughts that had been trapped are seized and bound, while only undistinguished generalities remain, together with the lowest rudiments that can still serve as a basis for later ordering. So it was with the interiors devoted to spiritual death: the combating good seized their deliberations and the allied forces active within, and the interiors themselves remained only as emptied containing forms in the same outward plane where they had been.

Accordingly, the domain of denial is in this condition, and the interiors that were within it are in this condition.

For as a person is an organized whole in which many subordinate functions depend on a ruling principle, so the domain of denial had from its beginning many interior compartments of perverted life and falsified thought, all dependent on a principal governing falsity. One origin served them all, because all were dependent on that ruling falsity that dominated the whole arrangement. If some particular derivative evil or falsity were removed at its outermost extension, the principal falsity governing them all would still remain in its own place, and the entire perverted system would continue in activity. All those lesser governing points were fixed with strength within that principal falsity.

But when those lesser governing points are removed altogether, they become void and lifeless, because they no longer appear to have power or stability from being fixed within the organized perversion to which they had been joined; severed from that connection, they are left without the seeming vitality they drew from it.

In the same way, the interiors of denial are such: they are the ordered forms from which the outflow of falsity and evil originally burst forth.

## Chapter 54: [Concerning] the Quality of the Garments

**¶1** At the time when the F[ir]st [Man of the Greatness arose] he gave the leadership to his five [sons, the five powers: the] Living Spirit and the Mother of Life [in the bond of the lower region,] as the entire ruling-power is entwined and crucified [in the place of mixture;] they were poured out and mixed with each other, the light with the darkness and the darkness with the light.

**¶2** At that time the Living Spirit called one of his sons, one rich with power. He gave to him the leadership of the garments. Now, he himself [after these things,] of [received the garments from him;] he hung them and set them up [the garments of the powers,] of the Father of Life [in the heights;] he set up all things [by the command] he opened [of the Father of Life;] ten firmaments [for the ordering of the All;] the wheel of the stars [the gates of the great firmament,] them and the [twelve signs,].

**¶3** ⟨p.132⟩ The Living Spirit is like this: Just as [a wise craftsman,] and an architect who might build a dwelling for the king, and divide it up into separate houses and [separate chambers] everything that is required for beauty [and he furnishes in it] and he cleans them. And also the stones [the stains, and scrapes away the defilement from] them for beauty and {loveliness}, and he arranges them and makes them so that should the king come and inhabit that palace, he might glorify him.

**¶4** Furthermore, the Father of Life resembles a wise and skilled carpenter [who has in his mind the whole form,] before it was worked [as a log,] before it was established and made beautiful and [unshaped,] carpenter [this] works in it, but [first exists] in him in his [thought,] in his understanding, and he brings the saw and the adze, and cuts it into separate pieces [and he separates] it and he pulls the [waste from] it with the {plane} and he smooths it. He shall make many [roughness from] wooden implements, arranging them shape by shape; he cuts and produces wooden implements from it, wooden implements of different kinds, (such as) dishes, bowls. This wood, that [useful] since previously it had not been worked before it was sealed by [ll]ll and wisdom of this dextrous carpenter. He cut it up into different sorts of implements useful to his master.

**¶5** So is it also with the Father of Life. In his wisdom he has [formed the whole likeness] in limbs [all the faculties] palace [of this great] in it or in a [or in a member] useful [for every service,] the ships of light [and all the lamps of light,] all the other works [and] in the [lowest region,] so that when the great king comes, he who is the Third Ambassador, who [is the Mediator of the Light,] he might dwell in this universe [having come in glory,]

**¶6** ⟨p.133⟩ ... live. The light, however, that is in it [that is gathered out from it will] wise, and he takes it up to the land; but the waste and the darkness will be gathered and thrown down to the abyss.

> - ¶1 [none]: Coherent substrate setup in correspondential voice. The teaching proceeds by function (mixture/entanglement) rather than by jurisdiction. No narrative seam or administrative inventory appears.
> - ¶2 [none]: Local corruption/condensation rather than an administrative insertion. The paragraph remains mostly correspondential (ordering ‘garments’ = external forms/truths; establishing cosmic architecture as f
> - ¶3 [none]: Clean correspondential likeness (architect/craftsman) that directly continues ¶2’s functional theme: ordering, cleansing, arranging for indwelling. No sign of overlay or seam expansion.
> - ¶4 [none]: Clean correspondential likeness (carpenter) with some lacunae but no voice shift. The paragraph teaches by process—conceived form → cutting/separating → removing waste → smoothing → producing diverse 
> - ¶5 [annotate]: Mostly coherent correspondential doctrinal restatement, but with two small seams: (a) abrupt anthropological/cosmic inventory (“limbs… faculties… ships of light… lamps of light… lowest region”) likely
> - ¶6 [none]: Coherent correspondential conclusion: separation by outcome/function (light gathered upward; waste/darkness cast down). The reader reports no stumble; the ending closes the process-narrative establish

**Changes:** No changes made. The critic issued no excision recommendations (Findings 1, 2, 3, 4, 6: none). Finding 5 recommends annotation only (no textual modification), so ¶5 remains unchanged.

**Spiritual narrative:** When the first good that stands forth to order the conflict entered its appointed operation, it entrusted the government of its outward working to a sufficient complement of powers. At the same time, living truth acting from Divine love, together with life-giving wisdom, held the whole ruling force bound in the lowest degree where all things are constrained in suffering and held in forced conjunction. In that state the powers were let down into the outermost plane and intermingled, so that wisdom was entangled with falsity and falsity with wisdom, and each seemed to carry something of the other.

Then living truth from Divine love sent a directed influx to one operative power distinguished by potency, and committed to it the ordering of external truths for use. Afterward living truth itself took those external truths into its own administration, fixed them in their places, and set them up in a higher state of reception under the Divine will. By that ordering it disposed all things, opened a full completeness for the stable arrangement of the whole, and set in order the governing revolutions of general knowledges together with the fullness of organized truths required for the administration of the entire system.

Living truth from Divine love works like wisdom that prepares an ordered form for the indwelling of the Divine end. It distinguishes interior regions and exterior regions within one form of life, assigns each what is needed for harmony, cleanses away what defiles reception, removes whatever disfigures, and arranges goods and truths into a coherent unity, so that when the ruling love comes to dwell, the whole form may honor it by concord and by uses.

Likewise the Divine love that sustains life holds within itself, in wisdom, the complete pattern of what is to be brought forth, before anything is formed in the outermost degree. From that inward pattern it provides the means of discrimination and correction, distinguishes what must be distinguished, removes what is superfluous, straightens what is uneven, and reduces what is rough into a state fit for conjunction. Thus it brings forth a variety of distinct functions in the natural degree, each according to its form, and disposes them for service under one governing end; and what had been unformed becomes fixed and determined by wisdom and skill, then distributed into different uses for the sake of that end.

So it is with the Divine love that sustains life: from wisdom it forms within the complete human pattern all faculties and capacities, each for every service, and it disposes within the lowest degree all illuminations and all other ordered effects, so that when the Divine mediation that unites higher and lower comes in manifest presence, it may dwell within this entire ordered system.

Afterward a final separation is made within that system: whatever is truly living is gathered upward into a clearer state where it can be held in wiser use, while what is merely obstructive and what is falsity is collected together and cast down into the lowest state, where it can no longer injure the ordered whole.

## Chapter 55: Concerning the Fashioning of Adam

**¶1** There are some amo]ng us telling us that we [understand] that the (Ambassador) consents about [that]t f[fig] of flesh; he who shall be fashi[oned] at another time. And next, he says: [The Ambassa]dor displayed his image in the un[iverse]. The rulers and the powers of the universe saw h[is image], and they formed their shapes after his likeness, who are Adam and Eve. Behold, you see that god con[sen]ts to its happening; because of this, he has displayed his image to the rulers; and they constructed [a figure of flesh] If he was not consenting to humankind, he would not have uncovered his image, nor they fashioned mankind after his likeness!

**¶2** Regarding the Ambassador, the occasion when he came displaying his image, he [d]id not come so th[a]t he might reveal his image to the ru[l]ers in the universe. Rather, he came and displayed to the universe be[cause of h]is soul and his son, who is [his soul] every [bond], that he might enable him to live [from death] and make for him a release; and to free (the soul) from every bond and all the fetters, in which it is entangled and bound.

**¶3** Still, you, look and see! The rulers and the authorities, in that they had no form in [truth in] him, they lusted for his image. They reflected upon themselves, that [they] had no [one] like him in their creation. They sealed hi[s im]a[g]e in their heart, within their soul. Afterward[s, they] built it according to his likeness; they formed Adam and [Ev]e. They sealed the image of the exalted one in their [soul]. So, they [co]pied with an imitation; but they have not [copied i]n truth! Rather, just as [in a painted image] it is made as these, for [i]t came [from their own matter] but it is not l[ik]e him in truth. [Again, so too] is this figure of ma[mankind. They copi]ed it against the image of the exalted one [and they did not make it in truth]

**¶4** Similarly the [t]ree, for while it is like the [image, yet] not [copi]ed in truth.

**¶5** This matt[er is comparable to] this: As a great, fre[e] woman [dwells hidden] within her [treasures and ornaments] rich in wealth [many] po[ss]essions; she is also beautiful in her beauty, and is virtuous in her modesty. She walks in a free and reputable position. The fame of her beauty has spread in every c[i]ty. She sur[surpasses all women in] Behold h[er] chamber [and there is not] one who is mad, because [of] the nobles and the great ones. Al[so], they lust for her beautiful face. They did not [able to seize] her, because she is hidden in her palace [nor to approach] within in a [for she is] meet that woman [and no one can] his so[n]; or rather the head [upon her head; for]hat garment. This free woman shall [put on] leave the cha[mber] behind her and come to the street [and she will] her head [lift up] and she reveals her [face and her beauty and her splendor] because of her beloved broth[er]. This wom[an], on whom no man ever looked, nor did they ever see her fa[ce], as she neither desires nor rejoices [in wantonness or in pride] leave her chamber behind her, and come in the midst of mankind [but grief compelled her] and everyone view her. The hones[t] men and the nobles, even the servants too, and the [common people also] look at her.

**¶6** ⟨p.135⟩ In accord with this simile: Again, [this] too [is] how [it is] the Ambassador becomes [in the same manner] as this free woman [not] according to wantonness, in pride, nor [nor that she co]nsented to the outer world, she [did not consent], she revealed her face and her [beau]ty [to those outside] but only due to the constraint of grief [about] her [bel]ov[oved one].

**¶7** Now, this too is the way of the Third Ambassador, when he came and display[ed his] image with a show towards all the powers. [He did] not [co]me so that all the rulers and the author[it]ies would sculpt a form [after h]is likeness. R[ather], he came because of his son being crucified in the totality; [fo]r he would release him, free him, and rescue him from oppres-sion. Yet, when the rulers looked o[n] him, [th]ey lusted for his image. They fashioned after his [like]ness Adam and Eve, without the consent of the gre[at]ness.

**¶9** [Understa]nd also this other (matter) that I will teach you. For, [a]s with Adam and Eve, whom the rulers fashioned and [se]aled after the image of the exalted one, so too this [i]s how a manifold divinity and many powers and angels [were stamped and received] the stamp of that image, which was displayed to them. For, in the majesty of the l[ig]ht and (the image's) love, they glowed with their desire and they were undone. Th[ey] were eager for their lust; like a m[man who looks] to the majesty of a lusted after face, and [is driven onward] and he is eager for the {} of the desire.

**¶10** ⟨p.136⟩ This is [als]o the case with those powers, when they looked on that majestic and beautiful [imag]e, they () [fashioned within themselves] a first image. They received the stamp of th[that image], which was displayed to them. They copied [outwardly, wh]ich th[ey co]pied out in a likeness, it being made [in the forms] the ones that they had put forth, which had received a [stamp of this im]age; but inward even to the rocks [were hardened] which occur from place to place. The {} of that image, which was displayed in the universe [likeness] they received a stamp, it being revealed there, and an impress.

**¶11** Now, this new birth that they begot in a new [form] in the world, is Adam and Eve. [There is not a] form in their world since the beginning like it; because that [im]age [occ]urs not in the worlds b[el]ow.

**¶12** Not all the figures that have been fashioned were fashioned by birth from displayed intercourse. Rather, there are other figures fashioned [without intercourse]

**¶13** One of them is the sea [gi]ant, the 'sweeping' from the sphere, which the [Father] of Life discharged into the sea. Its own fire and thought fashioned it [in] a figure.

**¶14** The second figure is the one [that] fell to the earth. It made the sea its womb. Its own l[us]t became its sculptor. It alone fashioned it to a nature, which is the root of death. Yet, when it came up from the sea, it [set itself to rise up] that it might destroy the works [that come from the works] of Life. They immediately sent against it the Adamant of Light, the great 'instruction of vigour'. He humbled it in the districts of the north. He has trampled underfoot and set h[him]self upon it, until the end of the world.

**¶15** ⟨p.137⟩ [The] third figure is the nature that fell on wh[at] is dr[y. It] fashioned the tree and set itself there. [and from] new figure that was fashioned [and it]isplays.

**¶16** So, in accord with the essence they [inclined toward]ness; but yet th[ey] did not come with the King of Dark[ness to the wo]rld. Rather, their fashioning [came] about [through mixture; for] they are composite and have been displayed in the world.

> - ¶5 [none]: Damaged simile with heavy lacunae and scrambled clauses; the spiritual reading had to reconstruct and smooth missing pieces. This is not an administrative insertion; it is a corrupt transmission seam 
> - ¶6-7 [none]: Clean correspondential ‘application’ and return to the main line. ¶7 repeats the thesis (manifestation for rescue, not to provide a model) and integrates the “crucified son” as functional oppression/r
> - ¶9-11 [none]: Mostly clean correspondential expansion: ‘stamping/impress’ is functional (how an image reproduces copies). However, there is localized semantic strain from damaged wording in ¶10 (multiple lacunae an
> - ¶12-16 [none]: Clean thematic shift that remains within correspondential teaching: a taxonomy by origin-mechanism (with/without intercourse; self-fashioning by lust; restraint by adamant instruction). No administrat

**Changes:** No changes made. The textual criticism findings (Findings 1–7) recommended no excisions or annotations; all difficulties were assessed as transmission damage/lacunae rather than later overlay.

**Spiritual narrative:** Some among us say that we understand that the Divine permits an external human reception to be composed in time. They add that the Divine made its own human quality manifest within the general field of the lower domain. The governing falsities and their operative forces perceived that manifestation, and from it they constructed an outward pattern of human life in their own realm, producing a paired appearance of will and understanding. From this it is evident that permission was given for such a result; for if the Divine had not allowed an external human reception in the lower degree, it would not have made its own human quality perceptible there, nor could those governing forces have fabricated a human pattern from it.

Concerning the Divine messenger: when that manifestation occurred, it did not occur in order to offer itself as a model to the governing falsities in the lower domain. It occurred for the sake of the interior living principle in humanity and for the sake of what proceeds from it, so that what was held in spiritual death might be restored to life and opened a way of deliverance, and so that the interior human might be loosed from every restraint and from all compulsions by which it was entangled and bound.

Nevertheless, consider what those governing forces did. Because they had no genuine participation in that manifested human quality, they burned with craving for it. They turned back upon themselves and recognized that nothing in their making was like it. They fixed an impression of it within their interior affection and thought; afterward they built an external structure in its likeness and produced a paired human appearance. Thus they impressed within themselves a likeness of what is higher, and from that they produced an imitation; but it was not produced from truth. It arose from what could be seized and shaped within their own perverted ground, yet it did not become that higher quality in reality. So also the external human appearance they produced was made against that higher manifestation and remained an imitation.

In the same way, even an appearance of perception in their domain can resemble what is from higher life, and yet it is not formed from genuine truth.

This can be grasped in this way: consider a lofty wisdom that dwells inwardly in its own power, rich with goods and truths, beautiful with the splendor of intelligence, and chaste with purity of love for what is right. It moves in dignified freedom, and the report of its excellence spreads through all external knowledges. Those who are eminent in pride and those who seek dominion lust after that excellence. Yet they cannot seize it while it is guarded within its own interior order and protected from violation. But when sorrow for what is most beloved presses upon it, that inward wisdom goes forth into outward manifestation—not for display, not from wantonness, not from self-exaltation, and not from consenting to be possessed by the outer domain, but solely because grief for the beloved compels it. Then what none had looked upon before in that lower realm becomes visible, and all states there—sincere and noble, servile and common—perceive it.

So it was with the third Divine messenger: when it came and made its human quality manifest before all operative powers, it did not come so that the governing falsities and their authorities might fabricate an outward form after it. It came because what proceeds from the Divine within humanity had been brought into the extremity of suffering throughout the whole lower domain, and it came to release it, free it, and rescue it from oppression. Yet when the governing forces looked upon that manifestation, they craved it and produced, from their own impulse and without approval from the higher Divine, a paired outward human appearance after that likeness.

Understand also this further point: just as those governing forces impressed their fabricated human pattern with the likeness of what is higher, so too a multiplicity of claimed divinities, together with many subordinate powers and intelligences, received an imprint of that likeness when it was presented to them. For in the splendor of wisdom and in the warmth of love shining through it, they flared with desire and lost their stability; they hastened into craving, like one who fixes upon a lusted excellence and is driven onward by the force of desire.

So it happened with those powers: when they beheld that majestic and beautiful manifestation, they formed within themselves a primary copied appearance and took in its imprint. What they then brought forth outwardly became likenesses shaped into the forms they had already projected—forms that bore that impressed quality in externals while remaining inwardly fixed in the lowest confirmations, hardened and unyielding in the outermost degree. Thus the likeness that had been manifested in the lower domain was received there as an impress and produced outward replicas.

This new production, which they generated as though it were a new origin within their lower realm, was that paired human appearance. Nothing in their domain from the beginning had taken such a form, because that higher human quality does not arise from the lower domains themselves.

Yet not every fabricated appearance in that realm arose from the conjunction that followed this manifested presentation. Other appearances were also produced without such a conjunction.

One of these was an enormous sweeping falsity moving through externals, which the living Divine permitted to be cast into the general field of external knowledges. From its own self-love and from its own persuasions it fashioned for itself an outward appearance.

A second was a perverted nature that fell into the lowest degree. It made the general external knowledges its matrix, and its own lust shaped its form. It alone fashioned itself into a disposition that became the root of spiritual death. When it rose into clearer manifestation out of those externals, it set itself to destroy the works that proceed from life. Immediately the Divine sent against it an unyielding truth from wisdom, a great strengthening instruction, which subdued it in the region of obscurity, brought it under restraint, and kept it subjected until the completion of that age.

A third was a perverted nature that turned toward what is devoid of living truth. From that dryness it organized a false system of perception and fixed itself within it; and from this another outward appearance was produced and made manifest.

According to their essential quality they inclined toward denial and obscurity; yet they did not enter the lower domain as one with the reigning love of evil itself. Rather, these fabricated appearances came into being through mixed and composite states, and so they were made visible in the lower realm.

## Chapter 56: Concerning Saklas and his Powers

**¶2** The sin that spurted out from the rulers, which is Matter, shot up [tow]ards the image of the Ambassador. It was cut off from [tha]t place and came down to the earth, for they did not accept [it wi]thin the firmaments. When it came do[wn to] the earth, it formed the tree. It was established within the wood and formed the fruits. And when [the] abortions fell [to] the ground, to the earth, at the [second] time [it came up again in them and entered the rulers]

**¶3** [And when it came up] fruits, it assumed the[m] and spoke in the ruler, their leader. He says to his companions: Come! Give me your light, and I will construct for you an image aft[er] the likeness of the exalted one. What he said, they did: they gave it to him, and he constructed [the form of Adam and Eve after the likeness of the exalted one].

**¶4** The sin that s[purted out from the rulers,] which is the Matter that saw the im[age of the Ambassa]dor and that formed the tre[e and was established i]n [i]t; afterwards it came up in the [fruits and assumed them]. It went in to the rulers [and by its power] they formed Adam and Eve after the [likeness of the] exalt[ed one]. Through the energy of the sin that had s[een the image of the] Ambassador, it went into the (rulers) [through the fruits. It] assumed them and they [t]oo sculp[ted with it. And they] begat each of them according to his destiny [in their generations]; one he resembles, one he does not resemble.

**¶5** Behold, I have taught you this lesson, how the rulers shaped Adam and Eve by the action of the sin that went into the [m] through the fruits, and he was ordained after the image of the exalted one.

**¶6** The moulder placed in the form of Adam and Eve limbs, outside and within, for perception and activity. He was apportioned house by house. For everything that his perceptions and elements will receive externally there are internal storehouses and repositories and cavities; and what is received in to them is stored in them. Whenever they will be questioned about what is deposited in their internal storehouses, they bring out what they have received within and give it to the questioner who requested it of them.

**¶7** In this way his faculty [of the eyes has] outer [limbs] to look at [all sights,] every type with [in its storehouses.]

**¶8** Also [the fa]culty of the eyes [has] houses and cavities and repositories and stores within, so that every image it might see, whether good or evil, whether loveable or detestable or lustful, it can receive into its storehouses and repositories. Also, when the faculty of the eyes is pleased to send out the image that it saw and took in, it can go in to its storehouses [at the] time and think and seek [every image that it stored within,] and it brings it out [and] gives it to the questioner who requested it and the one who wan[t]ed it—whether it be something from lu[stful thing,]n image of love [or something] hateful. And thus shall that faculty (of the eyes) produce and do what it does in each category.

**¶9** The faculty of the ears has its own storehouses also. Every sound it might receive, whether good or evil, shall be taken in and placed in its houses and inner repositories, and it is guarded in its [inner storehouses] for a thousand days. After a thousand days, if someone comes and asks that faculty about the sound that it heard [at] this time and took into its storehouses, immediately it [sh]all g[o] into its repositories and seek and review and search after this word, and send it out from where it was first put, the place in which it was kept.

**¶10** [In] like order, the faculty of scent [s]hall function just as that of the eyes and that of the auditory organs. Every [o]dour it shall smell it shall take in [to it] and [d]eposit in its inner storehouses. Every time it will be [a]sked by a questioner, it shall go in [to its inner repositories] and [seek in its] storehouse and remember [these odours] only these things.

**¶11** However, even the mouth and the tongue within it, and the taste organ, have a faculty dwelling in them. Again, that faculty too, of taste, has thus cavities and repositories set apart for it. It too receives these tastes and gathers them in. A[nd] at any moment when someone will ask of a taste, if [it wishes,] it shall send it out and remember tha[t] taste. It shall snare and give even the mar[k of] th[a]t taste; give its memory to the qu[est]ioner who asks for it.

**¶12** Again, the fac[ul]ty of touch by the hands is also s[o]: When it might touch, touch shall [receive i]ts memory. And it [takes] it in to its inner repository [until] som[eo]ne will ask this faculty for the memory. [Immediately], it shall go in again and bring out the memory of this touch that it made, and give it to whoever asks for it.

**¶13** And the faculty of the heart that rules over them all is much the most like this. Every thing that these five faculties will receive and put in store for the faculty of the heart it shall receive and guard. Any time that they will ask for their deposit it shall send out and give every thing that they gave to it.

**¶14** For the counsel of the heart is like the king. All the ad[v]isers of his city and the leaders of his a[r]my are placed with him. Any time if his genera[ls] will entreat him for whatever they wish, he shall give them the ad[v]ice and opinion by which they move.

**¶15** Furthermore, it is like a cellarman: [Eve]ry thing that will be entrusted to it by them, and given to it, it shall receive and put in separate vessels and places. Any time [if they ask it for what was entrusted to it, it brings it out]

**¶16** ... in the place that it put it.

**¶17** This also is the way of the faculty of the body. The vision that the eyes will see and give to it, and it receives it in, it shall bring out again from the storehouses wherein it had placed it; and give it to whoever asks for it.

**¶18** Again, [the] sound that the ears will hear and give to it, and it receives it from them. At any time when someone will ask it for that sound, it shall bring out and give the sound of the ears to whoever has asked it for that sound. It shall bring out and give the sound of the ears to whoever has asked.

**¶19** Again, [the] faculty of smell in this same pattern; and that of taste is of the same [ty]pe. The touch by the han[ds too,] they shall bring it out in to [its storehouses and repositories] And whenever they will as[k it] for it, it shall bring it out and gi[ve it].

**¶20** Indeed, watchmen are a[t] these doors guarding them, and bolts are fastened on the doors at the hands of the guards that guard them!

**¶21** Just like watchmen guarding a camp with towers and watch-posts. As the holding rooms come out from there and the bolts are upon the gates, the guards control the bolts of the gates while they sit at the entrance [of] the watchposts. So, someone who belongs to their city as a fellow [cit]izen of them, belonging to their country, for him they shall open [the ga]te and receive him in by their will. However, the foreigner who comes they shall prevent, and leave him standing at the gate. They shall not yield to let him enter. Again, another one whom they will receive in, with great trouble before [they] take him in, you shall find many holding rooms [behind them,] that [and locks and bolts;] there are a mass [within] them. The number of d[oor] bolts [of the gates] are [many,] Whenever someone will wish [to bring out,] the bolts at his hand, opens the gate and brings out whoever is hidden within.

**¶22** This body too is like the mighty camp. And the gates of the camp with their guards are like the orifices and organs of the body. Now, the orifices of the body are of sight, hearing and smell; and they that send out words. There are many sentinels and a mass of guards placed over the limbs of the body, guarding their orifices. And the faculty in it is the directive of the body, the queen of the entire camp; so that whenever it wishes it can open, or when it wishes it can shut.

**¶23** However, if and wh[en] the Light Mind comes, it shall tu[rn i]n to the orifices of the body. The rulers, who [a]re the door gua[rds], shall h[in]der and restrain it from en[ter]ing. They shall bind the circu[it] of the body so that the foreign[er] can not enter it.

**¶24** Nevertheless, the Light Mind by its wisdom and awe and diligence shall hum[ble] the guards who are set at the body's orifices. When it finishes humbling them it shall take fr[o]m them bolts to all the orifices of the body. Now, the orifices that had been opened before to the parades of lust, to receive in the sights of pleasure, to transfer the [wor]ds of the practice of li[f]e's concerns, to receive in the t[astes] of the different foods and unclean meals together with the sin therein; whoever stops receiving th[is] in, his heart and mind shall follow after.

**¶25** So, now, bec[ause] the b[olt]s to the body of the righteous person are in the hands of the Light M[in]d within, he is open [to receive] in all that is pleasing to God. He is op[en to take] in by his eyes the visions of lov[e and justice] and righteousness. Indeed, due to this, the si[ghts and] the [ad]ornme[nt of the gar]ments of kings, which are woven with gold; and the garments of silken wom[en], which are woven with gold and pearls, made beautiful for the shape of lust; first they were lovable before the eyes of the chosen person. Yet, now, by the power of the Light Mind that has suffused him and lived in the body he has humbled lust, the goddess of the body. The parades of lust have become loathsome in the presence of the righteous person. How-ever, the aspects of divinity and righteousness and humility and scorned clothes are pleasing to him.

**¶26** Again, [these] ears were first opening to empty sounds and to the melodies of lust, to the secrecies of [wic]kedness. All these were pleasing at first, at the time when he he[a]rd them. Yet, now, by the power of the Light Mind, the sounds of lust and the words of magic and evil mysteries have become loathsome in his presence; as he is not pleased to hear them from this time on. Rather, he likes all the time to listen to the sounds of the lessons of righteousness, the words of the psalms and of the prayers, the praise of the hymns and the lessons of truth, and the knowledge of charity.

**¶27** [S]imply, all orifices in similar fashion were opening at one time to wickedness, but now have opened to the good. Sin was ruling in the body this time, doing anything it liked, having none in command.

**¶28** Yet, [n]ow, authority has been taken from the Sin in the org[ans] of the body. The Living Spirit has become lord over all its limbs. It has bound them with a chain of peace, and sealed them with the seal of truth. It has opened the orifices of the body to the good, and because of this the good enters them through the ears and the eyes. It dwells in the heart. The H[o]ly Spirit reigns. It does all that it pleases. It annuls [the] will of [S]in, the begin-ning and the end. [In contrast], the will of the [Holy Spirit] comes about, and the purpose of the Light Mind.

**¶29** With wisdom and skill make yourselves secure at the circuit of the body's orifices, so that the sin that dwells in the body has no power over you. Nor does it take from you your light, nor scatter it from place to place, nor (drive) it from site to site in various forms.

> - ¶17-¶19 [none]: Clean continuation with minor scribal-style redundancy: the ‘faculty of the body’ reprises the earlier storage/retrieval mechanism at a higher integrative level. ¶18 repeats a full clause, which reads
