> - ¶11 [none]: Correspondential exhortation with a real syntactic knot at the key comparison (“these waters … greater than the stature of the First Man”). The difficulty is best explained as damaged transmission (om

**Changes:** No changes made. All findings (¶6–¶11) carried recommendation “none”; no excisions were warranted, and “annotate” actions were not requested.

**Spiritual narrative:** When the good that engages directly with evil took on the outermost conditions required for combat, and held steady against the first uprising of opposition formed in active falsity, it defied the dominant evils that rule by persuasive force. Their reach was beyond any ordinary measure, surpassing what countless multiplied states can comprehend. At the beginning of the resistance, that good allowed those evils to take into themselves the burning of their own distorted love and the volatility of their persuasive reasoning; and afterward it permitted the evil that tends to spiritual death to take on an outward covering of truth in its lowest degree. The ruling evils drew that truth into themselves and consumed it by turning it into falsity. From that outward truth, once seized and perverted, abundant derivative falsities poured out, and streams of reasoning flowed on; and the receptive craving of those evils, together with the powers that labor to destroy good and truth, strained to bring those outflows forth.

A full process of such streams, like a principal current of truth flowing with intelligence, entered the craving of each ruling evil; yet no influx could be gathered into a single satisfied reception and brought to rest, because as each descent reached their inmost appropriation it tormented them with the fire and heat of their own love. Those evils were self-renewing persuasions of deception; they were not exhausted, and continual descent of obscured and falsified influence sustained them through the whole span of their existing states.

This shows the scale of the conflict, because it has brought into view destructive affections with gigantic force. They remain entangled, held in constraint, and fixed throughout the whole range of their domain. They are kept from open manifestation and are not presented to the perception of everyone who lives in the merely natural degree under the rule of spiritual death, though in their own order they are made manifest. The entire structure of merely natural life separated from what is living will be ended and dissolved, and its appearances will pass away in an instant. Those evils will be destroyed by the outburst of their own furious persuasion, more devastating than any external shock.

What appears as a small outward comprehension is almost nothing beside the immeasurable abundance of falsities that arise from interior things when truth is seized and inverted. For what is known in externals is only one stream out of innumerable streams from inner arcana that are not disclosed to the natural mind.

Turn your attention to this and be astonished at the measure of truth even in its lowest form, and at the burden borne by the Divine Humanity in carrying such an outward covering through conflict. Understand the greatness of the Divine in active power: the scope of what was permitted for the sake of victory exceeded the measure of the good that entered the outermost combat, and thus an immense work was accomplished.

---

## Chapter 62: Concerning the Three Rocks

**¶2** Three great rocks, which exist, were appointed by the greatness. They were set in these outer worlds.

**¶3** So, the first rock is the [Pillar] of Glory, the Perfect Man, which was summoned [by the] glorious Ambassador. He set it in this zone; [and] brought forth its base (?) from the earth to heaven. It bore up under the [en]tire uni[verse], and became first of all the weight-carriers in [it]s powers. In its firmness it has been set upright, and has made fast every [t]hing above and below.

**¶4** [The] second great and glorious rock is the new earth of light, which was set upon the earth of darkness. It has squashed the fastenings of the five storehouses of death. It has established firmness, with great firmness, over and beyond the earth of darkness. It has flattened, crushed and fixed the root of death.

**¶6** [These] are the three great rocks, they that have come forth in great confidence, in three great and honoured places. They were appointed and distributed in three great places of these [self]same worlds. They were made fast according to the will of the greatness.

> **Textual criticism:**
> - ¶2 [none]: Clean correspondential incipit: sets up a triadic, functional teaching about supports in the ‘outer worlds’ without drifting into jurisdictional lists or rank administration.
> - ¶3 [none]: Largely clean correspondential exposition of the first rock; any ‘administrative’ feel comes from titles (“Ambassador”) but the paragraph’s logic is functional (bearing, fastening, joining earth-to-he
> - ¶4 [none]: Clean correspondential teaching with one possible systematizing numeral (‘five storehouses’) that the reading could not specify, but it still functions organically as ‘bindings/receptacles of death’ b
> - ¶6 [none]: Structural gap / damaged omission: the summary asserts “three great rocks” but only two are described in the extant text. This is best explained as loss (or non-preservation) of a paragraph describing

**Changes:** No changes made. All findings (1–4) recommended none; no paragraphs were targeted for excision, and no annotations were to be inserted into the text.

**Spiritual narrative:** Within the outermost degree, the Divine appointed three fundamental supports, and fixed them within the external domains so that all later order could stand.

The first support was the full manifestation of Divine truth in a perfect human form, called forth by immediate Divine commission. It was established in the region where ultimates are arranged for governance, and it was extended so that the lowest degree could be opened upward into higher life, joining what is ultimate to what is inmost. By this extension it sustained the entire ordered whole and became the primary bearer through which all operative powers are held and carried. By its steadfastness the whole was made upright, and everything in every degree was held together and made firm, both what is interior and what is exterior.

The second support was an enlightened new natural degree, established above a natural degree that had been immersed in falsity and opposed to life. This higher natural was set in a way that it could restrain the lower, and in doing so it broke the closures and bindings by which a sufficient series of interior receptacles had been held in the intention of spiritual death. It instituted stability—stability made strong—over the falsified natural, and extended this ordering beyond it so that the lower could no longer rule. Thus it subdued, crushed, and permanently fixed the originating principle from which spiritual death took root.

These are the three fundamental supports, brought forth in confidence and set with honor in three distinct degrees within the same external domains. They were assigned and established in their proper places, and made immovable by the Divine will.

---

## Chapter 63: Concerning Love

**¶2** ⟨p.156⟩Love is the Father of Greatness, who dwells in his glorious land. The entirety of the divine has been revealed [therein]in! These two are a single living body, the Father and his love; because he has given himself for every thing, as he exists in his aeons. Indeed, due to this, they called the Father, the Lord of the totality, 'love'. For he gave the victory to his aeons and his limbs.

**¶4** Hate is the first death that welled up. It revealed the land of darkness. This one called 'hate', because it has ruined all its perdition's offspring; it sinned even against a power foreign to it, and ruined it. Due to this they shall call it 'hated'; for it has caused destruction, both wounding in flesh, and what is not its own.

**¶5** Today again it causes wounding and destruction in all flesh. It kills them all with diverse deaths; these that are displayed today in every thing, in every type. Even the Cross of Light, the living soul, likewise too it ruins and kills them; as it did at the start. Indeed, due to this have they called it 'hated', this one from the hated; the envy from the envy; the devil from the devil; the bad that came about from the bad.

> **Textual criticism:**
> - ¶2 [none]: Clean correspondential teaching: love is defined by what it does (self-giving, revealing, granting victory), and the metaphysics (one living body) arises from that function. No narrative break or juri
> - ¶4 [none]: Clean correspondential contrast: hate is defined as a functional/ontological principle (“first death”) that generates a realm (“revealed the land of darkness”) and destroys even what is outside its pr
> - ¶5 [none]: Mostly clean correspondential continuation with a minor rhetorical expansion: the present-tense extension (“today again… today…”) and the climactic string of epithets (“hated… envy… devil… bad”) inten

**Changes:** No changes made. All findings (¶2, ¶4, ¶5) carried a recommendation of none; therefore no excisions or other edits were applied.

**Spiritual narrative:** Divine love is the origin of all greatness, abiding in its own splendor in the inmost blessedness of life. In that inmost, the whole Divine is made manifest; for the Divine and the love that proceeds from it are one living reality, not divided but united as a single form. This is so because the Divine gives itself wholly to all that can receive, being present in every ordered extension of heaven and in every faculty that lives from it. Therefore the Divine is named the Lord of all things as love itself, since from love comes all power of overcoming evil, and from love comes the strengthening and preserving of the entire heavenly communion and of every power within it.

Opposed to this, hatred is the first state of spiritual death that arose, and from it the entire domain of falsity and denial was opened. It is called hatred because it lays waste everything that is born from its own condemnation; it even transgresses its proper bounds and assaults a good outside its own nature, and brings it to ruin. Therefore it is called accursed, because it works destruction by injuring the life of the will and by profaning what it has no right to claim.

Even now it continues to inflict harm and devastation in every form of natural life. It extinguishes life by many kinds of spiritual death, which are evident today in every operation and in every manner of life. It also assaults and seeks to extinguish the saving Divine truth and the living spiritual life that ought to be joined to it, just as it did from the beginning. Therefore it is named from what is accursed, and from its own envy and adversarial spirit, and from its own evil, because it is the same perverted love multiplying itself in every degree.

---

## Chapter 64: [Concerning Adam]

**¶2** Three great things that are distinct were revealed in Adam the first human. Indeed, due to this, he was found to be better and more outstanding than the conjoined powers that are in heaven and on earth.

**¶3** The first: The image of the exalted one was placed upon him. The creators and moulders of his body sealed him after the shining form of the image that had been displayed to them above. Indeed, due to this, the image of Adam was found to be surpassingly beautiful beyond any conjoined power above and below.

**¶4** The second thing: Adam was shaped in his structure through the light of the first-born of heaven and [earth, through] the five sons. Indeed, due to this, his structure was found to differ from the structure of even the other creations; [sin]ce the form of all creations is different. As for Adam, the formation of his soul fits over the correct distribution of the elements. Therefore, he has intelligence surpassing that of the other creations and beasts.

**¶5** The third are the teachings and counsels and the seal of all the powers above and below. The creators, who set him in order, gathered them and sealed them in him. Adam and his consort Eve became a dwelling and home for the signs of the zodiac and the stars; and the months, the days and the years. For the seal of the entire universe is stamped upon Adam. Indeed, due to this, heaven and earth moved because of him; trouble and disorder arose on his account between they that are good and they that are evil. So, the good induce him to life because of their image and shape placed upon him; while the [wi]cked were drawing him to death, so that they would have power through him and they would conceive the kingdom, and through him humiliate the entire universe.

**¶6** Furthermore, understand that the rulers determined and formed Adam so that they would be masters through him and conceive the kingdom because of seven things.

**¶7** ⟨p.158⟩
The first: He became the enlightener in their creation; instead of the light of the image of the Ambassador who had displayed himself to them above. For they all desired the likeness of the image of the Ambassador. Their heart was drawn after it.

**¶8** The second: So that they would scoop up the light through him and restrain and obstruct it from its ascent to the heights.

**¶9** The third: So that because of the image of the Ambassador that is placed upon him the entirety of the divine that is above and below would be humiliated; on account of the likeness of the splendour, of the beauty of his superiority. For he is superior in his appearance to all creatures.

**¶10** The fourth: He is a seal-ring and a new impression, so that all births who will be begotten might be brought forth after his seal.

**¶11** The fifth: That might be displayed through him all the skil[ls] of the powers, they above and below, secret and manifest; and that they might determine through him the totality, and every thing be revealed in him.

**¶12** The sixth: That there would come to be a great protection for them through the image of the exalted one that was placed upon him; for as the heavenly would [restrain] even rulers; and no evil thing would be done to them on account of Adam.

**¶13** The seventh: That he might become chief and king and master over all creatures; and they might conceive the kingdom through him.

> **Textual criticism:**
> - ¶2–¶5 [none]: Coherent correspondential teaching: Adam is treated as a functional “seal”/container in which cosmic order and conflict become readable. No narrative seams; the spiritual reading arises directly from 
> - ¶6 [none]: Clean seam/transition, but not a corrupt one: it marks a rhetorical pivot from Adam’s excellences (three things) to the rulers’ *intention* (seven reasons). The voice stays largely correspondential (p
> - ¶7–¶11, ¶13 [none]: Coherent correspondential purpose-list (teleological expansion) rather than an administrative overlay: the seven items explain functional exploits of Adam’s stamped image (illumination-substitute, cap
> - ¶12 [none]: Local logical/grammatical difficulty (likely damage or awkward transmission), not an insertion. The clause conflicts with the list’s premise (rulers exploit Adam for dominion) by adding that Adam’s im

**Changes:** No changes made. All findings (1–4) carried a 'none' recommendation, with no excisions or annotations requested.

**Spiritual narrative:** Three great and distinct excellences were opened in the first human state, and by them it stood above every combined power acting in the inner and outer degrees.

First, the Divine likeness was impressed upon that first state. Those who ordered its outward form fixed it according to the radiant pattern shown to their higher perception. Therefore that first state appeared in a beauty surpassing any unified power in the higher and lower domains.

Second, that first state was arranged in its whole constitution by the illumination of primary truth proceeding from the inmost heaven, together with a sufficient fullness of operative powers. Therefore its constitution differed from all other formed states, because each order of life has its own distinct arrangement. In that first state the inmost life was fitted to a right ordering of fundamental receptive qualities, and so it possessed an understanding surpassing that of other formed lives and their lower affections.

Third, within it were gathered the teachings, the directing perceptions, and the conjoining fixation of all powers acting from within and from without. Those who arranged that state collected these things and made them permanent within it. That first human state, together with its paired reception of life, became a containing form for the complete ordering of recurrent changes and all measured variations of state, from the greater to the lesser and back again. Thus the full order of the whole domain was stamped upon the first human state. Therefore the inner and outer degrees were stirred because of it, and contention and disturbance arose on its account between goods and evils: goods drew it toward living conjunction because the Divine likeness and form had been set within it, while evils drew it toward spiritual death so that they might rule through it, establish dominion, and by means of it abase the entire order.

Further, it is to be understood that ruling evils determined and arranged that first human state so that they might become masters through it and establish dominion, and they did this for a full series of purposes.

The first purpose was that it might become the source of illumination for their own formed states, in place of the higher illumination of Divine mediation previously shown to their perception; for they all longed for that likeness, and their will was drawn toward it.

The second was that they might draw down wisdom through that first state and hold it bound, preventing its return to higher degrees.

The third was that, by means of the likeness of Divine mediation impressed upon that first state, everything of the Divine in inner and outer degrees might be brought into contempt, because the splendour of that likeness appeared in a superior beauty, and that first state outshone all other formed lives.

The fourth was that it might be a conjoining fixation and a fresh determination, so that all later human states brought forth might come into existence in the same general form.

The fifth was that through it all the abilities of the powers acting within and without—those hidden and those openly operative—might be displayed, and that they might set the measure of their whole reach by it, so that each thing would stand revealed within that human form.

The sixth was that through the Divine likeness placed upon it there would come to be a great safeguarding for them, because heavenly order would restrain even ruling evils, and no evil would be inflicted upon them on account of that first state.

The seventh was that it might become chief and ruling over all formed lives, and that they might establish dominion through it.

---

## Chapter 65: Concerning the Sun.

**¶2** He says, it is the gate of life and the vessel of peace, to this great aeon of light [which comes daily to the world]. ⟨p.159⟩ However, since Satan knows that it is the gate of the souls' departure, he placed an exclusionary judgement in his law that no one worship it, saying: Whoever will worship it can die. He has otherwise called it: The light that will be nullified. He has hindered the souls from themselves turning their faces towards the light. He has caused them to deny the light of their being.

**¶3** Now, people are blind about everything! They have not understood the greatness of this great light-giver. They have denied the grace of this great light that shines upon them. They have not perceived its greatness and its divinity. Nor, also, have they understood the good things that it does for them, even today, as they are entrenched in their body; when it comes to this world and shines. Behold: How many are the charities that it does for mankind! Yet they, for their part, deny its graces, which they know not.

**¶4** Every day, would this great light-giver shine its light, it shall do seven good things for them. Shall they not see them?

**¶5** The first good thing that it does for them is its light, which it shines upon them. It opens the eyes of all mankind. They see underfoot as they walk by it. It takes away the blindness of the night, which made them blind with its darkness.

**¶6** The second: When (the sun) is come to the world, with its coming it shall bring calm and peace to the entire world. With its coming it shall also take away from them all the fear and trembling, which their heart is filled with the whole night; for it shall take away the darkness present by its light, and sweep it out. Also, this is how it extinguishes fear by its calm.

**¶7** The third is its vigil; because when (the sun) comes to the world all sleeping mankind shall arise and awake. And the watchmen too shall cease from watching. The guards who sit at the gates of camps, they break vigil at the watchposts, because of its light that has shone upon them in the world.

**¶8** ⟨p.160⟩ The fourth: It shall nourish, and give strength, taste and scent, to the trees and fruits and vegetables, and all the herbs, and the flowers and grasses, which are upon the entire earth.

**¶9** The fifth: When (the sun) shines in all the world, the evil reptile and the sharp-toothed beasts that are filled of wickedness, they shall flee to hiding in their dens.

**¶10** The sixth: When it shines its light, the wound of any person is eased from pain. Its light shall melt away the evil arts of every doer of wickedness.

**¶11** The seventh: (The sun) shall display and manifest to the world the sign of the splendour of the aeons of light, from which it has come out; it here in this world being their sign. It brings light to the whole of creation.

**¶12** Behold: How great is the foolishness that has befallen the sects of error! Not only that they have not perceived the mystery of the divinity of this great light-giver; but also, even all the benefits that it does for them. They forget them. They do not perceive them.

**¶13** Now, just as I have shown you the sun, which does seven good things in the world, hear also what I teach you about the night, for it has all wickednesses therein. Every occasion, would the night fall, it shall do seven evils in the world.

**¶14** The first wickedness that the night shall do in the world is the darkness, with which it shall fill the entire world; because when the sun sinks from the world and draws to it its rays, at that instant the shadow of the night shall spread out over the whole world. The eyes of the people fill with blackness. They neither see nor wonder with their eyes, because of the darkness of the night which shall fill the whole world.

**¶15** The second evil thing that the night shall do is its fear; because when its shadow looms forth, its fear and its trembling shall possess all of mankind. The entire world shall fill of the night.

**¶16** The third: When the night falls, all creatures take up revolt. They rebel in their heart, to do wickedness and perdition.

**¶17** The fourth: The heaviness of sleep shall lay upon them, and they fall asleep; they slumber and doze, and they are like these corpses in the night.

**¶18** The fifth is its ugliness; again, when the night would fall, the form of the people shall be hidden in the darkness and the ugliness of the night. The beauty and the form of men and women shall not be displayed before one another, because of its darkness and its ugliness, which it shall spread out over all their forms.

**¶19** The sixth occasion is when the night would fall on the world. At that instant evil people shall come out to do wickedness; the adulterers, and the robbers and poisoners. The evil beasts shall come out, and all the reptiles that are filled with wickedness, from their dens. And they stalk in the night. Also, the demons and fiends find power to do wickedness in the night. Again, the blows and buffets and wounds and discharges shall torment people in the night; because in it the whole of evil shall find strength.

**¶20** The seventh: The night shall display the sign of the darkness, its father. It has arisen out of its essence; because this night that came out of this first darkness has been displayed in the world. Note this night, the shadow of this first darkness, the one that is entwined and bound in all things above and below.

**¶21** Behold, I have taught you about these seven wickednesses of the black night; the ones that it shall do daily in the world, in the night. The sects have not seen this black night: what it is; nor from whence is its essence. That all these evils find strength in it!

**¶22** There are five archetypes occur in the sun, it making them [five archetypes] as it finds the world.

**¶23** ⟨p.162⟩ The first is its light; for it illumines by its light the world, and all the creatures who exist in it.

**¶24** The second is its beauty; because when it shines, it shall flood beauty and loveliness upon all creatures and everything.

**¶25** The third is its peace; for, again, when the sun would shine on the world, all people shall receive affection through it, and give peace to one another.

**¶26** The fourth is the life of the living soul, which it shall release from all the bonds and fetters of heaven and earth.

**¶27** The fifth: It gives a strength to the elements; and also it gives a scent and a taste to the entire Cross of the Light.

**¶28** So, you yourselves see that its light illumines more than all lights that occur in the world. Also, its beauty is more beautiful than all the beauties of mankind. Again, its peace is more surpassing than all the powers and guards who exist in the world. Its releasing action, by which it releases the living soul, is a full day ahead of all releasing actions! The strength that it gives to its limbs is a great strength, being mightier than all strengths!

**¶29** Once again, there are another three archetypes made apparent by the sun, in respect of the mystery of the first greatness.

**¶30** The first is the filling up of the disk of its ship; because its ship has filled up every season, and shall not wane at all, the way that the ship of the moon wanes. This continuous filling, by which it is filled, displays the mystery of the Father, the great greatness; from whom all the powers and the gods have come forth. He shall never wane, nor shall lack ever exist in him!

**¶31** The second: (The sun's) light surpasses the light of all the stars, and all the lights that occur in the universe; corresponding to the mystery of the light of the Father; for his light surpasses and is greater than the light of his aeons.

**¶32** ⟨p.163⟩ The third: This sun is very high above all else. It surpasses the height of all the mountains and hills that exist upon the entire earth, corresponding to the mystery of the Father of Greatness; for he is high above all the household of his light-earth. Also, because the Father himself is high above all the heights and the mountains of his light-earth. He is 'filled up' in his image [every] the time and every hour from eternity to eternity!

**¶33** [Behold]ld the two mysteries I have taught you, O my beloved ones! The mystery of the night and that of the day, which daily come forth in the world. Now, the mystery of the light and the mystery of the darkness are displayed daily in the created order. The sects have not seen them in their error!

**¶34** The mystery of the first great light is the sun, which is this second light-giver, the one that comes daily to the world. It displays all the marks of its graces, displaying and signifying on its own behalf that it is good, out of the essence of the good. It came from the good Father and appeared in this world.

**¶35** In contrast, this night that is made of fear exists after the mystery of the darkness. Behold too the mystery of its essence! It displays it in the world in all these wickednesses. It displays this mystery daily in the world, but the sects know it not in their error. They do not distinguish the mystery of the light from that of the darkness.

**¶37** You are the children of the day and the children of the light. However, you <[if you shall take up] the character of the darkness> are the children of the night and the children of the darkness.

**¶38** ⟨p.164⟩ Yet, blessed are you, my beloved ones! Blessed are your souls, for you have known the mystery of the day and that of the night. You have understood, that the day exists after the mystery of the light, but the night after the mystery of the darkness. They have not arisen from one another. Blessed is he who can know this mystery and distinguish these two essences, that of the light and that of the darkness, the ones that have not arisen from one another; so that he can inherit life eternal!

> **Textual criticism:**
> - ¶2-21 [none]: Clean, continuous correspondential teaching structured as two parallel sevenfold sequences (sun/day vs night/darkness). No narrative break; the spiritual reading tracks smoothly because the spiritual 
> - ¶2 [none]: Minor ideological polemic embedded within otherwise functional teaching: the ‘Satan placed an exclusionary judgement in his law that no one worship it’ is not administrative taxonomy, but it is a pole
> - ¶22-28 [annotate]: Potential seam/overlay in didactic mode: a new enumerated schema (‘five archetypes’) is introduced after the completed seven/seven contrast. This is not administrative (no jurisdictions, named rulers,
> - ¶29-32 [none]: Secondary cosmological allegoresis (“ship/disk,” comparative astronomy, height) that recasts the sun into a metaphysical proof-text for the Father of Greatness. This remains correspondential in method
> - ¶33-35, ¶37-38 [none]: Clean concluding recapitulation and ethical application of the day/night dualism. The voice is correspondential-exhortative, not administrative; it ties back to the observable alternation and draws id

**Changes:** No changes made. The only critic action requested was to annotate ¶22–28 as a secondary fivefold systematizing overlay (Finding 3), and annotations do not alter the restored chapter text. All other findings recommended none (Findings 1, 2, 4, 5), and there were no excision recommendations.

**Spiritual narrative:** A Divine admission into living conjunction and a containing calm stands open within the heavenly domain of wisdom as an ever-present inflowing illumination into outer life. Yet the adversarial love of self, knowing that this same opening leads minds out of bondage, set within its ruling falsity a forbidding decree that no one should acknowledge that source, insinuating that anyone who turns to it will lose life. It recast that inflowing wisdom as something doomed to be extinguished, blocked minds from directing their inner regard toward illumination, and drove them to reject the truth by which their humanity stands.

People are in ignorance about spiritual realities: they do not grasp the magnitude of that illuminating Divine presence, they reject the free gift continually extended to them, and they fail to perceive its holiness. They also do not understand the goods it accomplishes for them even while they still live in external states; for whenever wisdom flows down into the outer person it performs innumerable mercies for the human race, and yet they deny benefits whose origin they will not recognize.

Whenever this illumination is received into external life, it brings a complete course of operations that can be perceived.

First, it enlightens: it opens the understanding in all people so that they can discern in conduct what lies before them, and it removes the obscurity of falsity that had made them unable to see.

Second, it brings tranquility and peace: when wisdom enters the outer person it brings calming order throughout, and it removes the anxieties and tremors that filled the inner life during obscurity; it dispels falsity by truth, and through that quieting influx fear is extinguished.

Third, it awakens into vigilance: when illumination comes, those who were in spiritual sleep rise into attention, and the outermost faculties that had been strained in anxious guarding relax from that strained watchfulness, because truth present in ultimates restores orderly use.

Fourth, it fructifies the whole lower life: it gives nourishment and vigor, discernment and delight, to perceptions and to the works that proceed from them, and it vitalizes general and particular truths and tender beginnings of good throughout the whole extent of external life.

Fifth, it restrains predatory evils: when enlightenment spreads through the outer person, malicious cravings and violent falsities withdraw into their hidden interiors.

Sixth, it heals and breaks enchantment: when truth shines, the pain of spiritual injury is eased, and the deceptive practices by which the wicked act upon others are dissolved.

Seventh, it bears witness and makes manifest: it makes evident in the natural degree a sign that the heavenly domain of wisdom is real and present; it is an outward testimony of inward Divine splendor, and through it illumination is extended through the whole created mind.

Great folly has seized those who are in falsified worship: they neither perceive the holy secret of this illuminating presence nor even attend to its obvious benefits. They let these benefits pass without recognition.

There is also the opposite state, the state of obscurity, within which evils find opportunity; whenever it descends upon external life it brings a complete course of infestations.

First, falsity spreads: when illumination is withdrawn from the outer mind, obscurity extends through the natural degree; understanding is filled with denial and confusion, and people neither discern nor rightly perceive because falsity dominates their outer life.

Second, fear strengthens: dread and trembling seize human minds, and external life is filled with the oppressive presence of that state.

Third, rebellion arises within: when truth is absent, affections and thoughts rise into disorder, and the heart turns toward doing evils and rushing into spiritual ruin.

Fourth, stupor settles: negligence and listlessness take hold, and people become deathlike in respect to what is living.

Fifth, what is human is concealed and distorted: when falsity covers outer life, the distinct form of each person’s quality is hidden; mutual recognition of what is good and true is obscured, and what should appear as spiritual beauty is not manifested.

Sixth, evildoing is empowered: when obscurity rules, profanations of good, thefts of truth for self, and secret poisons against understanding come into action; bestial affections and crawling falsities rise from hidden loves and prowl in external life; infernal influences gain freer operation; conflicts, assaults, injuries, and outpourings of harm torment people because every evil is strengthened.

Seventh, infernal origin is displayed: obscurity makes manifest in outer life the active falsity from which it proceeds, because it is an effect of primal denial made present in externals; this effect intertwines and binds disordered things in both higher and lower regions of the mind.

These recurring infestations of obscurity can be understood, yet those in error do not know what this state is nor the source of its strength—that evils are empowered within it.

Within illumination as it acts in the outer person there is a sufficient set of universal qualities.

First is enlightenment itself, by which the natural mind and all affections and thoughts in it are enabled to see.

Second is spiritual beauty, by which ordered truth gives loveliness and attractiveness to the life.

Third is peace, by which gentler affection is received and mutual concord is established.

Fourth is liberation of the living inner life, by which the mind is released from compulsions and entanglements in higher and lower bondage so that it can act in freedom according to order.

Fifth is strengthening in ultimates together with delight in the whole complex of doctrine that bears witness to Divine love and wisdom.

These qualities are pre-eminent: the enlightenment surpasses all lesser understandings in the natural degree; the beauty it imparts surpasses merely natural attractiveness; its peace exceeds all external securities; its liberation is beyond other kinds of release; and the strength it supplies to operative powers in ultimates exceeds every natural power.

Beyond these, further universal marks become apparent through this illumination, disclosing something of the First Divine.

First is unceasing plenitude: the receptive form through which this influx acts remains continually full and does not diminish by alternations as derivative illumination does; thus the Divine Source is inexhaustible, from whom all capacities and objects of worship in the mind proceed, and in whom there is no lack.

Second is incomparable supremacy: the wisdom of this influx exceeds all lesser truths and derivative lights in the whole mental universe, bearing witness that the Divine Light surpasses even the light of heavenly degrees.

Third is transcendence of degree: this illumination stands above all elevated natural states and every exalted condition attainable in external life, bearing witness that the Divine is above the whole ordered heaven of wisdom and above every height of spiritual life, and that Divine fullness remains in its own likeness from eternity to eternity.

Two mysteries are thus set before the created mind again and again: enlightenment and obscurity, and with them the difference between truth from good and falsity from evil. Yet those in error do not recognize them.

The primary mystery of Divine illumination is made present in the natural degree as a secondary inflowing light that comes continually into outer life. In all its operations it shows that it is good, proceeding from good itself; it comes from the good Divine Source and becomes apparent in external existence.

In contrast, the state of obscurity, full of fear, exists from active falsity; it displays its origin by its evils in external life. This too is manifested continually, yet those in error do not know it and do not distinguish enlightenment from obscurity.

Those who receive and live from illumination are born from it and belong to it; those who take on the quality of obscurity are born from that state and belong to it.

Blessed are those who know within themselves the mystery of these two states: that enlightenment comes from the Divine Light and obscurity from active falsity; that they do not arise from one another; and that by distinguishing their two origins—good with its truth, and evil with its falsity—the mind is led into eternal life.

---

## Chapter 66: Concerning the Ambassador.

**¶2** Two great mysteries are apparent in this shining sun, at its rising and setting. Now, at the moment when it rises and is visible in the universe, all creatures of flesh shall lift up their heads fr[om their sleep] towards the splendour of the sun, and open their doors. While at evening time, when the sun sinks from the universe, all people from every place and even all beasts shall go in to their dark hiding places. The population of the entire universe shuts their doors and dark night overcomes them. They go inside and stay within the dark night in their hiding places.

**¶3** [*] Very great is this mystery revealed by the sun! For when the sun rises and [be]come[s] visible at the beginning of the day all people open their doors, and all of humanity and every creature emerges onto the ear[th].

**¶4** This pertains to the mystery of the First Man, because when he came out from the grea[t]ness he shone forth over all the children of darkness. At that mome[nt] all the children of darkness came forth from their storehouses, its powers and its armies, abandoning their caves and their black abyss. They destroyed the [outer] body of his five sons, who were swathed over hi[s b]o[dy, in t]hat first struggle. ⟨p.165⟩

**¶5** [*] Again, when the sun sinks from the universe and sets, and all people go in to their hiding places and houses and conceal themselves; this also pertains to the mystery of the end, as it presages the consummation of the universe. For, when all the light will be purified and redeemed i[n] the universe at the last, it is the last hour of the day, the time when the Last Statue will go up to the aeo[n of] light. The enemy too, death, will go in to bondage; to the prison of the souls of the de[ni]iers and blasphemers who loved the darkness. They will go in with it to bondage and the dark night overcomes th[em]; and thenceforth this name 'light' will not shine upon them.

**¶6** Behold the two mysteries made visible daily in the sun, which is this splendrous light-giver.

> **Textual criticism:**
> - ¶2 [none]: Clean correspondential substrate: the teaching arises directly from the function of the sun’s rising/setting and the creaturely response (opening/closing, emergence/withdrawal). No narrative seam or j
> - ¶3 [excise]: Mostly clean correspondential continuation, but with one phrase that forces the spiritual reading into a strained, imported equivalence: “destroy this Light Cross… established in silence.” This reads 
> - ¶4 [none]: Clean correspondential application to the First Man myth as an archetypal instance of the same functional pattern (light appearing → darkness’ stored forces surge out). The named elements here are act
> - ¶5 [excise]: Substrate remains largely correspondential (sunset → hiding → consummation/separation), but there is a localized technical insertion: “the collector of all things, the Last Statue, will gather in and 
> - ¶6 [none]: Clean closure in the correspondential voice: summarizes the two mysteries as daily visible in the sun. No administrative inventory, no seam-expansion, and it matches the chapter’s opening claim.

**Changes:** Executed the critic’s excision recommendations only: (1) In ¶3, removed the clause “to destroy this Light Cross that is established i[n] silence.” per Finding 2 (localized technical slogan intrusion), leaving a coherent sunrise-correspondence sentence. (2) In ¶5, removed “the collector of all things, the Last Statue, will gather in and sculpt itself.” per Finding 4 (specialized doctrinal label insertion); no other changes made.

**Spiritual narrative:** Two universal arcana are disclosed in the daily alternation of manifest wisdom and its withdrawal in the lowest degree. When wisdom first flows down into outward awareness, every affection in the natural level that can be stirred is lifted from its torpor toward that illumination, and the receptacles of thought and life are opened. But when that illumination withdraws from outward awareness, every state—down to the most sensuous impulse—recedes into obscurity; the whole natural level closes, and a prevailing falsity and lack of perception takes possession, so that each faculty turns inward and remains shut up in that darkness.

Great indeed is the arcanum shown by this alternation: when wisdom dawns at the beginning of a new state, the outward forms are opened, and the whole natural person with every lesser power comes forth into action, driven to oppose and extinguish the quiet establishment of saving truth in the outermost degree.

This belongs to the arcanum of the primal heavenly human: when the first Divine good went forth from higher fullness into lower things and directly engaged what is infernal, it poured illumination over all states immersed in falsity. Then all the evils and falsities that had been stored up in interiors surged out with their derivative abilities and organized persuasions, abandoning the hidden restraints and profane deeps in which they were held. They assaulted and tore away the outermost protective truths of a sufficient complement of operative powers that had been bound about that Divine good in the first combat.

Again, when illumination withdraws from outward manifestation and sinks from presence, and all states retreat into interior concealments and cover themselves, this also pertains to the arcanum of the end, because it prefigures the completion of the lower order. For when every genuine good and truth in that outward domain has been cleansed and delivered at last, the unifier of all things in ultimates—the final formed receptacle of the whole—will draw all remaining goods and truths into itself and bring itself into determinate completion. This is the final state of that order, when the completed receptacle is elevated into the realm of pure light. Then the opposing principle, spiritual death, is shut up in servitude within the confinement of those who denied and profaned, who loved falsity; they enter with it into that bondage, and enduring falsity prevails over them, so that thereafter no perception of truth is granted them.

These are the two arcana made visible unceasingly through the alternation of manifest and withdrawn illumination from the one source of all wisdom.

## Chapter 68: Concerning Fire.

**¶1** This fire, which burns and is apparent today in the world, it does five works.

**¶2** The first work that it does is this: It incinerates and burns wood, and everything that shall be placed upon it.

**¶3** The second: It separates four aspects of the wood, which are these: fire and light, ash and smoke.

**¶4** The third: It gives a taste and an aroma to whatever will be cooked on it. It also gives heat and cheer to whoever will be close to it.

**¶5** The fourth work that it does: All craftsmen shall work the utensils that they will fabricate in it.

**¶6** The fifth: It shall display light, and the people see by it.

> **Textual criticism:**
> - ¶1 [none]: Clean substrate setup: a correspondential teaching framed by function (what fire does), organized as a fivefold sequence. No narrative break, no jurisdictional/authority layer.
> - ¶2 [none]: Clean functional correspondence: burning/consuming as a first operation. No intrusion, no seam marker, no change of voice.
> - ¶3 [none]: Genuine difficulty from compression/ellipsis, not an insertion: the fourfold list is under-explained, creating interpretive strain, but it remains within the same functional, physical process (combust
> - ¶4 [none]: Clean continuation: fire’s imparted qualities (taste/aroma; heat/cheer) provide a natural-functional basis for the spiritual sense. No signs of overlay.
> - ¶5 [none]: Clean correspondence with a slight lexical pressure (‘craftsmen’), but still substrate: artisans using fire to form utensils is a functional image for formation of instruments/structures. No administr
> - ¶6 [none]: Clean culmination: fire as light enabling sight. Strong correspondential closure, consistent with the chapter’s progression.

**Changes:** No changes were made. All findings (1–6) recommended none; there were no excisions or annotations to apply.

**Spiritual narrative:** The love that is active and openly felt in the outer degree performs a sufficient set of operations. First, it consumes and removes what is merely natural and without life, together with whatever is adjoined to that state, so that such things no longer govern. Second, it discriminates and brings into order the mixed contents within that state, setting apart what can be raised into love united with wisdom from what must be cast off into what is rejected, and from what remains only as persuasive appearance without substance. Third, it gives an inward quality and pleasantness to the uses being brought into effect through it, and it also communicates enlivening warmth and gladness to those who are in nearness and conjunction with it. Fourth, it enables those who are in the skills of service to form the containing structures and instruments by which life can act, so that the powers of the mind are fashioned into means for use through that love. Fifth, it makes wisdom outwardly manifest, so that people are able to perceive and discern in the natural degree by means of it.

## Chapter 69: Concerning the Twelve Signs of the Zodiac and the Five Stars

**¶2** Whether these twelve signs of the zodiac that are fixed on the sphere, or these five stars that circle upon them; those and these are rulers, all of them, according to their essence. They are all enemies and rivals to each other ⟨one oppresses another, they threaten each other⟩. They plunder one another by the action of the great craftsman who has formed all the worlds. He has gathered [them] and bound them on the sphere above, which circles constantly. Now, while it circles and tumbles them, so they shall do everything that they desire, above and below, according to their pleasure. Nevertheless, a guardian is over them, a debt-collector above them; compelling, extorting, and taking what is theirs away from them!

**¶3** [*] Now, this is how it can rightly be understood. These are the twelve rulers of depravity, the ones that wicked[ness] shall not [cease] For they cause all the evil and [disorder in the world]rld, whether in tree or in flesh.

**¶4** ⟨p.168⟩ And understand also about these five stars, the leaders: from where were they formed? The star of Zeus (Jupiter) was generated from the world of smoke, which is the mind. In contrast, Aphrodite (Venus) came about from the world of fire. And then Ares (Mars) belongs to the world of wind. Hermes (Mercury) belongs to the world of water. Cronos (Saturn) belongs to the world [of] Darkness.

**¶5** And also, the two ascendants are counted to fire and desire, the dry and the moist. They are the father and the mother of all these.

**¶6** [*] And also, the sun and the moon, which are counted in with them as a rule so that they would reign over them. You will discover they subjugate them at every moment.

**¶7** These seven we have named, the five stars and the two ascendants: They are the doers of wickedness that do every wicked and bad act in every land, the whole of it, above and below, in every creature, in the dry and the moist, in tree and flesh.

**¶8** Once more I reveal to you about these signs of the zodiac: [They] are distributed, appointed on four sides, three per angle in these four places. And they are fixed to this revolving sphere. Aries, Leo and Sagittarius, they three belong to a single side. In contrast, Taurus, Capricorn and Virgo, these other three belong to another side. And then, Gemini, Libra and Aquarius belong to another side. Scorpio, Pisces and Cancer belong to another corner. Now, they are placed like this, appointed to these four parts, and distributed on the sphere.

**¶9** So, when the side of Aries, Leo and Sagittarius will be plundered by the guardian who is over it, who extorts from it and the leaders who move upon it; at that instant shall affliction strike all the four-footed creatures below. However, when [the side] of Taurus, Virgo and Capricorn will be plundered; [affliction] shall ⟨p.169⟩ at once befall the herbs, together with the vegetables and all the fruits of the trees. Yet again, when the side of Scorpio, Pisces and Cancer will be plundered; scarcity shall befall the waters upon the earth, and drought be from place to place. Conversely, should the side of Gemini, Libra and Aquarius be plundered; deformity and stuntedness shall befall the form of mankind from place to place.

**¶10** Behold now, I have explained to you about these twelve signs of the zodiac: They were fashioned from the five worlds of Darkness, and bound on the sphere.

**¶11** Also, I have taught you about the other five, the stars: They too came about from the five worlds of the land of Darkness.

**¶12** I have recounted to you also about the [t]wo ascendants: They are set to the mystery of fire and desire. They are the dry and the moist, the father and the mother.

**¶13** Again, I have revealed to you about the sun and the moon: They are strangers to them. Still, because of the constraint of the arrangement, in that the thing receives light, and as they belong and plunder them; because of this they were counted in with them in relation to the calculation of the number. For, the sun and the moon are from out of the greatness, not belonging to the stars and the signs of the zodiac.

> - ¶4 [annotate]: Administrative overlay block: the five planets are introduced as “leaders” and then allocated to the five dark worlds by origin (“generated from… belongs to…”). The spiritual reading reports the names
> - ¶5 [none]: Genuine difficulty inside a mostly functional move. The “two ascendants… dry and moist… father and mother” can be read correspondentially (polarity generating effects), but the terminology is cryptic 
> - ¶6 [excise]: Damaged/marginal gloss intrusion inside the running text. The bracketed question-like aside (“but why did they count them in…?”) is a different discourse mode (reader/scribe questioning) and causes th
> - ¶7 [none]: Coherent correspondential summary. It re-integrates the preceding items into functional causality (“doers of wickedness… do every wicked… in every creature”), which is substrate-style even if it relie
> - ¶8–¶9 [annotate]: Administrative distribution plus correspondential effect-claims: a mixed seam where a territorial/quadrant appointment list (administrative) is immediately used to explain effects in nature (correspon
> - ¶10–¶13 [none]: Concluding recapitulation is coherent, but it contains a procedural/numerical justification (counting sun/moon) characteristic of later systematization. Still, it reads as a stitched conclusion to the

**Changes:** Implemented excisions per critic recommendations: (1) Finding 2—removed the five-world zodiac allocation catalogue from ¶3, retaining only the coherent framing and the functional conclusion about the twelve rulers and their effects, with minimal smoothing for prose continuity. (2) Finding 5—excised the intrusive interrogative gloss from ¶6, leaving the underlying statement about the sun and moon being counted in so they would reign and subjugate them. No other paragraphs were modified; items marked for annotation (Findings 3 and 7) were left unchanged per instructions.

**Spiritual narrative:** Whether it is the whole arrangement of general governing influences fixed in the outermost plane, or the sufficient set of dominant impulses that move through that arrangement, all of them are ruling powers, each acting from its own quality. They are mutually hostile and rivalrous, with one pressing down another and each threatening the rest. They take from one another through the operation of the ordering power that has formed all states into a coherent system. That ordering power has gathered these influences, bound them in the outermost degree within a continually recurring cycle; and as that cycle turns and drives their alternations, they carry out whatever their ruling loves crave, in inner and outer life, according to their delight. Yet above them there is a restraining oversight and superior exaction that compels them, draws from them what is due, and takes away what they would seize as their own.

This is the right way to understand the structure. These ruling tendencies were seized out of a full series of fundamental evils and falsities and fixed into the outermost cycle, and from each fundamental origin a paired determination was taken so that every kind of perversion might have a place in the general governance. From obscured understanding that feigns intelligence came the twin power of divided reasoning and confident persuasion. From the burning of self-love came the power of initiating from self and the power of domineering over others. From volatile persuasion that disturbs discernment came the power of possessive craving, the power of self-justifying balance, and the power of casting truths into mere opinion. From falsified truth that should have been living came the power of enclosing affection within appearances, the power of separating purity from charity, and the power of immersing the mind in fluctuating pretense. From active denial of good came the power of hard restraint and the power of secret malice. These are the complete set of ruling depraved tendencies, by which wickedness is kept in motion and not allowed to cease, because they bring forth every disorder in the human mind and in its effects, whether the disorder appears in perception or in act.

Understand also the chief leaders among these influences, the more immediate dominant impulses: the impulse of self-derived counsel and proud reasoning arose from obscured understanding; the impulse of alluring delight arose from the burning of self-love; the impulse of contention and assault arose from volatile persuasion; the impulse of subtle calculation and shifting interpretation arose from falsified truth; and the impulse of cold resistance and despairing constraint arose from active denial of good.

Also, two chief determinations are counted with burning love turned into craving: one mode that hardens into resistance, and another that yields into appetite. These two act as a generative pair for the whole, as a kind of father and mother for these perversions.

Also, higher governing principles were numbered among them in the rule of the arrangement so that the whole might be held under superior governance. It can be perceived that these higher governing principles continually subjugate the depraved influences at every moment.

These seven—those five chief impulses together with the two generative determinations—are the operative powers of evil that bring about every wicked and corrupt act throughout the whole extent of life, in inner and outer things, within every affection and thought, in what is resistant and what is receptive, in perception and in deed.

Again, the general governing influences are arranged into a complete distribution in the outermost plane: they are appointed to four quarters, three to each quarter, and fixed within the recurring cycle of external life. The group that springs from self-initiating pride, domineering ambition, and confident persuasion stands in one quarter; the group that springs from possessive craving, hard restraint, and the separation of purity from charity stands in another; the group that springs from divided reasoning, self-justifying balance, and casting truths into mere opinion stands in another; and the group that springs from secret malice, fluctuating pretense, and enclosing affection within appearances stands in the remaining quarter. Thus the whole is set in fourfold completeness, distributed through the outermost cycle.

Therefore, when the quarter governed by self-initiating pride, domineering ambition, and confident persuasion is forcibly exacted and stripped by the restraining oversight above it, together with the chief impulses active there, then immediately disorder strikes the lower affections of the natural mind. When the quarter governed by possessive craving, the separation of purity from charity, and hard restraint is forcibly exacted, then disorder at once befalls elementary perceptions and their derivative knowledges, together with the uses and works that proceed from them. When the quarter governed by secret malice, fluctuating pretense, and enclosing affection within appearances is forcibly exacted, then there is scarcity of living truth in outer life and a drying up of understanding from one state to another. Conversely, when the quarter governed by divided reasoning, self-justifying balance, and casting truths into mere opinion is forcibly exacted, then distortion and diminishment befall the form of human will and thought as it comes forth into life, varying from one state to another.

Now it has been made plain concerning the full set of these general governing depraved influences: they were formed out of the series of fundamental evils and falsities and fixed into the outermost cycle.

It has also been made plain concerning the other five chief impulses: they too arose from those same fundamental origins of denial and perversion.

It has also been made plain concerning the two generative determinations: they belong to the hidden activity of craving in its resistant mode and in its yielding mode, acting as the generative pair.

And again it has been made plain concerning the higher governing principles: they are not of the same kind as these depraved influences; yet because the outermost plane must be held in order by what governs, and because whatever is present in the outermost plane receives determination by higher influx and is compelled to serve within bounds, they were numbered together in the external reckoning. For those higher governing principles are from a superior source of life and order, not arising from the depraved impulses or from the general perversions of the outermost cycle.

---

## Chapter 70: Concerning the Body: It was constructed after the Pattern of the Universe.

**¶2** This whole universe, above and below, reflects the pattern of the human body; as the formation of this body of flesh accords to the pattern of the universe.

**¶3** Its head is like the first-fruits of the five garments. And, from its neck down to the site of its heart, it resembles the pattern of the ten firmaments. And again, the heart accords to the wheel of the rotating sphere. And, from its heart down to its intestines (?), is like this atmosphere that extends from the sphere down to the earth. The [male] part of the body corresponds to this great earth. And also, from its intestines (?) down to its loins is like the three earths. And also, its shinbones to the pattern of the space in which the Porter stands. Its footsoles to the great earth upon which the Porter is stood; and the four fastenings that are under his feet. Its liver to the vessel of fire. Its flesh to the vessel of darkness. Its blood to the vessel of water.

**¶4** Now, this is how the small body corresponds to the macro-cosmos in its firmaments, in its orderings, in its mountains, its walls and its vessels, as I have made clear to you!
