However, the number of the twelve images [and] the three great pillars of mightiness that [is broken] pillars supporting the disk [are set there] the other three [of the earth] they are the garments that the Living Spirit [great pillars] he put them at that time [of truth] that threw them, together with [clothed himself in] which is displayed therein [when] this [he descended] perfect before [the forms] great Porter [what is within them] in his power [arrangement] these are the three great [the] against [the] are in the midst of people [are set] knows [powers] entrusts his soul to them [disorder].

> **Textual criticism:**
> - ¶3 [none]: Clean correspondential substrate. The teaching proceeds by function (tree/fruit/basket) to correct a premature claim of spiritual identity; no administrative jurisdiction-listing intrudes and the narr
> - ¶5 [annotate]: Major disruption from heavy damage plus a mixed-voice catalogue. The paragraph reads like a spliced inventory of hypostases/angels/pillars/sea/four angels/five gods/summons-and-obedience. The spiritua
> - ¶6 [none]: Genuine difficulty from severe textual damage, not a clear insertion. The spiritual reading ‘reconstructs from probable function rather than explicit wording’ because the paragraph has broken clauses 

**Changes:** No changes made. Finding 1 (¶3) recommended none. Finding 2 (¶5) recommended annotate only (no text modification). Finding 3 (¶6) recommended none.

**Spiritual narrative:** The whole ordered system of life in which you now stand is a formed structure of perception and operation, and you are like a work still held in living connection with that structure. If you had already been separated from that general order by a prepared gathering of uses, then you could rightly claim a new origin from that gathered use itself. But if you are still bound into the general order and draw your state from it, how can you ascribe your origin to what only receives at the end, before you have been separated into it as a completed use? For so long as a work remains in immediate dependence on its producing structure, it is not yet to be named charity in act. Likewise you, while you remain entangled in the general order and not yet gathered into a distinct use, are not properly to be called one whose origin is from that receiving of charity.

There are interior and supreme governing principles, and a ruling understanding that gives form and direction, together with an initiating vigor of spiritual life that stands in the first power of truth, attended by complete arrangements of ministering intelligences. Beneath these are further subordinations of ministering intelligences, yet their specific ordering here breaks into obscurity. There are stable supports of order set in the center, and an encompassing expanse of general knowledges in outermosts surrounding the whole system. There are universal guardians distributed through the entire extent, sustaining power in the lowest degree, which is the natural plane on which all higher degrees rest. There are also derivative ruling tendencies proceeding from the primal spiritual human, by which the whole is held in firmness; and there is the directed awakening of influx with the answering compliance of lower things to higher government.

In a third domain, in a lower degree, there is a full organized set of operative forces fixed in their station. Yet the account of their forms and their numbering is broken and cannot be wholly recovered. Still, there are three chief supports of power by which stability is given, and three further chief means by which that stability is carried into what is outermost and actionable. These outermost means are external truths by which living influx clothes itself for use when it descends; what is said of their being cast forth together with what appears within them is textually unclear, but the sense tends toward an arrangement in which interior things are manifested through ultimates. There is then an orderly completeness established before a great regulating function that admits and governs approach to a higher state. Within its power are three principal protections set to stand against disorder; they are active among people, and whoever recognizes their governance and entrusts the inmost of life to their keeping is preserved by the order they maintain, though the closing linkage is imperfect where the wording fails.

---

## Chapter 122: Concerning the 'Assent' and the 'Amen'

**¶2** The assent and the amen have come about from these great ones of the powers. They were given the name as the assent belongs to the summons and the amen belongs to the answer.

**¶3** Just as the summons and the obedience are the ones that opened the portals of mercy and entreaty for the First Man, so they also became portals for the Mother of Life and the Father of the living. The First Man sought and found his redeemer. And also the Mother of Life and the Father of the living sought [and found] this runner, who came forth from them, who is the First Man; but the summons and the obedience became the portals for them.

**¶4** ⟨p.293⟩
Now, in this way they have become portals for the aeons of greatness towards the First Man, and the soul that was conjoined with death. They became helpers [and supports] of the First Man during his ascent. They gave him [remembrance] the Father of Life and the Mother of Life [according to his measure]. They are purifiers of the living soul, being helpers and bestowers of remembrance for it, be it either in [in the ship, or] in the tree or in the creation of flesh [or in the formed body].

> **Textual criticism:**
> - ¶2 [none]: Clean correspondential setup: two interior functions are defined by what they do (assent aligns with summons; amen aligns with answer). No narrative break, no administrative inventory, and the spiritu
> - ¶3 [none]: Mostly clean correspondential development, with one compressed relational identification that creates minor interpretive strain but not a textual seam. The paragraph stays in functional causality (por
> - ¶4 [none]: Genuine difficulty from a damaged/unstable list at the end (fragmentary loci/conditions), not an administrative insertion. The correspondential line is stable up to the final clause; then the text shi

**Changes:** No changes made. The critic’s recommendations for ¶2, ¶3, and ¶4 were all “none,” with no excisions indicated.

**Spiritual narrative:** From the higher ruling faculties there arose a full consent that receives an initiating influx, and a full consent that seals a responsive return. Each took its name from its use: one from agreeing with what is sent in, the other from confirming what is answered back.

As the initiating influx and the willing compliance first opened interior access to compassion and to humble reception of aid for the good that directly confronts evil, so the same interior access was opened for wisdom and for the originating good from which living capacity proceeds. That embattled good turned itself toward deliverance and was conjoined with its rescuing Divine operation. Likewise wisdom and the originating good turned themselves toward the swift outgoing operation that proceeds from them and is that same good in active form; and for them too the initiating influx and the willing compliance were the means by which such access was made.

In this way, the same consenting powers became interior access for the higher heavens toward that embattled good, and toward the inner life that had been bound to spiritual death. They became supports for it during its elevation from lower to higher degrees. They restored to it, according to its capacity, awareness of the originating good and of wisdom. They purify the living inner life by supporting it and by returning to it remembrance of its source and end, whether that life is held in a state of perception, or in an outermost state where life is being organized into acts and embodied expression; at this last point the sequence becomes difficult to render with full certainty, though the general sense remains that purification and restored awareness operate in every degree of life.

---
